# Bee bear

**Bee Bear**  
---  
[![Bee Bear](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/bee-swarm-simulator/images/e/ec/Bee_Bear.png/revision/latest?cb=20230414231323)  
Overview   
Species  | Traveling Bear   
Location  | In front of the Dandelion Field   
Bee Prerequisites  | None   
Color Scheme   
|  Fur #ffffff |  Fur Shade #a09c8c  
---|---  
Snout #401c1c #180c0c |  Torso #181c24 #98841c  
Arms #ffffff |  Legs #ffffff  
  
_Not to be confused with[Bear Bee](/wiki/Bear_Bee "Bear Bee"), an Event bee with a similar name._

**Bee Bear** is a [traveling bear](/wiki/Category:Traveling_Bears "Category:Traveling Bears") who appears during [Beesmas](/wiki/Category:Beesmas "Category:Beesmas"). He is one of the four temporary [quest givers](/wiki/Quest_Givers "Quest Givers"), the others being [Sun Bear](/wiki/Sun_Bear "Sun Bear"), [Gummy Bear](/wiki/Gummy_Bear "Gummy Bear") and [Bubble Bee Man](/wiki/Bubble_Bee_Man "Bubble Bee Man"). 

In 2018, his [quests](/wiki/Quests "Quests") were focused on creating [Presents](/wiki/Present "Present"), which could be later given to [NPCs](/wiki/Category:NPC "Category:NPC"). At the end of his quests, he gave players a special bee, [Festive Bee](/wiki/Festive_Bee "Festive Bee"). Alternatively, if the player already had one by buying the [Festive Bee Pack](/wiki/Robux_Shop "Robux Shop") or from the [Ticket Tent](/wiki/Ticket_Tent "Ticket Tent"), he gave ![Ticket](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)500 [Tickets](/wiki/Ticket "Ticket") instead. He was located in front of the [Ant Gate](/wiki/Ant_Gate "Ant Gate") between December 19, 2018 to January 31, 2019. 

His second visit was from December 22, 2019 to February 10, 2020. Bee Bear rewarded the player a ![Cub Buddy](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Cub Buddy](/wiki/Cub_Buddy#Skins "Cub Buddy") after the 10 quests that a player completed, and the ![Bee Cub](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Bee Cub](/wiki/Cub_Buddy#Skins "Cub Buddy") after another 5 quests. His quests consisted of a variety of different requirements. Some included collecting [Pollen](/wiki/Pollen "Pollen"), [Tokens](/wiki/Ability_Tokens "Ability Tokens"), [Goo](/wiki/Goo "Goo"), defeating [mobs](/wiki/Mobs "Mobs"), putting [Ornaments](/wiki/Ornaments "Ornaments") on the [Beesmas Tree](/wiki/Beesmas_Tree "Beesmas Tree"), and more. He stood in front of the [Dandelion Field](/wiki/Dandelion_Field "Dandelion Field"), next to the [Festive Present](/wiki/Ornament_Presents "Ornament Presents"), the first time that a traveling Bear was not located in front of the Ant Gate. 

His third visit was from December 25, 2020 to March 1, 2021. His quests were used to help introduce [Beequips](/wiki/Beequip "Beequip"). His quests also required completing and using Beesmas Decorations from multiple NPCs, collecting ![Snowflake](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Snowflakes](/wiki/Snowflake "Snowflake"), purchasing items from his [Catalog](/wiki/Bee_Bear%27s_Catalog "Bee Bear's Catalog"), and delivering ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Presents](/wiki/Present "Present"). He rewarded players with a Festive Bee ( or ![Ticket](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)500 [Tickets](/wiki/Ticket "Ticket") if [Festive Bee](/wiki/Festive_Bee "Festive Bee") was already owned by the player) when his first 15 quests were completed, and rewarded a ![Festive Wreath](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Festive Wreath](/wiki/Festive_Wreath "Festive Wreath") [Beequip](/wiki/Beequip "Beequip") for the Festive Bee when all his quests were completed. 

His fourth visit was from December 26, 2021 to April 1, 2022. His quests were used to help introduce the new [planters](/wiki/Planter "Planter"), rewarded a special type of his own (![Festive Planter](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Festive Planters](/wiki/Festive_Planter "Festive Planter")), alongside giving players the opportunity to obtain a ![Cub Buddy](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Cub Buddy](/wiki/Cub_Buddy#Skins "Cub Buddy") (or a ![Star Treat](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Star Treat](/wiki/Star_Treat "Star Treat") if already owned) by completing his first 15 quests once again. In the second part of Beesmas, he rewards players a ![Snow Cub](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Snow Cub](/wiki/Cub_Buddy#Skins "Cub Buddy") after all of his quests (new ones included) were completed. 

His fifth visit was from December 26, 2022, to May 7, 2023. His quests were used to help introduce the [Robo Bear Challenge](/wiki/Robo_Bear_Challenge "Robo Bear Challenge") and [drives](/wiki/Drives "Drives"), another new addition in the update. He rewarded players with a ![Cub Buddy](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Cub Buddy](/wiki/Cub_Buddy#Skins "Cub Buddy") (or a ![Star Treat](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Star Treat](/wiki/Star_Treat "Star Treat") if Cub Buddy was already owned by the player) when his first 15 quests were completed. In the second part of Beesmas, he rewarded players a ![Peppermint Robo Cub](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Peppermint Robo Cub](/wiki/Cub_Buddy#Skins "Cub Buddy") after all of his quests (new ones included) were completed. 

His sixth visit was from July 17, 2024, to October 29, 2024 (confirmed with an in-game timer). His quests were used to help introduce [stickers](/wiki/Sticker "Sticker"). After 5 of his quests were completed, he rewarded players a ![Sticker Planter](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Sticker Planter](/wiki/Sticker_Planter "Sticker Planter") and a ![Star Jelly](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Star Jelly](/wiki/Royal_Jelly#Star_Jelly "Royal Jelly"). After 10 of his quests were completed, he rewarded players a ![Hive model wavy festive](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Wavy Festive Hive Skin](/wiki/Sticker#Sticker_Index "Sticker"). Upon completing a further 5 quests, he rewarded players a ![Cub Buddy](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Cub Buddy](/wiki/Cub_Buddy#Skins "Cub Buddy") (![Cub Buddy Voucher](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Cub Buddy Voucher](/wiki/Sticker#Sticker_Index "Sticker") if the player already owns a Cub Buddy). When completing the final 5 quests, he rewarded players with a ![Doodle Cub](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Doodle Cub](/wiki/Cub_Buddy#Skins "Cub Buddy"). 

His seventh visit was originally for a Roblox event, the Winter Spotlight; however, he remained in the game for a few more months even though the event had ended. His visit lasted from December 17, 2024, at 9-10 pm 2024 to May 1, 2025. The focus was on almost everything in the game, not just one specific concept. The rewards included a ![Festive Planter](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Festive Planter](/wiki/Festive_Planter "Festive Planter"), a ![Cub Buddy](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Cub Buddy](/wiki/Cub_Buddy#Skins "Cub Buddy"), and a ![Gloomy Cub](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Gloomy Cub](/wiki/Cub_Buddy#Skins "Cub Buddy"). 

His eighth and current visit started on December 25, 2025; however, none of his quests were introduced until December 27, 2025, where he gave out ten quests for rewards including a ![Festive Planter](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Festive Planter](/wiki/Festive_Planter "Festive Planter"). On January 16, 2026, 5 more quests were introduced for a ![Cub Buddy](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Cub Buddy](/wiki/Cub_Buddy#Skins "Cub Buddy") (![Cub Buddy Voucher](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Cub Buddy Voucher](/wiki/Sticker#Sticker_Index "Sticker") if the player already owns a Cub Buddy). On April 23, 2026, the last 5 quests were released, rewarding the player with the Petal Cub skin on completion. The main focus on his quests are [Blooms](/wiki/Bloom "Bloom"). His visit will end on June 1, 2026 at midnight EST (GMT-5). 

  * 2025

  * 2024 (Winter)

  * 2024 (Summer)

  * 2022

  * 2021

  * 2020

  * 2019

  * 2018

  * Catch 1 Violet Bloom Petal in the Pineapple Patch.

  * Collect 50 Neonberry.
  * Collect 25 Purple Potions.

  * Pop 500 Level 10+ Blooms.
  * Catch 1,000 Petals in the Hub Field.
  * Catch 500 Red Bloom Petals.
  * Catch 500 White Bloom Petals.
  * Catch 500 Blue Bloom Petals.
  * Catch 500 Yellow Bloom Petals.
  * Catch 500 Merigold Bloom Petals.
  * Catch 500 Green Bloom Petals.
  * Catch 500 Pink Bloom Petals.
  * Catch 500 Violet Bloom Petals.
  * Catch 500 Cyan Bloom Petals.
  * Catch 500 Periwinkle Bloom Petals.
  * Catch 500 Scarlet Bloom Petals.
  * Catch 500 Grey Bloom Petals.

1 Petal Cub   
  
### Dialogue

Quest name  | Dialogue   
---|---  
Beesmas Begins  | Ho ho ho hoo! Merry Beesmas!! Happy Honeydays!!! It's me, Bee Bear! The magical half-bee, half-bear winter mascot of Beesmas. Here to spread festive cheer with my jolly crew: Festive Bee, Reindeer Puppy Bee, and Cub Buddy too! Yes, it's Beesmas, the most wonderful time of the year. A time to celebrate our bees. A time for making honey. And a time for handing out [Presents]! If you help me complete my quests, I've got many surprises in store for you. Including [Presents] you can give to the bears around the map for [Ornaments] to hang on the Beesmas Tree! Each [Ornament] on the tree grants special boosts to you and your bees, all winter long! With all the boosts Beesmas has in store, it's never been a better time to play Bee Swarm Simulator! Ho ho! So lets [sic] get to the festivities! We've so much to do. The joy of all the bears and bees on the mountain depends on me and YOU! Convert 400 Pollen into Honey at your hive... Collect 200 Pollen in the Dandelion Field behind me... Collect 200 Pollen from Red Flowers... And collect 10 [Snowflakes], which you'll see fall onto the fields every now and then.   
_-During-_ Beesmas is a blessing to every bear, bee and beekeeper. With all the buffs the Beesmas tree provides, honey flows abundantly! NOONE has to go lacking! But to get those buffs, we've got to spread some cheer with these quests. That's how we make the [Presents]!   
_-Completion-_ 🎵 We wish you a merry Beesmas 🎵 🎵 We Wish you a merry Beesmas.. 🎵 🎵 We WISH you a merry Beesmas... 🎵 🎵 Full of honey and cheer! 🎵 Splendid work, little one! We're off to a good start. I've got a few rewards for you: A [Royal Jelly], which you can use to transform a bee in your hive into a random type... And a [Gingerbread Bear], which you can use to purchase goods from my extremely generously priced Catalog! Have a look for yourself. To open my catalog [sic] Gingerbread Bear icon on the right of the screen. In there, you can spend [Snowflakes] and [Gingerbread Bears] on all sorts of goodies! I've got everything a beekeeper needs to start making serious moolah. Now let's continue! Complete 1 more quest, and we'll wrap up the first [Present] for you to hand out.   
Blooms For Beesmas  | Collecting Pollen can be quite a chore if you aren't smart about it! But there are so many magical things that really speed things up. Like the Ability Tokens of your Bees! Boosts, Bombs, and so much more. And there's something new and extra useful this year: Blooms! Blooms are big flowers that sometimes pop up on the field. Collect pollen near them to pop them! When they do, their petals will fly into the air. Catch them for honey and special, powerful buffs! Ho ho hoo. Collecting petals is yet another joyful activity that will make this Beesmas truly special. Go test them out to complete your first [Present]: Collect 2,500 Pollen from Blue Flowers... Collect 25 Ability Tokens spawned by your bees... And catch 50 Petals from Blooms!   
_-During-_ Catching Blooms of the same color in a short duration increases their power. They reach their maximum potential at 100 stacks. Ho ho! That's a lot of Blooms to catch.   
_-Completion-_ Ho hoooo! I saw you catch those! You're quick! But not as quick as me and my Festive Bee. Each Beesmas night, we fly around the world at 10,000 MPH, delivering gifts to every cub and keeper. I don't mean to brag. Just saying though - we're fast! And we've just completed wrapping up the first [Present]! Take this and deliver it to a bear of your choice. You'll bring them so much Beesmas joy! And they'll give you something very special in return...   
Time To Decorate  | During Beesmas, the bears all work to set up seasonal decorations, which are as useful as they are pretty! But they need YOUR help to compelte them. Beesmas is about teamwork, after all! So for my next quest, you'll need to complete someone ELSES [sic] quest, too. The more decorations you help set up, the more honey you'll be able to make! Because each decoration offers special powerful perks and abilities. Now let's get out there and start sprucing this place up! Collect 20,000 pollen from the Clover Field... Collect 10,000 Pollen from White Flowers... Pop 10 Level 3 Blooms, or higher. Blooms increase in level each time you pop them! Catch 5 Green Bloom Petals, which you'll probably see on the Clover Field... Defeat 1 Spider... Deliver 1 [Present] to a character of your choosing... And complete one other character's Beesmas quest, to set up a special decoration.   
_-During-_ Green Blooms grant Critical Chance. Which is absolutely splendiforous! Critical Chance grants your pollen collection and attacks a chance to be doubled in power.   
_-Completion-_ 🎵 Oh Beesmas tree.. Oh Beesmas treeee... 🎵 🎵 Your Beesmas buffs are growing 🎵 🎵 Oh Beesmas tree, oh Beesmas tree... 🎵 🎵 To get the honey flowing! 🎵 Ho ho! Oh yes, that [Ornament] looks so nice up there. It's inspired awe in little Cub Buddy here. And if you continue completing these quests, I think a Cub Buddy may just want to join you! But we're still a ways from there... Complete 1 more quest, and I'll have another [Present] for you to give out.   
A Season For Stickers  | Beesmas is blooming with decorations and joy! The whole map is getting more pretty. So lets [sic] make your hive more pretty, too! Let's find a Sticker, somewhere around the map. You might see them stuck walls [sic]. Collect them to stick on your hive. They [sic] purely cosmetic, but that stuff is important too! Why? Because nobody wants to look at a boring old basic bland hive, silly. Not during Beesmas! That wouldn't be right! We're all trying to look our best out here, for this most wonderful time of the year. Collect 100,000 Pollen from Red Flowers... Catch 50 Bloom Petals... Collect 10 Pink Bloom petals, often found on the Strawberry Field... Collect 100 Bomb Ability Tokens... Collect 50 [Strawberries]... Collect a Sticker... And stick a Sticker onto your hive.   
_-During-_ If you find a Sticker, be sure to look it up in the Sticker Index. And try to learn the worth of each one of your Stickers. Some are quite valuable - if you find one used in quests, you could trade it for some cool stuff!   
_-Completion-_ Oh ho ho. That's a nice [Sticker] you stuck up there. I think you deserve another [Present]! Go hand this out, and stick another [Ornament] on the Beesmas tree. Then come back for another quest. Make haste, the success of Beesmas depends on it!   
Searching For Snowflakes  | [Snowflakes] are so special, and they only appear at this time of year. You can use them to give temporary blessings to all players in your server! They'll be very thankful, because those blessings speed up honeymaking. But think before you use them - because you might want to spend them in my catalog. It's quite a conundrum. Do you save them up for the catalog, or do you use them? I'll let you choose... but you should probably save them. Just saying. Collect 100,000 Pollen from White Flowers... Collect 5,000 Goo by using [Gumdrops] on a field... Catch 50 Bloom Petals in the Spider Field... Catch 50 Bloom Petals in the Pineapple Patch... Pop 10 Level 4+ Blooms... Collect 100 [Snowflakes]... Defeat 5 Mantises, which you can find on the Pineapple Patch and in the Pine Tree Forest... Use Black Bear's Honey Wreath decoration... And purchase 1 offer from Bee Bear's Catalog!   
_-During-_ Every bundle in my catalog was meticulously assembled by hand. by [sic] one of my many Festive Bees back at my workshop. There they work all day, 24/7, without even a peep of complaining. Because they live purely to spread the joy of others! Isn't that right Festive Bee? ... That's what I thought. Ho hoo!   
_-Completion-_ 🎵 Siilent niight... 🎵 🎵 Hooney night... 🎵 🎵 Beesmas quests are quite alright 🎵 🎵 Making honey, so sweet and so and [sic] mild 🎵 🎵 Spreading joy to each bee, bear and child 🎵 🎵 [Snowflakes] make things a bree-eeze... 🎵 🎵 [Snowflakes] make it a breeze! 🎵 Ho ho ho ho. [Snowflakes]! Great work out there! Here, Festive Bee prepared something special just for you. It's a [Ticket Planter]! Place this on a field, and it'll grow over time. Once its [sic] fully grown, you can collect it for lots of special goodies and Nectar, which is extremely important. Complete 1 more quest, and your next [Present] will be ready.   
Let Beesmas Joy Sprout  | Want to know the best way to collect lots and lots of [Treats] and fruits? From Sprouts, of course! They're magical plants that spread gifts all around the field when popped. These spawn every once in a while in the middle of fields. They've got huge spotlights on them, so you can't miss them. They can be quite difficult to pop, so it's best to work as a team! I'm sure other players will just love having you there to collect tokens from their Sprouts. Because Beesmas is about sharing. And who wouldn't want to share with other players??? Collect 250,000 Pollen from the Sunflower Field... Collect 300 Yellow Bloom Petals... Collect 50 Violet Bloom Petals, which are quite rare, but you can sometimes find them on Mushroom, Clover, and Blue Flower field and more. Collect 50 Tokens from Sprouts... Collect 10 Gumdrops Tokens... Defeat 3 Spiders... And defeat a level 5 Snowbear! Talk to Panda Bear to learn more about that.   
_-During-_ Violet Petals are a little tricky, but they show up most often in the Clover Field. They grant Super Crit Chance! It's like critical hits for critical hits! And Super Crits even instantly convert! Sounds like magic to me.   
_-Completion-_ Wonderful! Your work out there has helped spread enough joy to manifest yet another [Present]! Deliver it to another NPC to make them really happy, and to make the Beesmas Tree even more powerful.   
The Presents of Planters  | What ARE Planters??? Do you know? Not many do. I gave you some earlier, but do you know how they work? Well, I know! Because I'm magic. And also because I read up on them on the wiki. Listen closely, little one. If you're serious about honeymaking, you NEED to use your Planters. They are the best source of Nectar - special buffs which grant insane multipliers to your pollen and more! Farming without Nectar is like shoveling snow with your hands instead of a shovel. Or like writing a book with a pencil, instead of a computer... Like, you COULD do it, but why would you?? Unless you just like taking 100 times longer to do stuff. Ho ho! Nectar is magic, and magic is joy, and joy is love and love is what Beesmas is all about. Plus, Planters give lots of materials you will need for crafting serious gear. That's where Festive Bee gets all the fruits for her Festive Gifts, you know. So for this next quest, go plant some Planters!   
_-During-_ Want to know a jolly little tip for collecting Planter tokens fast? Ho ho! Well, just use a [Paper Planter]! [Paper Planters] may not give great Nectar, but they give plenty of Tokens, and they grow extra fast. Don't forget, you can buy them in the shop near the Pineapple Patch.   
_-Completion-_ 🎵 Planter pot, planter pot, planter pot rock 🎵 🎵 They're gonna bring you so many things 🎵 🎵 Wax and Nectar and even a [Treat] 🎵 🎵 That's the Planter pot rocckk ~ 🎵 Ho hoooo! Did you get anything good in those things? Maybe some [Soft Wax]? That stuff is great for crafting even BETTER planters! Now, let's keep spreading that cheer to each and every field. Complete my next quest, and I'll have another [Present] ready for you!   
Slaying the Slimey Scrooges  | The mountain is reall starting to look merry, thanks to you and your bees! But the mountain isn't the ONLY place we need to worry about. Every inch of the universe needs to know Beesmas cheer! And there's one inch which is straight up un-jolly, overrun with nasty scrooges. The Retro Swarm Challenge! A forsaken land, forgotten to time. In there, nasty Zombies and Slimes from the year 2010 are terrorizing the hives, and RUINING Beesmas! They come from a time before Bee Swarm Simulator even existed. A time before the world even knew proper JOY! Sad! And now they've come to destroy our fun in the present. Oh ho no no no, not on our watch. Quickly, take yourself to the purple portal and go clean that place up. Teach that naughty 1x1x1x1 the meaning of Beesmas, by obliterating his forces. If you don't, Beesmas will be ruined, and this [Present] we're working on for you may as well go in the trash!   
_-During-_ Reindeer Puppy Bee LOVES the Retro Swarm Challenge. Why? Well, I think it thinks all the Slimes are just giant tennis balls! A world full of smelly rotting Zombies and giant tennis balls?? That's like puppy heaven!   
_-Completion-_ Ho hooo! We're saved! Cub Buddy was so worried, but you saved the day! Not just for destroying those Slimes. But for helping to fund this whole operation through my catalog! Every [Snowflake] and [Gingerbread Bear] spent there goes straight to my quest reward fund for next year Will [sic] a small percentage kept to fund my jolly lifestyle, of course. But that means it's actually YOU who is bringing Beesmas joy to all the Bee Swarm players! And for that, you deserve the next [Present]. Go get yourself another [Ornament], and keep pumping up that Beesmas Tree! I want it to glow bright and gaudy so that the naughty mobs might know its might.   
Festive Fetching Frolic  | This Reindeer Puppy Bee is useless in the fields nowadays. Why so? Well, with all the falling Petals, [Snowflakes], and Beesmas Lights... He just can't stay focused! He loses track of his ball, and keeps chasing random stuff around. But maybe he's got the right idea. You see, those jolly things are great for honeymaking! Blooms, especially, can amplify your boosting to an incredibly [sic] degree! You might even call it magic! The Instant Conversion they provide can present your backpack from filling up too quickly. Especially helpful when you have field boosts and other effects multiplying your pollen Such as the Inspire stacks you obtain from the Falling Beesmas Lights. So go make like a Puppy Bee and try to catch all the stuff you can!   
_-During-_ Marigold Petals look like a dark, orangish yellow. Sunflower Field is your best bet for finding them. Although they show up in lots of fields, like Clover and Blue Flower.   
_-Completion-_ 🎵 Jingle Blooms, Jingle Blooms, Jingle all the wayyy 🎵 🎵 Oh what fun it is to catch those Petals as they sway!🎵 🎵 Jingle Blooms, Jingle Blooms, Jingle all the wayyyy 🎵 🎵 Unique instant converting will help to save the day! 🎵 Yaaaay! That looked like so much fun. But we don't have time to frolic around forever... We've got many more [Presents] to wrap. So let's get right back into it!   
An Extra Merry Mission  | As a magical half-bee myself, I know how it can be for bees sometimes out there. It's a hard life, digging around in flowers. Shooting lasers out your butt at ladybugs. A thankless life, often. ALL that work for what? A couple hundred thousand [Treats]?? How is a poor little bee supposed to even fit those in its square little body? But it's times like Beesmas that makes it all worth it. Times when the WHOLE world comes together to celebrate these little troopers. Their precious, wobbly little wings. Their derpy, low-resolution faces. They bring us such joy! And they make us a LOT of honey! Let's celebrate them extra hard with this next, more challenging quest. Complete it, and I'll have another [Present], as well as a few more extra special things for you.   
_-During-_ Scarlet Petals are quite tricky to spot. They're basically just slightly darker Red Petals. You can find a LOT of them in the Pepper Patch, if you're not a little noobie who can't even go there. But for everyone else, the Rose Field's not a bad option, too. Ho ho!   
_-Completion-_ Ho ho ho! That was faster than I expected! Your bees seem so exhausted! But also fulfilled. That’s exactly where they want to be - a day well lived! And with that, Festive Bee and I have finished a few extra special rewards for you. Cub Buddy helped wrap a little bit too. It’s not very good at it but we try to keep it busy too. Here’s what you earned: a [Present], of course... But also a [Festive Planter]! This is the most special type of single-use Planter of all. It grants TONS of Nectar and crafting goodies. You never know what you might get! And you’ve also earned this: a [Ticket Voucher]! You can redeem 1 [Ticket Voucher] per day for 100 [Tickets]! A pretty decent chunk, if you need it. But you could also trade it. Now, go enjoy your gifts, but return soon. Because I think it might be time for you to start earning a Cub Buddy of your own...   
The Night Before Beesmas  | Twas the night before Beesmas, and all through the flowers... There were beekeepers farming, for hours and hours. Catching those petals, and popping those Sprouts... Spreading Honeyday cheer, without fear or doubts. Until all of a sudden, oh ho ho, what a fright: The Vicious Bee appeared to ruin the night! And it killed every beekeeper, bee, cub and bear... And Beesmas was ruined. Despair. Despair, Despair. ... We can't let that happen. We still have [Presents] to hand out! And we've even got a Cub Buddy just for you. If you complete these next 5 quests. So get out there and save Beesmas! Put that naughty spikey scrooge in its place!   
_-During-_ Violet Petals in the Strawberry Field and Grey Petals in the Rose Field are super rare! But don’t lose hope - they spawn with small chances. It might take 10, 50, or 100 Blooms but you’ll find them! They’ll show up eventually, as long as you bee-lieve.   
_-Completion-_ 🎵 It's beginning to look a lot like Beesmas 🎵 🎵 Everywhere you gooo 🎵 🎵 There's a spirit in the air, jolly junk is everywhere 🎵 🎵 And there's soooo muuuch snoowww ~ 🎵 Ho ho ho ho hooo! I like snow. Splendid work! Showed that Vicious Bee who's boss! Oh, and that Petal catching - now that was impressive. Your honeymaking moves make even the scroogiest non-beeliever feel the spirit of the Honeydays! Complete 1 more quest, and we'll have another [Present] for you! And complete 4 more quests, and I think this Cub Buddy might want to ditch little ol Bee Bear and hang out with someone cooler.   
Family Festivities  | Beesmas is about celebrating the ones we love most - OUR BEES. But also, we mustn't forget our dear families. Ho ho. That's why every winter I come to this mountain to spend quality time with my dear dear cousin, Polar Bear. But with all the [Present] packing and quest giving and catalog stocking I'm tasked to do, I haven't been able to spend much time with him. Say, why don't you and your bees go pay a visit to my culinarily gifted cousin? Keep him company while Festive Bee and I wrap up your next [Present]. You know, completing his quests for Polar Power is one of the greatest gifts you can give to your bees. Each Polar Power stack permanently increases the maximum energy of your bees. So if you're sick of your lazy bees leaving you hung out to dry, you'll need to whip up some dishes with Polar Bear! And remember, if you complete 4 more quests, there's a Cub Buddy waiting for you!   
_-During-_ Advanced beekeepers will have hundreds, even THOUSANDS of stacks of Polar Power! With that much bee energy, your bees will hardly ever have to sleep at all! Considering that bees spend energy every time they gather from flowers or attack an enemy, that's a big help. So do make sure you're spending time with dear Polar Bear. Those Scorpion Salads aren't going to toss themselves!   
_-Completion-_ How is dear Polar Bear doing? He still up there cooking stuff? Ho ho hoo! You could say whipping up magical recipes runs in the family. Except while he's busy baking boring old potatoes and turkeys, I'm cooking up merrily magical [Presents]!Like this one! It's the 6th [Present] I've given you so far. Deliver it with haste, and return for your next quest. The joy of the Honeydays is at stake! So do make sure you're spending time with dear Polar Bear. Those Scorpion Salads aren't going to toss themselves!   
Black Beesmas  | Sometimes I wonder, what would the world look like without Beesmas? Oooohh... I shudder at the thought! A dark, dark world, without the splendorous glow of the Beesmas Tree. No Gift Boxes to open, no [Presents] to hand out, OOOOhhh, cover your ears, Festive Bee! Don't even imagine such horrors! A world without a Cub Buddy, or equivalently valued voucher, to look forward too... A world without the ridiculously cheap offerings in my generous catalog. A world without value! A dark, dark world, which we shall NOT let happen. By the merry might of questing and petal catching, we will preserve Beesmas for all eternity! We will pluck out every petal of sadness, and cast them into oblivion! As the world shifts and shakes and melts away in the inevitable flux of time - BEESMAS will remain. For as long as there is a spark of consciousness in this universe, Festive Bee and I will work diligently in my workshop! For the ONLY thing eternal is the relentless merry might of BEESMAS. By the great Bubble Bee Man in the sky - SO LET IT BE! HO HOOOO!!   
_-During-_ Black Petals double the pollen you receive from Bomb abilities! Which sounds like a blessing - although too much Pollen can become a bag-filling curse. But Petals also grant UNIQUE INSTANT CONVERSION! What does that even mean? Well, think of it this way. Before your Pollen even has a chance to go into your bag, the Petal converts 40% of it into Honey immediately! And each type of Petal will perform this independently. If you catch 3 different colored Petals, that means around 78% of all Pollen you collect will be instantly converted for the duration of their buffs. Too confusing? That's alright. Just think of it as MAGIC!   
_-Completion-_ 🎵 I'm dreammming, of a Blaaaack Beesmas ~ 🎵 🎵 Where we have finally met our doom 🎵 🎵 Where the mobs, so scary 🎵 🎵 Destroy everything merry 🎵 🎵 And all that remains, is a singular.. sad and lonely... black bloom 🎵 ... Ugh! Enough of all that doom and gloom! We've got [Presents] to wrap, and a Cub Buddy to build! Good work out there, little one! Just 2 more quests until your Cub Buddy is done.   
Stocking Up On Stickers  | Pollen is splendid, and honey's oh so sweet... But there's something else, extra magical, which makes Beesmas complete. Something everyone asked for. And everyone loves! Ho ho! Oh yes, something sticky, and scrappy, and doodly, and just so jolly I can barely stand to look at them. I'm talking about STICKERS! The real reason everyone plays this beekeeping themed game. Stick them to your hive to express yourself, in a very limited fashion. Trade them for Beequips to maximize your profits. Heck, the Cub Buddies themselves are just elaborate stickers brought to life by honeyday magic! Stickers are everywhere! They're stuck to walls, stuffed into Sticker Sprouts, and even given in exchange for eggs at the Sticker Printer. And most importantly, they're sometimes required for quests. Like this one. Don't worry, though. I'll keep it simple. Just a dash of sticker magic to spruce the season up. And in exchange, we'll be able to wrap up yet another [Present] for you to deliver! Ho hoo, sounds fair??   
_-During-_ Some stickers are super valuable, worth a bunch of "signs"! What are signs? They're Star Signs! The stickers representing the astrological symbols. Why do Bee Swarm players assign values in signs? Are they weirdly obsessed with semiotics? Ho ho hoo! No, I don't think so. It just happened. Nobody really knows why! Just roll with it!   
_-Completion-_ Marvelous! Merry! I can feel it - the magic of Beesmas emanating off that Sticker you got for me! Now - we'll just extract that magic very carefully... ...(clip clip)... ...(krumple krumple)... Now we have to feed the scraps to Puppy Bee... ...(nom nom nom)... Watch as his nose begins to GLOW! Cool! And what's that, hidden behind his reindeer horns??? It's a [Present]!!! Ho ho! Wow! Here, you take it and give it to someone special. You'll make their day. Then return for an extra special quest... Cause it's time to make your CUB BUDDY!   
Festive Bee's Trial  | Look around, little ones! Look at all the cheer we've already spread! And it's all thanks to YOU and your merry bee crew! Ho hooo! In fact, I think you may have proven yourself worthy for a CUB BUDDY! ... Oh? What's that, Festive Bee? ...(she's buzzing in his ear)... Hmm. You make a good point. Festive Bee says she's just about convinced. But not quite. You see, she pours hundreds of hours of jolly, joyful labor into each and every one of these cubs. She stuffs them with fluff, fills them with cogs, and then performs the secret ritual which brings them to life! It's a lot of work! And she doesn't want all that to go waste on just any old beekeeper. Each Cub Buddy should only be given to one of the chosen HEROES of Beesmas. And right now, you're more of a sidekick. You've spread a lot of cheer, sure. But not, you know, a magically HEROIC amount. But if you complete this final trial, Festive Bee agrees you will have proven yourself worthy! Are you ready to complete this ridiculous quest to prove Festive Bee wrong once and for all? We all want to see you do it! We're all rooting for you! And Beesmas itself depends on it. So go, get out there and make us proud! Or else Festive Bee might just give this Cub Buddy to someone a bit more worthy. You can live up to our expectations!!! As long as you bee-lieve...   
_-During-_ This Festive Bee is the most magical one of all. That's why she gets to wear a Festive Wreath! It's a special Beequip which teaches her the Festive Mark ability, which grants boosts and gifts to all players! If you want a Festive Wreath for YOUR Festive Bee, well, you'll need to make this Beesmas truly special. We've hidden one in the Festive Gift Box behind Onett's Lid Art... But you'll need to complete 13 Beesmas decoration quests to unwrap it! And no, my quests don't count. Keep up, silly! Ho ho ho   
_-Completion-_ 🎵 Joy to the world! 🎵 🎵 The quest is done! 🎵 🎵 You earned, a Cub, Buddyyyy ~ 🎵 🎵 You caught that stuff, and popped those blooooms 🎵 🎵 And proved yourself finally 🎵 🎵 As worthy to Festive Bee 🎵 And earned yoursellllf, a Cub Buddy!!! 🎵 Ho ho hooo! Yes, you did it! Festive Bee is truly impressed, and has deemed you a true hero of Beesmas! So take this little cub, and add it to your jolly crew! Cub Buddy will stick with you through thick and thin, trying its best to collect tokens. But don't expect too much, it's a baby after all. It's mostly there for moral support! Its cuteness will inspired joy year round. Thank you! You have proven yourself to be Bee Bear's most valuable helper. Keep spreading that cheer! And completing those decoration quests! And then return to me in a while - when the timing feels right - and we'll have some even harder quests for you... Quest that will reward this year's yet-to-be-revealed and exclusive Cub Buddy Skin! What could it be? Ho hooo! That's a Bee Bear's secret ~   
Presents For Petal Cub (1/5): Daisies  | Ho ho! You did it, you earned a plain old Cub Buddy! Sure, it's cute. But what if it could be BEAUTIFUL? As beautiful as a blooming flower... Ooooh, wouldn't that be splendid? Well it can! Through the power of Beesmas, we can yassify your Cub Buddy with pretty pretty petals! But it won't be easy. If you want this year's exclusive [Petal Cub Skin], you'll have to complete 5 brutally bloom-centric quests. Are you ready to catch even MORE petals? I hope so, because Beesmas depends on it. And all the other Honeydays depend on it too. It's April, so let's save Beester while we're at it! Catch petals to save Beester and we'll give you another [Present] to deliver! On your mark, get set, GO!!!!   
_-During-_ Petal Cub flower fact: A single daisy is actually made of a bunch of tiny flowers called flourets bounched together. Every petal is it's [sic] own flower, and every yellow thingy in the middle is too. It's like an entire flower swarm - how appropriate! Ho ho ho! _-Completion-_ Petal Cub Buddy sure is sweet, but also a bit hard to please. We'll have to hope these gifts of flower themed stickers will win it over. Wonderful work catching all those white petals, by the way! Each one you catch spawns 100 [Snowflakes] in a different server. In fact, that's where all [Snowflakes] come from. If you stopped catching white petals, it would stop snowing altogether, and Beesmas would be impossible to complete. Complete 4 more quests, and the [Petal Cub Skin] will be yours!   
Presents for Petal Cub (2/5): Forget-Me-Nots  | Petal Cub is a botany expert. It knows more about flowers than any bee, and flowers are all bees think about. Heck, Petal Cub knows more about botany than I do! And I'm magic! Petal Cub taught me that flowers are made of 4 main parts: petals, sepals, pistils, and stamens. Petal Cub also taught me that there are sooo many different types of flowers. Daisies, Sunflowers, Roses and uh... This one other type of flower that's blue... What's it called again? Hmm. I forget, ho ho hoo! Why don't you go pluck a bunch of Blue petals while I try to remember.   
_-During-_ Petal Cub flower fact: In real life, blue petals are super super rare. The blue color actually comes from chemically altering red pigments. In fact, they're almost as rare as blue petals are in the Coconut Field. I'd guess it'll take around 200 Blooms to find a single blue petal in there. _-Completion-_ TBA   
Presents for Petal Cub (3/5): Tulips  | TBA   
Presents for Petal Cub (4/5): Lilacs  | TBA   
Presents for Petal Cub (5/5): The Final Bouquet  | TBA   
Conclusion (before the [2026-1-16 update](/wiki/Updates#2026-1-16 "Updates"))  | Beesmas is in full swing! But I'm wrapping up a few things for my next few quests... Return in a few days, and I'll have more quests that reward a [Cub Buddy Voucher]! Until then, keep helping the other bears with the decorating. Let's cover this whole map in festive nonsense! To celebrate the bees!   
Conclusion (before the [2026-4-23 update](/wiki/Updates#2026-4-23 "Updates"))  | Beesmas is in full swing! But I'm wrapping up a few things for my next few quests... Return later this winter, and I'll have more quests for this year's exclusive Cub Buddy skin [sic]Until then, keep helping the other bears with the decorating. Let's cover this whole map in festive nonsense! To celebrate the bees!   
Conclusion (latest)  | TBA

Cheering Up Gloomy Cub (5/5): Anything???  | 

  * Make 100,000,000,000 Honey.
  * Use 100 [Field Dice](/wiki/Field_Dice "Field Dice").
  * Use 25 [Whirligigs](/wiki/Whirligig "Whirligig").
  * Use 5 [Caustic Waxes](/wiki/Caustic_Wax "Caustic Wax").
  * Collect 500 [Treats](/wiki/Treats "Treats") from Puppy Bee’s “Fetch” ability.
  * Collect 250 [Cogs](/wiki/Cogs "Cogs").
  * Collect 1,000 Tokens from [Slimes](/wiki/Slime "Slime").
  * Collect 250 Tokens from [Stick Bug](/wiki/Stick_Bug "Stick Bug").
  * Collect 250 Tokens from [Sprouts](/wiki/Sprout "Sprout").
  * Collect 10 Tokens from [Bean Bugs](/wiki/Bean_Bug "Bean Bug").
  * Collect 5 Tokens from Sticker Sprouts.
  * Deal 100,000 Damage with the Slingshot in the Retro Swarm Challenge.
  * Chase 50 Fireflies.
  * Catch 50 Falling Beesmas Lights.
  * Collect 50 Stacks of [Mondo Chick](/wiki/Mondo_Chick "Mondo Chick") Blessing.

  * Use the Sticker Printer 5 times.
  * Admire Riley Bee's Honeyday Candles 3 times.
  * Use Bucko Bee's Snow Machine 3 times.
  * Obtain a Grey Raining Cloud Sticker to give to Bee Bear.
  * Complete Rank 20 of your Robo Party.
  * Summon and Defeat a Level 13 Snowbear.
  * Deliver 10 [Presents](/wiki/Present "Present").
  * Use 10 [Cloud Vials](/wiki/Cloud_Vial "Cloud Vial").

### Dialogue

Quest name  | Dialogue   
---|---  
Winter Spotlight (1/3): Let It Snow  | Ho ho ho ho! Look whose arrived with the snow. It's me, Bee Bear, the magical mascot of Beesmas! Along with my jolly crew. Each year, we come to this mountain to spread Honeyday Cheer. We give out gifts and celebrate bees in the festive tradition of Beesmas! We already celebrated Beesmas this summer, and I said we'd skip it this winter... But by the magic of promotional spotlights, we're back! It's a Beesmas miracle! Now, there's nothing surprising about this Beesmas - just good old fashioned Honeyday grinding. No gimmicks. But, I HAVE brought something special with me. This beautiful blue spinning flame! What is it, you ask? Why, it's the [Roblox Winter Spotlight Standard Token]! Ooooh! If you help me spread some festive cheer, wrap up a present, and deliver it to a friend... This Standard Token will be all yours! It'll take just 3 simple quests. Let's get right into it! Beesmas day is approaching and we've got so much preparation to do.* Collect 2,500 White Pollen. Convert 150 Pollen at the Hive. And collect 5 Snowflakes! Those occasionally fall from the sky onto flower fields. Keep an eye out!   
_-During-_ We had not intended to celebrate Beesmas this winter. Not so soon after Summersmas, nuh uh. But when Roblox gave me this [Roblox Winter Spotlight Standard Token], what was I to do? It would only be right to hand them out to you! Ho ho.   
_-Completion-_ 🎵 Splendid! You've done it! You've completed the easiest Beesmas quest of all! For that you've earned some goodies. A [Micro-Converter], which will automatically convert all Pollen in your bag to Honey! A [Royal Jelly], which can transform a bee of yours into a different type! And a [Gingerbread Bear], which you can use to purchase special items in little ol' Bee Bear's Catalog! I can feel a hint of jolly joy just beginning to bud up. But there's many more quests to do before it'll blossom! Complete just 2 more quests, and the [Roblox Winter Spotlight Standard Token] is all yours!   
Winter Spotlight (2/3): Preparing a Present  | There's nothing more festive than a wrapped up [Present]. Tis the season for giving, after all. But preparing a [Present] takes time, effort and LOVE. Love expressed through completing quests! Complete this quest to wrap up a [Present] so that we can deliver some Honeyday cheer! Collect 300 Pollen from the Mushroom Field... Colect 200 Pollen with the Clippers you can purchase in the Shop to my left... Collect 5 Tokens from Ladybugs. And collect 1 [Treat]!   
_-During-_ A [Present] is the perfect way to express your gratitude to those you love! But if you want to show ME your love, there's only one way. Completing my quests!   
_-Completion-_ 🎵 It's beginning to look a lot like Beesmas 🎵 🎵 Everywhere you go ~ 🎵 🎵 Wrapping [Presents] with our bees, placing stars atop the trees 🎵 🎵 And scooping pollen up like snow! 🎵 Ho ho ho! And just like that, we've finished wrapping a [Present]! I'll hand it over to you, but it is NOT for you to open! Talk to me again when you're ready to start the FINAL quest for the blue flame thing-a-ma-bob.   
Winter Spotlight (3/3): The Gift of Giving  | The love of a bee for it's [sic] keeper is pure magic! Bees give us their [sic] all all year long. We should learn a thing or two from them. It's time to give back to those we appreciate! Deliver the [Present] we wrapped to a different character to show them that you care! But more imporantly [sic], so that they feel obliged to give you something back in return... Help me spread this little bit of cheer, and I'll gift YOU the [Roblox Winter Spotlight Standard Token]! Collect 2,000 Pollen... Collect 750 Pollen from Red Flowers... Collect 750 Pollen from the Clover Field... Use 5 [Snowflakes] to share a soothing breeze with all players in this server... Feed 1 [Gingerbread Bear] to a bee of yours, to demonstrate your gratitude... And deliver a [Present] to an NPC of your choice!   
_-During-_ When you give a [Present] to a bear, they'll always give you something back! Which begs the question, is giving truly an altruistic act? Our motivations mean nothing in the grand scheme of Beesmas.   
_-Completion-_ How jolly! You brought a bit of Beesmas magic to the otherwise mundane life of one of this game's NPCs! And, at the same time, spent some meaningful quality time with your bees! For that reason, I believe you've earned this fair and square. The Bee Swarm Simulator [Roblox Winter Spotlight Standard Token], most rare! May it's icey [sic] bluish flame forever burn bright... Within the Badge section of your profile page - a lil' BSS blight! And if what's described as "Standard"s not enough... Keep decorating the map with festive fluff! Then, eventually, you may aquire [sic]... A flame more elite than you likely desire. If you'd like to keep questing, just talk to me again. There's so much more Beesmas preparation to do!   
Ability Token Tidings  | The bees are so un-bee-lievably talented. In fact, SO talented, that some call them... 🧙 ~ M A G I C ~ 🧙 Ho hoo, and they're right. Every bee is a little bit magical. Just like me! That's how they're able to produce Ability Tokens! Many bees drop coin-looking Ability Tokens on flowers for YOU to pick up. When you do, their ability will activate! And they have so many types of abilities! Finish this one with haste, because your next [Present] is nearly ready!   
_-During-_ Basic Bee and Brave Bee are the only bees that can't produce Ability Tokens. Sadly, yes, that does in fact mean that they are the only bees who aren't magic. Poor little merely mortal bees. But at least they try!   
_-Completion-_ 🎵On the 4th day of Beesmas, my bee swarm spawned for me:🎵 🎵4 Focuses🎵 🎵3 Blue Bombs🎵 🎵2 Honey Marks🎵 🎵And some other cool ablilitiesss!🎵 Ho ho ho! Neat! Those Ability Tokens generated enough magic for us to complete the next [Present]! Who will you give it to, hmm? It's an important decision. Each bear will give you a different [Ornament] in return. And each [Ornament] has different powers that last all Beesmas long. I won't go into detail about them here, but a smart Beekeeper might look up in advanced [sic] which [Ornament] does what. Ok, here's your [Present]! Go deliver some cheer, then come back for another quest!   
Shopping In A Winter Wonderland  | During this most special time of year, we reflect on what really matters. Like family, and our bees, and the economy. It's tough out there for a struggling up-and-coming beekeeper, barely scraping by. That's why I've put together something special: Bee Bear's Catalog! This convenient shop can be accessed by clicking on the Gingerbread Bear timer on the right of your screen. In it, I've prepared a bunch of wonderful wares for you to purchase! So many useful tools and gadgets to help your honeymaking process. At bargain bin prices! But I don't accept honey as money - oh ho ho nooo! My wares can only be bought with magical [Gingerbread Bears] and [Snowflakes]! Lucky for you, these things are everywhere during Beesmas! For this next quest, let's celebrate something truly special... Capitalism! That's what Beesmas is REALLY about. And bees, of course.   
_-During-_ Astute beekeepers might notice that many quests this Beesmas require you to collect specific items. And they notice that, with a little planning, my catalog can be a convenient way to fulfill those tasks fast! And, if you let other beekeepers be your guinea pig first... You might be able to time your quest completions, and [Present] deliveries, in a way that'll help too! It's all part of the jolly jigsaw puzzle that is Beesmas!   
_-Completion-_   
Splendid work, little one! Your funding goes to a good cause. It subsidizes the rewards I hand out for my quests! It also helps me level up my Festive Bee and Puppy Bee. And, if there's anything left over, I put the profits into a jolly ol' investment portfolio. The dividends will secure the Beesmas festivies [sic] for centuries to come! But funding's not enough: Beesmas magic only comes to life through hard work! We've many more quests to go, and [Presents] to deliver.   
Present From A Planter  | Have you ever used a Planter? Ho ho ho! I hope so! Because Planters make collecting pollen and materials a breeze! You plant one in a field, and it'll magically grow all on it's own over time. Once it's done, you can harvest it to instantly collect pollen and many other goodies! But most importantly, Planters give you special boosts called Nectar. Different fields produce different Nectars, and different Nectars power up different things! It sounds complicated, because it is. But you don't need to understand all the details. The important thing is to bee-lieve in the magic of Planters. And to use them to complete this quest! You can purchase single-use Paper Planters in the yellow shop in the 10 Bee Zone. Dapper Bear also sells many reusable planters inhis boutique beyond the Stump Field. Now go collect that Nectar! Your next [Present] is nearly complete.   
_-During-_ Planters don't grow when you're not in the game. Why? Because Planters thrive off the joy of a keeper! When you're gone, the poor planters fall into a listless stasis... No pain, but no joy. No decay, but no growth... Those poor Poor Planters! I would that electric bills not exist so they need never be alone.   
_-Completion-_   
  
Sprout Time For Scrooge Sleighing  |    
_-During-_ Zombies live, er, well, UNlive within the Retro Swarm Challenge. You can access it through the purple brick portal set up by some naughty hacker near the Stump Field. Zombies don't know the joy of Beesmas. They don't even know love or friendship. They don't want companionship or anything jolly. All they want is brains. Gross!   
_-Completion-_ That's the spirit! Scrooges begone! With those pests at bay, Beesmas can truly get underway! Say, have you been keeping up with those Gift Boxes around the map? Festive Bee and I put them there just for you! But then, we also put a spell on them, a cursed seal of sorts. The only way to break the seal is to continue decorating the mountain with the many NPC friends who live here! Just between you and me, there's some good stuff in those Gift Boxes... Keep completing those decoration quests so you can open them! Your bees deserve it, ho ho ho!   
O Thorny Night  | I can fly around the world in under 48 seconds... I can remember the name of nearly every boy, girl, bear, bee, and everyone else... I can lift a sack of gifts that weighs over 70,000 tons, telepathically! But even I have a weakness, an Achilles heel of sorts... My paws are so delicate! One prick from a rose and I may just faint. Ho ho, yes, that's why I don't mess around in the fields myself. There are so many pokey things out there. I stick to doing what I do best while you beekeepers work the fields, all day and all night. So wear your callouses with pride knowing that it was YOU who let BEE BEAR bring magic to the world! With each new callus, your next [Present] draws nearer!   
_-During-_   
  
_-Completion-_   
  
Hark! The Honey Tokens Sing  |    
_-During-_   
Sharing is caring, and giving is magic. And magic is Beesmas. That's why we share! If you can't find anyone to take your [Jelly Beans], just hop over to the Hive Hub. There's so many people in one field there that it's impossible for them to NOT accept your sharing.   
_-Completion-_   
Marvelous! Splendiforous! Truly, truly, nice! By the power of the Honeydays, we've overcome all vice! Farming pollen, making honey, that's what it's all about... Whatever's happening in real life - it can wait, no doubt.   
Festive Planter Prep  |  Puppy Bee! Festive Bee! Look at the time! The beekeeper is already on our 10th quest! Ho ho ho ho! Miraculous! You are quickly proving yourself to be one of Bee Bear's most valuable little helpers. And so, if you complete this quest, I've got something extra special in store for you, in addition to your next [Present]. A [Festive Planter]! The most magical single-use Planter of all! They're jam-packed with jolly junk and festive funk! Surely worth the wait. And they require a LOT of magical effort to make. Poor Festive Bee is wearing herself out! But she told me, seeing you and your bees out on the fields spreading cheer makes it all worth it. Can you make it worth it for her? Can you? Can you ACTUALLY spread such copious amounts of cheer? Because if you can, we'll make it worth it for you!   
_-During-_   
Festive Bees are teamwork bees! Back in my workshop I have dozens of them constantly working together. All consensual volunteers, of course! For this quest, the more Festive Bees, the merrier. Try to find servers with as many of them as possible for faster Festive Gift Token collection.   
_-Completion-_   
🎵Oh, Festive Bee, o Festive Bee🎵 🎵Making gifts is your duty🎵 🎵Oh, Festive Bee, o Festive Bee🎵 🎵So hurry up you cutie!🎵 Ho ho ho! How wonderful! A genuine display of yuletide yearning! You've invigorated me and Festive Bee, alike. And we are compelled to give you this: the [Festive Planter]! And a fifth [Present], of course! Yes, Beesmas is coming along so nicely. You've made such timely progress! You're working so fast, that Bee Bear's run out of quests for you! But don't worry, little one. There are many more quests to go, more presents to wrap... And much more special gifts for you to earn, still to come this Beesmas. Stay tuned and check back within the coming weeks. Until then, partake in the Honeyday festivities. Help decorate the map!Take advantage of the [Ornaments] you've earned so far! Before you know it, I'll be back with much more challenging quests!   
Conclusion (up to March 1, 2025)  |  Come back later and we'll ramp us this Beesmas with some cheerily challenging quests. And, more importantly, I still need to give out this winter's special new Cub Buddy skin! We've got many more [Presents] to make, ho ho! Don't cry little ones, Bee Bear's not leaving. I just got here!   
Conclusion (March 1, 2025 to March 16, 2025)  |  Oh ho ho! Looks like Onett still hasn't added the rest of this year's quests. No Cub Buddy skin in sight! Could this be a Beesmas gift in disguise? Because we're extending the timer through March! Who could have seen that coming? And to celebrate, until Onett adds the rest of the quests, you can use this code: "MarchIsMerry" March IS merry. Yes, very… It grants: 1 [Marshmallow Bee], 1 [Pink Balloon], x5 Wealth Clock, x10 Robo Party Blessing, x10 Festive Nymph Blessing, 8 hours of Beesmas Repentance, the Super Smoothie Buff, x4 Cactus Field Boost, x15 Cactus Field Winds, and 30 minutes of the Cactus Field Code Buff. It'll be removed NEXT WEEKEND, when this year's Cub Skin will certainly be ready. Right?   
Boostin' For A Buddy  |  The Honeydays, enjoyed alone - are they really enjoyed at all? The magic of Beesmas only comes to life when we share special moments with family and friends! And, sure, you've got your bees. You've got some pals. You're doing alright. But to make a Beesmas truly special, you'll need a Cub Buddy to enjoy it with! A Cub Buddy is a special little bear who follows you around, collecting tokens and granting gifts. They're little robots animated by pure Beesmas spirit! They're basically pure joy incarnate. Well, except for Gloomy Cub. Who[sic] is somewhat miserable to be around if I'm honest. If you complete 5 more quests, I'll give you a Cub Buddy of your very own. Or, if you already have one, I'll give you a voucher that'll let you grant a Cub Buddy to someone else! More giving!   
_-During-_   
It's easy to boost fields, ho ho oh yes. Just use the Field Booster in the Blue HQ. Or [Field Dice]. Or [Glitter]! You should ALWAYS be boosting your fields. Or else you're just wasting time!   
_-Completion-_   
Ho ho! Marvelous work! Black Cub says you're clearly a talented keeper and to keep up the good work! Gloomy Cub says this quest was nothing compared to the ones coming up so you may as well give up now. I say we ignore Gloomy Cub's whining and keep spreading Honeyday happiness! Just 4 more quests until the Cub Buddy (or Voucher) is yours.   
The Sweetest Season  |  Beesmas is the sweetest season of the year! What better place to spend it than at this very mountain, overflowing with honey and other sugary stuff. [Gumdrops], Nectar, [Treats], and more! So many sweet goodies the mountain has in store. And all those sweets things help you collect MORE pollen and make MORE honey! So go on then, treat yourself! It's Beesmas after all. Just make sure you're doing your cardio to keep that Honeyday bloat at bay.   
_-During-_   
How do I stay so slim and svelete despite my sweettooth, you ask? Well, that's a Bee Bear's secret ~ ... (Here's a hint: a whole lot of magic and a lil bit of beezempic)   
_-Completion-_   
🎵 Have yourself a tasty little Beesmas 🎵 🎵 Feast on sweet deliiiights 🎵 🎵 Eat until your belly blocks your toes from siiiiiight 🎵 Ho ho hoo! That was yummy. Now your bees have fully satiated tummies! And with that, we've also wrapped up another [Present] for you to deliver! Complete 3 more quests and we'll wrap up your new Cub Buddy as well.   
Retro Ho Ho  |  Cub Buddies are precious and delicate creatures, oh yes. They require constant defending, so their owners must be tough! And there's no better way to build up backbone than to test yourself in the Retro Swarm Challenge. Put you and your bees to the test. Complete as many waves as you can! I'll be watching from high in the sky, judging your combat prowess Just as the Bubble Bee Man watches your every move, judging your moral character.   
_-During-_   
Purchasing the junk food in the Retro Swarm Challenge is key towards advancing to later rounds. The magical rush from all the sugar and additives grants temporary superhuman strength!   
_-Completion-_   
You sliced and diced and collected [Bricks] so nice! Theres (sic) no doubt you're tough enough to defend a Cub Buddy. Festive Bee has been hard at work crafting one just for you... Complete just 2 more quests and we'll hand it over!   
Make Haste On The Honeydays  | The end of Beesmas looms near, yet we've still gotta spread that cheer! Let's do everything in our power to speed things up. If you want that Cub Buddy, you'll need to hustle, hustle, hustle! Collect some Haste tokens and lather up your bees with [Oil] to really get things moving. Hurry up now! We haven't got all year... Unless Onett keeps extending. Fingers crossed!   
_-During-_   
You can only have one Cub Buddy. Any more would result in a fatal overdose of love. If you already have a Cub Buddy, I'll give you a [Cub Buddy Voucher] instead. That is, once you complete this quest, and the next one. [Cub Buddy Vouchers] can be traded to others to grant them the joy of a Cub Buddy! They're quite valuable, ho ho.   
_-Completion-_   
🎵 O Windy Bee, oh Windy Bee... 🎵 🎵 You are a proper menace 🎵 🎵 O Windy Bee, o Windy Bee 🎵 🎵 You blow away our progress 🎵 🎵 Though we may try to run away 🎵 🎵 You terrorize us anyway 🎵 🎵 O Windy Bee, ooh Windy Beee 🎵 🎵 You are a proper menace 🎵 Ho ho! That's right, you naughty naughty cloud monster. Keep those [Cloud Vials] coming cause we'll be needing many more! Anyways, your timely completion of this quest has resulted in another wrapped [Present]! If you complete just 1 more quest, there's also a special little bear in store for you.   
Crafting a Cub Buddy  | While you've been questing, we've been hard at work putting together your Cub Buddy! Festive Bee's been sewing, gluing, and stuffing non-stop. Puppy Bee's been providing the moral support. And I've been performing secret chants and mantras, conjuring up enough Beesmas spirit to make a full-blown soul! We're nearly finished, but we still need YOUR help. Through the hard work and sacrifice of you and your bees, we can finally finish your Cub Buddy! Or [Cub Buddy Voucher], if you already have one. Go fetch some gear and spread that cheer and before you know it, we'll be done!   
_-During-_   
A Cub Buddy follows you around sort of like a bee! But unlike bees, Cub Buddies don't collect any pollen. They only collect tokens. And they're not even that good at that. But they're sooOooOOoo cute! Say, here's a hint: Since you'll be opening [Cloud Vials] for all of these quests, you may as well do it in the Clover Field. They have a 1/250 chance of giving a [Grey Raining Cloud Sticker] when opened there. And that might come in handy later...   
_-Completion-_   
Quickly, give me those Stickers. I'll just palce them right here... ... Oh! What's this? Can it be? Oooh ho hoo! Yes, that did it! Beesmas is saved! And your Cub Buddy is complete! Now you'll have a little helper of your own! Cub Buddy will follow you faithfully wherever you go, snatching up tokens and granting occasionally valuable gifts! You've earned it by spreading so much joy! Every bee, bear and keeper on this mountain is feeling the festive spirit thanks to you. EVERY SINGLE ONE... ... Well, except one... That's right. The Gloomy Cub Buddy. It's still sad, without a drop of joy in its heart. Cheering that debbie downer up will be a WHOLE lot more work... If you're up for it, well, just talk to me again. And we'll try our best...   
Cheering Up Gloomy Cub (1/5): Tools?  | Gloomy Cub Buddy, what is your problem? Your bad attitude drains the joy away from everyone around you. My jolliness wanes at the mere sight of your dreary little cloud! We live in a world of flowers and honey. Happiness and love! The joy of bees! And yet you slouch there, crying. Ugh. It's sort of pathetic. Say, beekeeper. If anyone can cheer up Gloomy Cub, it's you and your bees. If you complete 5 more really hard quests, I think Gloomy might just smile for the first time ever! And if that happens, you can take it with you. Clearly Gloomy Cub Buddy is NOT happy here with me. Now, what could POSSIBLY cheer it up? Maybe there's some tool, some collector, that it likes. Let's give everything a try...   
_-During-_   
Ho ho! That's a lot of pollen to collect with the Vacuum. And there isn't really much you can do to speed it up. Using [Glues] and Field Boosts, I guess. I bet Gloomy Cub will cheer right up after watching you spend hours trying to collect pollen with terrible tools.   
_-Completion-_   
🎵 Here comes Gloomy Cub, here comes Gloomy Cub 🎵 🎵 Right down Gloomy Cub lane 🎵 🎵 From the sight of all those tears 🎵 🎵 You'd think it was in pain! 🎵 Ho ho, but it's not! It's never known any hardship whatsoever. Apparently all those random tools did nothing to cheer Gloomy Cub up. Rats! Well, let's keep trying. I believe with all my might we can cheer it up somehow. Complete 4 more quests and Gloomy Cub is yours.   
Cheering Up Gloomy Cub (2/5): Puffshrooms?  | You know what brought me joy as a wee cub? Chasing Puffshrooms!! Those splendid spores with loads of loot are an endless source of fun. Oh yes. I remember when Puffshrooms first arrived on this mountain. Every one of my quests that year involved them. And every beekeeper was SO, so happy about that. The only tears I saw back then were tears of joy, as people shouted out for more and more Puffshrooms quests! So let's give them a shot! If Gloomy Cub doesn't like Puffshrooms, then perhaps all hope is lost.   
_-During-_   
Beesmas is about spreading cheer and joy, like an infectious festive mold. Yes, just as the Puffshrooms spread from field to field, our joy spreads from bee to bee and bear to bear.   
_-Completion-_   
Gloomy Cub, did you see all those Puffshrooms popping? Wasn't that a sight to behold? Why are you still crying?? Oooh... I just can't take it... I'm... *sniff*... I'm about to cry too! But.. but I can't cry! I'm the jolly spirit of Beesmas incarnate! *sniff sniff* Keep it together, Bee Bear. Don't let sadness win. Please, complete 3 more quests so you can take Gloomy Cub far away from me.   
Cheering Up Gloomy Cub (3/5): Shiny Stuff?  |  You know what they say... "All that glitters is gold... when you wish upon a star." Or something like that. Ho ho ho hoo, nothing brings more joy than seeing something sparkle! In fact, it's the glistening of [Snowflakes] that makes the wintertime the happiest by default! A kiss on the paw may be quite continental. But [GLITTER] is a bear's best friend. So let's try brightening Gloomy Cub's day with stuff that twinkles and shines! Please, Gloomy Cub, just try to enjoy this. You are single-handedly buzz-killing Beesmas right now. _-During-_ Sparkles are the dark, shimmering flower patches that appear when a weird face spits a bubble at it. Such jolly faces occasionally appear on Pumpkins, Pineapples, Sunflowers, and the Bubble Wand on the Blue HQ. Fireflies also leave Sparkles on flowers when you chase them. Kind of gross. But also kind of pretty! Ho ho ho hooo _-Completion-_ 🎵 A kiss on the paw may be quite continental 🎵 🎵 But [Glitter] is a bear's best friend ~ 🎵 🎵 A kiss may be grand, but it's not implemental... 🎵 🎵 Towards your field boost stack 🎵 🎵 Which really is a pollen hack! 🎵 🎵 Love goes cold as friends grow old 🎵 🎵 And we all lose our charms in the end... 🎵 🎵 But shining and twinkling's eternally pleasing 🎵 🎵 [GLITTER] is a bear's best friend! 🎵 Ho ho ho. Spirit Bear's favorite song. But who could have guessed? Gloomy Cub is not impressed! Probably couldn't even see the sparkling with those tears in its eyes. Bah humbug. Well, we've only got 2 more quests to go. If those don't cheer you up, then you'll just have to be sad forever... And that stupid cloud will never go away... permanently blocking Puppy Bee's view...   
Cheering Up Gloomy Cub (4/5): Material Things?  |  If experiences and spectacles don't bring Gloomy Cub joy, then there's only one explanation... Gloomy Cub is incredibly materialistic, and can only find satisfaction through possessions. Sort of sad. Arguably nihilistic. But hey, it works for late-stage capitalism! Heck, I spread so much cheer each year by giving out cheap plastic junk to girls and boys worldwide. So why don't we try collecting a bunch of random junk to give to Gloomy Cub? There's got to be something out there that will cheer this cub up. _-During-_ Bet you had a lot of that stuff already sitting in your Egg Menu, didn't you? Lucky for you! This quest is basically free! May as well give all that extra stuff to Gloomy Cub. Tis the season for giving, you know that! _-Completion-_ Here Gloomy Cub, take all this random stuff the nice beekeeper just brought you. ... Fun, yes? You like those things, right? ... Of course, you don't. Oh, bother. Well at least Gloomy Cub's not as materialistic as we thought. But that means we've got just one more quest to go to make this miserable being happy. If it doesn't work, then I'm afraid Gloomy Cub will never, ever know the joy of Beesmas...   
Cheering Up Gloomy Cub (5/5): Anything???  |  Beesmas is saved! For all but one... Oh, dear Gloomy Cub. What ever can we do with you? Look around, at all the festivities! See all the beautiful decorations, and the splendor of the Beesmas Tree! Does none of that move you? It brings you not even a tinge of joy? Well then. We'll give it one more shot. We're going to make SOOOO much honey, and we'll play every single game I can think of! While you complete this quest, I'll try channeling all my magical powers into forcing a smile out of that scrooge. By the power of Beesmas, we CAN cheer this cub up! We've just got to believe hard enough! Belieeeve!!!! _-During-_ Let's turn that frown upside down once and for all. By the power of bees and all that is merry, let this young bear know the JOY of life! If this doesn't work then I doubt anyone could cheer this thing up. Not even the great B.B.M. in the sky... The all-powerful, and all-knowing... _-Completion-_ 🎵 I don't want a lot for Beesmas... 🎵 🎵 I've already got it all... 🎵 🎵 I don't care about the [Presents] 🎵 🎵 Just let all the [Ornaments] fall... 🎵 *sniff sniff* 🎵 I j-just wish we'd made it so 🎵 🎵 More than you could ever know 🎵 🎵 M-make my wish come true... 🎵 *sniff* 🎵 All I want for Beesmas... 🎵 Is for Gloomy Cub to know what it means to be happy. But unfortunately, I failed. WE failed. Gloomy Cub is as miserable as ever. Downtrodden. Drenched. A miserable sight. But you know what, at least we tried our best! And while we were at it, we made this Beesmas the best Beesmas ever! For everyone except Gloomy Cub. So I thank you, dear beekeeper. You and your bees have brought so much joy to this world! I know I said you'd earn Gloomy Cub by cheering it up, but now we see that is actually impossible. So please, take the [Gloomy Cub Skin] anyways. Get it out of my sight. It's giving me seasonal depression. In the jolliest season of the year somehow. Maybe by following you around it will finally know joy. But that's your problem now, not mine! Ho hooooooo Thanks again for all your help! Merry Beeesmassssss!!!!   
Conclusion  |  My Festive Bee and I can move so fast, that it appears like we're still standing here when we're actually not. We're just popping in every other millisecond to keep things looking festive! Ho ho ho! I'm delivering thousands of gifts as we speak. That's the power of Beesmas! As long as you beelieve.   
  
## Words of Farewell

Multiple announcements were made by Bee Bear before his departure. These announcements are:   
  
---  
Bee Bear: "The timer's not lying to us this time, little ones! Let's wrap our festivities up..."   
Bee Bear: "Our time is up, but there's no need to cry like a miserable little Gloomy Cub. Be thankful for the joy we've shared!"   
Bee Bear: "Ho hooo ho ho! That's it, let's go! So long, farewell, and M E R R Y, M E R R Y BEEEEESMASSSS!!!!! 💫💫"

  * Use [Dapper Bear](/wiki/Dapper_Bear "Dapper Bear")'s [Samovar](/wiki/Samovar "Samovar") 3 times.
  * Obtain 5 [Baseball Swing](/wiki/Sticker#Stickers "Sticker"), 5 [Red Palm Hand](/wiki/Sticker#Stickers "Sticker"), 2 [Orange Green Tri Deco](/wiki/Sticker#Stickers "Sticker") OR 2 [Orange Step Array Stickers](/wiki/Sticker#Stickers "Sticker") to give to Bee Bear.
  * Obtain 4 [Rhubarb Stickers](/wiki/Sticker#Stickers "Sticker") to give to Bee Bear.
  * Obtain 3 [Yellow Right Arrow](/wiki/Sticker#Stickers "Sticker") OR [Yellow Left Arrow Stickers](/wiki/Sticker#Stickers "Sticker") to give to Bee Bear.
  * Obtain 2 [Eighth Note Stickers](/wiki/Sticker#Stickers "Sticker") to give to Bee Bear.
  * Obtain 1 [Diamond Cluster Sticker](/wiki/Sticker#Stickers "Sticker") to give to Bee Bear.
