### Dialogue

Quest name  | Dialogue   
---|---  
Beesmas Summer Starts  | Ho ho hooo! Yes, it's me, the magical Bee Bear! Right on time for this year's Beesmas festivities. I've arrived with Festive Bee, the Cub Buddies, and a metric ton of magical atmospheric coolant! By the magic of bees, it's snowing in July!!! And with the snow comes so much more... Like [Presents] to wrap, to give out to our friends. And decorations to put up, by helping other bears with their quests! And Hive Skins and Cub Buddies for you to earn through completing MY quests! Oh yes, ho hooo! Help Bee Bear spread Beesmas cheer, and you will be greatly rewarded! Every 2 quests you complete, we'll wrap up a [Present] for you to hand out. Exchange those [Presents] with NPCs for special Ornaments to decorate the Beesmas Tree! If you complete 5 quests, I'll also give you [Sticker Planter] and a [Star Jelly]! A [Sticker Planter] can be planted in flower fields to grow Stickers!! What's a Sticker, you ask? Ho hoo, you don't know? That's weird. Stickers are magical little pictures you can stick to your hive! You can earn them from all SORTS of things. Like gathering leaves, or slaughtering foes! You can trade them with friends and learn the harsh realities of the free market economy! Oh ho hooo they're as splendid as they are sticky! Stickers™: they're here to stay! Now, let's not get ahead of ourselves. We've so much work to do, and so little time! To begin Beesmas off right, let's participate in a lil questing delight: Collect 2,500 Red Pollen... Collect 500 pollen from the Clover Field... Collect 500 pollen with the Rake (sold in the shop to your right)... And collect 10 [Snowflake] tokens! Should be easy. [Snowflakes] are falling all over the flower fields thanks to my coolant!  
  
_-During-_  
  
"You can't celebrate Beesmas in JULY, are you serious?" "May as well wait and do it in December!" "This doesn't feel right. It's just sad at this point. Why do people even play this game?" Quotes from nasty non-believers, going straight onto the naughty list.  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
🎵 It's beginning to look a lot like Beesmas 🎵 🎵 Summmmmer's got to goooo 🎵 🎵 Even though the dates aren't right, 🎵 🎵 We'll believe with all our might 🎵 🎵 And maaaake itttt snooooow!! 🎵 Ho ho ho! Yes, it's working! Climate change to churn out the cheer! Good work out there, little one. Complete 1 more quest, and I'll give you a wrapped-up [Present] to deliver to an NPC of your choice! Complete 4 more quests, and I'll give you a [Sticker Planter], and a [Star Jelly]! And after that, there's way better stuff to earn... Like the cute little Cub Buddies standing to your left. Or the Wavy Festive Hive Skin we're standing on now! Don't know what any of that stuff is? Don't worry! Beesmas has just begun - there's still time to learn! Ho ho ho!   
Blueberries For Beesmas  | There's no purer love than a bee's love for its keeper. That's why each year, during winter (or whatever other season we feel like) We celebrate the bees! Beesmas is a time for giving back to our buzzy buddies! BUT it's also a time for giving back to ourselves. With [Presents]! Complete this quest and I'll give you a [Present] to hand out to another character. Whoever you like! That's right, you'll have to pick favorites! And when you do, they'll give you an Ornament to hand on the Beesmas Tree! [Presents] are much too big to give to your bees. They're more suited for bears. Instead, why not gather some [Blueberries] for your bees? Something they can handle! You can find [Blueberries] when gathering in fields with mostly blue flowers. Find some and feed them to your bees to show them you care. That's what Beesmas is all about!  
  
_-During-_  
  
[Blueberries] can be found in fields with mostly blue flowers. Like the creatively named Blue Flower Field, or the Bamboo Field. They can spawn at any time as you're gathering, but usually show up when you collect Leaves.  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
Oh! Your bees look so well fed! And what's that, you've got 10 [Blueberries] left over? For Festive Bee? Ho ho ho - don't think so! Festive Bee hates [Blueberries]. Oh, for Reindeer Puppy Bee? No way! He's already had 10 [Treats] today, don't let him fool you. All for me! Yummm! Much too good for Cub Buddies. Anyways, while you were out picking berries, Festive Bee was wrapping up this [Present]! Now that it's done, you can hand it out to another bear of your choice. When you do, they'll give you some gifts, including a special Ornament to put on the Beesmas Tree! Each Ornament hung on the Beesmas Tree will grant you a boost that'll last all Beesmas! Yes, the beauty of the Beesmas Tree will inspire your swarm to work ever harder and harder! So take this [Present], and deliver it with haste! Who knows how long this environmental coolant will last - We haven't time to waste! Complete 3 more quests, and I'll give you a [Sticker Planter] and [Star Jelly]!   
Tis The Season For Stickers  | Festive Bees love nothing more than to prepare gifts for good beekeepers! My Festive Bees and I work all year long in my workshop to prepare for Beesmas. But nothing could have prepared us for the demand for Stickers. On every beekeeper's wish list - nothing but Stickers! But who can blame them, ho ho! They're jolly good fun. And there's sooo many kinds! Some are little drawings of bees and bears, so cute! Some look like little doodles and swirlies and geometric shapes! Others are obscure references you're probably too young to get. Yes, sooo many Stickers to collect! Each one special in its own way, ho ho! To see them, open up your Sticker Book located at the top of your Egg Menu. The Sticker Book is where you keep your Stickers. At the top of the Sticker Book is a button that opens the Sticker Index. The Sticker Index lists almost every Sticker in the game! Click on a Sticker in the Sticker Index to learn where to obtain it. How convenient! Ho ho ho! For this quest, you'll need at least 1 Sticker. Keep an eye out around the map - sometimes they're hidden on walls for you to collect! After you find a Sticker, you'll need to stick it to your hive. This is vital practice for all the Beesmas decorating to come!  
  
_-During-_  
  
Within each [Snowflake] is a special breeze... Activate a [Snowflake] to share that breeze with ALL players in the server! It'll give you all a brief bonus, a "buff", that'll slightly speed up your honeymaking. How joyfully generous, ho hooo!  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
🎵 Deck the hive with lots of Stickers 🎵 🎵 Fa la la la laaa, la la, la, laaa 🎵 🎵 They'll make it look so much sicker 🎵 🎵 Fa la la la laaa, la la, la, laAA~ 🎵 Splendid work! You stuck that Sticker on tight! But you might want to peel it off later. Why? Well, for the Sticker Stack, of course! The Sticker Stack is a special machine located in the 15 Bee Zone. You can stick 1 of each type of Sticker onto the Sticker Stack. Just 1 of each! Every time you do, the stacks grows a bit taller! And more importantly, it grants you a temporary boost that improves with every Sticker stuck to it! Each Sticker type has a different Stack Boost, which you can see in the Sticker Index. Every time you activate the Sticker Stack, you receive the Stack Boost of every Sticker you've ever stacked. +2% Honey From Tokens here, +1% Red Pollen There... it all adds up! And the Sticker Stack boost lasts longer depending on how many Stickers you've stacked, too! Ho ho ho. Sounds quite complicated, but it's simple, really: Collect 1 of every Sticker to add to the Sticker Stack and you'll be making more honey than ever! Now let's get back to Beesmas business. Complete 2 more quests, and I'll give you a [Sticker Planter] and a [Star Jelly]! And after that, another 5 quests for a [Wavy Festive Hive Skin]... And after THAT, another 5 quests for a Cub Buddy (or [Cub Buddy Voucher] if you already have a Cub Buddy)! So much to earn, and so little time!   
Festive Fundraising  | July, August, December, January - What's the difference? Time is just a concept. Beesmas is a FEELING, a joy, a spirit! That can overcome us at any moment. Especially whenever little ol' Bee Bear is running low on [Gingerbread Bears]. That's why I've brought back this: Bee Bear's Catalog! You can open it by clicking on the [Gingerbread Bear] timer on the right side of your screen. In there you will find a plethora of deals up for purchase. Truly magical offers! But you can't buy them with honey or Robux, silly. That wouldn't be very merry. In the land of Beesmas, we use [Gingerbread Bears] and [Snowflakes] as money! And Bee Bear is running low on funds. Spent a fortune on all that environmental coolant... And even more bribing away the EPA, ho hoo! So please, be a good helper and buy some stuff from my catalog. The deals are a steal! And it's for a good cause! Oh, and grab me some [Moon Charms] from Fireflies while you're at it. Pretty pleeease. Tis the season for giving after all.  
  
_-During-_  
  
How do I do it? How do I offer such low low prices in my catalog? Through a bit of Beesmas magic, a dash of vertical integration, and a lot of economies of scale. Be sure to take advantage of my offers while they're there. Bee Swarm is an unforgiving grind, and the catalog is a sweet little shortcut!  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
That's the spirit! The spirit of spending! Every last [Gingerbread Bear] and [Snowflake] spent on my catalog Goes right back into funding Beesmas! And you may as well spend those [Snowflakes], because when Beesmas is over, they all melt anyways! Through the magic of bees, and the funding of you, ANYTHING is possible. Festive Bee, quickly, wrap up a [Present] for our new customer. You've earned it! Give this to whomever you like to brighten their day. But return soon: just 1 more quest and you'll earn a [Sticker Planter] and [Star Jelly]!   
Wrapping up the Sticker Planter  | A Planter is sort like a Beesmas gift. You unwrap them to reveal secret gifts inside! One of those gifts is always Nectar! Which grants a boost to your honeymaking for a while. But just like with Beesmas gifts, you have to be patient with Planters. They take a while to grow. But they're worth it, oh yes! Ho ho! Especially [Sticker Planters]. They contain lots of goodies, including Stickers! You'll see. Your [Sticker Planter] is nearly done, but it's missing one key ingredient... [Bricks]! Yes, little plastic [Bricks]! A toybox staple. And there's only one place in all of Bee Swarm Simulator to get [Bricks]... The Retro Swarm Challenge! Ho ho hoo! That's right, there's even more stuff to learn about. This game's a merry little convoluted mess. To get to the Retro Swarm Challenge, take a trip through the purple portal made of bricks. You can find it past the Stump Field in the 10 Bee Zone, on the way to Dapper Bear's Shop. There's a suspicious R6 Robloxian sitting on top of it, probably naughty if I had to guess. Once inside the portal, you'll team up with up to 3 other beekeepers to play a special game! You'll defend your hives from waves of old-school Roblox enemies: Slimes and Zombies! And as you do, they'll drop some [Bricks] for you. How nostalgic! Those slippery Slimes think they can stop Beesmas. That's because they don't have a brain. Everyone knows the magic of Beesmas is the strongest force in the whole universe! Go knock some [Bricks] out of those baddies! Show them your merry might! And you'll earn yourself a [Sticker Planter] and [Star Jelly], alright!  
  
_-During-_  
  
[Bricks] are ONLY obtained through the Retro Swarm Challenge. Part of the Classic Event, but now it's here to stay. Hooray! It's a round-based wave defense minigame in which you slaughter Slimes and Zombies. How jolly! To get there, enter the purple portal, beyond the Stump Field in the 10 Bee Zone. Go on then, go spread some festively forceful cheer and get those [Bricks]!  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
🎵 Jingle [Bricks].. 🎵 🎵 Jingle [Bricks]... 🎵 🎵 Jingle all the way~ 🎵 🎵 Oh what fun it is to cut up 🎵 🎵 Slimes and make them pay, HEY! 🎵 🎵 Jingle [Bricks].. 🎵 🎵 Jingle [Bricks]... 🎵 🎵 Jingle all the way 🎵 🎵 Purchase the Illumina 🎵 🎵 And get ready to slay! 🎵 Ho ho ho hoo! You slashed those Slimes to smithereens! And for that, you've earned 2 very special things... First, a [Sticker Planter]! Plant it in a field and let it grow. Then pop it open and let it snow! They ALWAYS spawn a Rare Puffshroom, or better, so maybe hold onto that thing for a future quest *wink* And for your second reward, a [Star Jelly]!!! Use it to transform one of your bees into a random Gifted Bee! Every bee is a gift, but Gifted Bees are the best gifts of all! They collect more, convert more, and grant a bonus to your entire hive! Ho ho ho, now you're really good to go! But this is just the beginning... the Honeydays are about to heat up big time. If you complete 5 more quests, I'll give you what's behind me The [Wavy Festive Hive Skin]! Hive Skins are special Stickers that can transform the look of your hive. Stick this one to your hive, and it'll look 100% more merry! Festive Bee designed it herself. And she's very sensitive, so act excited!   
Beesmas Boosting  | Beesmas is the PERFECT time of year for boosting! Why? I'm telling you why! As you help bears complete their Beesmas quests, you'll unlock many useful decorations. And once you unlock enough of them, you'll be able to open Gift Boxes around the map. Inside those Gift Boxes you'll find sooo many useful gizmos and gadgets to speed up your farming! Between all that, and Beesmas Tree Ornament buffs, Honeyday Honeymaking is much more efficient than the boring old regular grind. Now, why not try doing a boost? The recipe for a perfect boost has certain key ingredients... Obviously, you need to activate a Field Boost, which you get from the Field Booster machines, or [Field Dice]. Then you activate your Sticker Stack, and charge up with magical Sticker power. Then, on top of that, use items like [Oils], [Enzymes], [Snowflakes] and Extracts. Oh, and you can't forget the Nectars! Harvest those Planters for even more buffs! Each buff you stack compounds together for magically insane multipliers! Ho ho hoo! THAT'S the secret to speedy honeymaking. For this quest, you'll need to experience a Beesmas Boost yourself! Oh - and you'll need to find me a [Rubber Duck Sticker]. That's also essential.  
  
_-During-_  
  
Field Boosts are 15 minute buffs that increase the pollen you collect in a specific field by 100% (or more!) You can obtain Field Boost stacks from using [Field Dice], which chooses a field at random. But [Field Dice] can be hard to find, and pricey to craft... That's where Field Boosters come in! Field Boosters are magic machines that grant Field Boosts to random fields for free, once every 45 minutes. If you want to make honey fast, you should use them as much as possible! You can find one in the 25 Bee Zone, one in the Red HQ, and one in the Blue HQ.  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
That sensation you feel: that's the magical thrill of BOOSTING! Combining Field Boosts with the Sticker Stack, and Nectars, and Extracts, and so much more That's the secret to successful honeymaking, ho hooo! And the only way to make substantial progress in a reasonable amount of time. With your boosting complete, another [Present] is ready! Complete 4 more quests, and the [Wavy Festive Hive Skin] will be ready as well.   
Teamwork Makes The Dream Work  | Being the magical Bee Bear is such a joy! But it's also a HUGE responsibility. It's my duty to make Beesmas truly special for every bee, bear and beekeeper in the entire world. And while my magic is potent: I can't do it all. That's why I'm so thankful for you and your bees! For example, I can make it snow in the middle of summer, sure... But I can't collect pollen with my boots like you can. Ho ho, oh no. My precious paws need to breath. Can't stand wearing anything on my feet. And while I might able to able to fly at over 25,000 MPH... I could NEVER match pairs in the Memory Match minigame. That's some genius level stuff! And, sure, I'm capable of telepathically communicating with the great Bubble Bee Man in the sky... But only YOU are capable of collecting Stickers and keeping them in a Sticker Book! That's why we NEED each other. Through the power of teamwork, we can make this Beesmas truly special!  
  
_-During-_  
  
Me, my bees, and the Cub Buddies. We work as a team to spread Honeyday joy! And you're part of that team too! Our newest recruit. Some might say, our weakest link. Not me, oh no, I'd never. But Festive Bee tells it how it is, ho ho! Keep completing these quests, spread that cheer, and prove her wrong!  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
🎵 O come, all Beekeepers 🎵 🎵 Joyful and triuumphant 🎵 🎵 O come ye, o come yee, to Bee Swarm Siiim~ 🎵 🎵 Come finish my quests, to wrap up those [Presents] 🎵 🎵 O come let us wrap [Presents].. 🎵 🎵 O come let us wrap [Presents]... 🎵 🎵 O coOme let us wrap [Presents], 🎵 🎵 Until we get bored!🎵 (That won't be happening, ho hoo) Splendid work, little one! Just 3 more quests until the [Wavy Festive Hive Skin] is ready! And after that, sooo much more to earn!   
The Scoop On Stickers  | Where did the Stickers come from? Not even I know, and I'm magic! I think Doodle Cub Buddy knows, but it can't talk. So we'll never know. But Stickers are everywhere, obtained through so many things, and stuck on SO much stuff. Each collector tool has a chance to find a Sticker in it's image. Makes the ol' Scooper more useful! And there's one way to get Stickers that's even more magical than the rest... The Sticker Printer! Oh yes, ho ho ho! Stick an egg in that bad boy and it'll spin, spin, spin. Then it'll print out a random sticker! You never know which one you'll get. It can be quite thrilling... Apparently it's limited to 1 egg per hour to "temper gambling tendencies". Booo! That's not very merry. Anyways, for this next [Present], I'll need you to give it a whirl!  
  
_-During-_  
  
Not everyone cares about decorating, and that's sad... But even if you don't care about that kind of stuff, Stickers will surely come in handy! They'll power up your Sticker Stack, and can be traded away for items with more utility. For example, for Beequips! Neat little accessories you can stick to your bees to increase their stats! And every good beekeeper cares about stats, ho ho! Min-maxxing is what this game is all about!  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
What'd you get from the Sticker Printer??? I know - you got the joy of spinning! It comes free with every use. Wonderous work, little one. This untimely Beesmas is truly coming together! Now give me those Stickers and I'll give you another [Present]. Another [Present] means another happy NPC, and another Ornament! With each Ornament on the tree, your farming will get faster and faster. And that Beesmas Tree is truly starting to fill out now! Ho ho ho, how glorious. Just 2 more quests to go until your [Wavy Festive Hive Skin] is ready!   
Beesmas Beat Em Up  | The bees are our most precious friends. And they're also our bodyguards. Without them, Beesmas would be ruined by naughty meanie-heads like Spiders and the Mondo Chick. Bees do the dirty work of subdueing mobs so that us bears can keep our hands clean for the festivities! Just one more reason why we celebrate the bees each Beesmas. Oh, have you seen? Our good friend Panda Bear has put up a Snowbear for the bees to beat up! A way to train them to take on even the grouchiest scrouges. For this next quest, why not put your bees' battling skills to the test? Give them a wonderful workout! Toughen em up! I mean, just look at my bees. Festive and Puppy Bee, they're so puny. They're only good for looking cute and eating treats. It's up to YOUR macho bees to save Beesmas!  
  
_-During-_  
  
So many naughty critters, just slowing us down! We're simply trying to put up decorations and wrap [Presents] and they just keep physically assaulting us. WHY? Because they're sad little scrouges, who never saw the light... and never can... They don't believe in the magic of Beesmas. They are truly, eternally, lost.  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
🎵 Frosty the Snowbear 🎵 🎵 Is a very naughty bloke 🎵 🎵 Wears a black top hat 🎵 🎵 While his snowballs splat 🎵 🎵 And freeze up all his foes! 🎵 But your bees put a quick end to that, ho ho! Fantastic work. Beesmas is saved, for now. That means we can KEEP. ON. QUESTING! Ho ho Hooray! And your [Wavy Festive Hive Skin] is nearly finished! Just 1 more quest to go. Festive Bee's been working overtime on that one... Your bees defend us while my bees do manual labor. We all have our own Beesmas burdens.   
Walkin' In A Wavy Wonderland  | We're on our 10th quest! And I'm SOOO proud and thankful for your help so far. But Festive Bee isn't that impressed just yet. If you want that [Wavy Festive Hive Skin], you've got to win her over. It's designed in her likeness, after all. She could DMCA you, ho ho! And she would. So I've prepared an extra special, extra challenging quest just for you... One that will surely prove to Festive Bee that you're Bee Bear's worthiest helper! At least I hope so. She has incredibly high standards. That's why I trust her in my workshop! You'll probably be spending a good amount of time in the Hive Hub for this one. Trading for Stickers is practically the only way to complete these quests going forward! And you'll definitely need the Sticker Sprout that spawns once every 3 hours in the Hub Field. Good luck, little one! Put forth your best efforts! Remember that Festive Bee is watching you closely... judging your every move...  
  
_-During-_  
  
Can't find a specific Sticker? Just look it up in your Sticker Index! There's a button in your Sticker Book that'll show you each Sticker type, and explain where they're obtained. But it might not be that helpful, ho ho hoo. Cause most Stickers are obtained with a hefty amount of RNG! Trading - that's the way to go. Everyone needs different Stickers for these quests, so work together and swap Stickers to get them done!  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
By golly you've done it! You've blown us all away! Even Festive Bee was impressed by all the junk you just did! And now she's willing to give you this: the [Wavy Festive Hive Skin]! Stick this to your hive, and it'll miraculously transform... It'll look all wiggly and red and green. Not the most festive looking per se, but you get the gist! These are ONLY available during Beesmas, one per account, so they're quite valuable. Don't get tricked out there by a naughty scammer! Know what it's worth, and think before you trade it. Now, shall we continue? There's so many more [Presents] to wrap And, more importantly, a Cub Buddy (or market equivalent [Cub Buddy Voucher]) to earn! If you complete 5 more quests, it'll be all yours! Just look at these little fellas. They're too precious to pass up on.   
Festive Flower Fun  | Normally at this time of year, the weather is warm, and the sun is bright. Flowers bloom naturally, and plants thrive in the heat! But by the magic of environmental coolants and chlorofluorocarbons, we've altered the climate and made it SNOW! Snow in July!! Truly a Beesmas miracle! And yet, the flowers are still growing. Somehow they haven't frozen over and lost their petals... How can that be? Well, ho ho hoo, that's a Bee Bear's secret! Just be thankful that I'm magical and I know what I'm doing. This could have gone REALLY bad! For this next quest, you'll need to do a bunch of festively flower-themed tasks. Like using [Honeysuckles]! Turn them on in your Egg Menu and they'll automatically be used when your bag becomes full. They cause your bees to instantly convert Pollen into Honey! What a time saver! But they're kind of hard to get. Planters in flower-themed fields grant them, and you can win them in Memory Matches. They're also obtainable through the Retro Swarm Challenge And even the Robo Bear Challenge, if you're a no-lifer that far into the game. Now, are you ready to begin earning that Cub Buddy? Well ramp up that Beesmas spirit and get ready to spread obnoxious amounts of joy and cheer! Every quest from now on will require more and more effort. I hope you haven't begun losing faith! You can do it - that Cub Buddy is good as yours! You can do anything, so long as you bee-lieve!  
  
_-During-_  
  
"Bee Bear, dear Bee Bear, will Beesmas always be this way?" "Will it return in December? Next summer? Will it ever return at all? Shhh, little one, there's no time for such questions! You've got quests to do, Stickers to collect, and flowers to cut! Beesmas is about living in the magic of the moment, with your bees. It's here now, and that's all that matters! We'll think about next year when my magical chemical coolant starts running out and the snow starts melting.  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
🎵 Have a Holly, Jolly, Beesmas! 🎵 🎵 It's come 'round quick this year 🎵 🎵 See the flowers grow 🎵 🎵 Even though it snows 🎵 🎵 In this changing atmosphere! 🎵 Ho hoo! These are joyfully unprecedented times for sure. Great work collecting those flower Stickers. I'll be taking those now, thank you! Complete 4 more quests and that Cub Buddy is all yours! (Or a tradable [Cub Buddy Voucher] if you already have a Cub Buddy. One Cub per account.)   
Beesmas Bee-leaf-er  | Cub Buddies love frolicing in fields while you and your bees are busy collecting pollen. They do a little work, sure, but they aren't, like, essential or anything. But their cute little faces, and silly little dances, bring us so much joy that they're worth it! Plus, they never stop working, even if they work slowly. They're always ready to collect tokens! That comes in handy when collecting [Snowflakes] or from Sprouts. But they won't help you with collecting from Leaves. To collect a token from Leaves, you need to gather pollen near it. And Cub Buddies don't know how to use scoopers, or any tools. For this quest, YOU'LL have to collect tokens from Leaves. And do other leaf-related things. Why? Ho hoo. Because it's Beesmas, and Beesmas is all about be-LEAF-ing in the magic of bees. Duh!  
  
_-During-_  
  
Did you hold onto that [Sticker Planter] I gave you a while back? Ho ho ho, I sure hope so. When you harvest a fully grown [Sticker Planter], it ALWAYS spawns a Rare Puffshroom, or rarer! That will surely come in handy for this quest. If you already used it, well, just find a friend who still has one! And if you have no friends who have one, then I guess you'll just have to find a Rare Puffshroom the old-fashioned way.  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
Bee-leaf in the magic of Beesmas! That's what makes it REAL! If you stop believing in Beesmas, it all just fades away. Bee Bear too! Oh ho ho no! But I don't have to worry about that, because you're the truest believer of all. Splendid work. You believed so hard that you manefested this [Present]! Quickly, go give it to someone whose losing faith! Then complete just 3 more quests to earn that Cub Buddy / [Cub Buddy Voucher].   
The Season for Giving  | Tis the season for giving. That's why you've been giving me so many Stickers. But you can't just give to one person, especially the mascot of the season himself... Say, for this quest, why not give something to my dear, sweet cousin Polar Bear? Magic runs in our family! I've been gifted with immortality, and he's been gifted with cooking skills! Polar Bear slaves away on the stove 24/7, cooking magical meals for all you hungry beekeepers. The least we can do is complete his quests! And while you're at it, why not give back to the other, less fortunate beekeepers? Hop into the Hive Hub and stick some trash Stickers on the Public Sticker Board. They're not picky. And give them some Jelly Beans while you're at it, will ya? It's Beesmas for goodness sake! If the Cub Buddies can give out gifts all year, SURELY you can give some too.  
  
_-During-_  
  
Reindeer Puppy Bee's Beequip grants him special powers! They cause his nose, and his tennis ball, to glow! And make his Fetch ability grants a Capacity bonus, ho ho! They also teach him the Focus ability. Wow! Truthfully, without the antlers, this un-gifted Puppy Bee's not very remarkable at all. Anyways, go spend some time with Polar Bear. He deserves it!  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
🎵 Here comes Polar Bear, here comes Polar Bear 🎵 🎵 Turning up that flame 🎵 🎵 Cooking up those bugs and stuff 🎵 🎵 That you've mercilessly slain ~ 🎵 🎵 Choppin', spicin', stirrin', slicin', all throughout the night 🎵 🎵 So get ready to eat that feast 🎵 🎵 Cause Polar Bear cooks it up right! 🎵 Ho hooo! He sure does, doesn't he? Marvelous work out there! What a grand show of generosity! Nearly as generous as a Cub Buddy... Which can be yours, if you complete just 2 more quests.   
Rain, Rain, Snow Away!  | Snow in the summertime, rainbow Sprouts, and the offers in my catalog... All examples of incredible miracles brought to life by pure Honeyday Magic! They're evidence that anything is possible, as long as you bee-lieve... When times are rough and gloomy, and you're feeling like you want to give up Just remember the bees, and how they always persevere. Even when they're sad, they just keep working! No concept of work-life balance! Just constantly grinding away. Isn't that right, Reindeer Puppy Bee? ... Not the best example. All he does is play. Spoiled pup. But you get the point. Bees never stop. Ho ho, yes, even when the skies are dreary. When it's pouring rain... When you're trying to get some peace and quiet and the Mondo Chick spawns and just won't shut up... When you're just trying to farm and Windy Bee flings you into the stratosphere... When you're tasked with collecting yet another obscure Sticker to give to some lazy NPC... When you're on your 3rd hour in the Hive Hub, desperately trying to trade that Red X for a Yellow Umbrella... When your quests seem impossible to complete, as if Onett didn't consider how long they'd take at all... Just bee-lieve in the magic of Beesmas with all your might, and sure enough, they'll be done!  
  
_-During-_  
  
You think Beesmas quests are bad? Dapper Bear's quests are even HARDER! Ho ho hoo, popping Puffshrooms and harvesting Planters non-stop. But you've got to do them if you want to unlock [Beequip Case Slots]. Truthfully he could ask you to do anything at all cause he's got a monopoly on those things. Just let me know if he ever asks you to pop a Mythic Puffshroom... I'll put him straight on the naughty list!  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
Splendid, just splendid! Even though you had sooo much to do, You bee-lieved in your bees, and, by the great Bubble Bee Man in the sky, made it through! With all these [Gingerbread Bears], [Snowflakes], and Stickers you've given me... My powers are ramping up to full capacity! And Beesmas is really getting into gear! Just 1 more quest to go, now, and I should have enough to bring your Cub Buddy to life! (Or [Cub Buddy Voucher], if you already have a Cub Buddy. Those take a lot of power to print too.) For now, take this [Present]! And get another Ornament up on that tree!   
Here Comes Cub Buddy  | Do you want to know a secret? I'm barely a bear at all! Genetically, I'm 50% eternal divine spirit, 50% bee, and only, like, 7% bear. That's more than 100% but you get the point. And you want to know another secret? A Cub Buddy isn't even a baby bear at all... Ho ho, that's right. They're something more special. Cub Buddies are delightful automotons, robotic toys, built in my workshop! Festive Bee knows all about it. Her tiny wings assembled each and every last one. A Cub Buddy doesn't eat, and it doesn't run on batteries. Instead, they're powered by pure Honeyday MAGIC! And that's where you come in. Without you beekeepers completing my quests, and purchasing goods from my catalog There wouldn't be enough magic to keep the Cub Buddies alive! Noooooo! So nows your chance, little one, to bring one to life yourself! Complete this quest, and a Cub Buddy (or voucher) will be ready for you!!!  
  
_-During-_  
  
Vouchers are a splendid new invention! They replace the gamepasses of ol', but have a similar function. They're special Stickers that can be redeemed once per account When redeemed, they grant a permanent perk. Like unlocking the Cub Buddy, or Bear Bee! There's also one that grants x2 Bee Gather Amount, and one that grants x2 Honeymaking Speed. These used to be paywalled, locked away by Robux! Now every girl and boy can obtain them freely, by trading away their hard earned Stickers. Just remember, time itself is a precious resource, ho ho ho. Trade wisely!  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
🎵 OooooOOooohh... 🎵 🎵 I don't want a lot of Beesmas... 🎵 🎵 Just a couple Stickers, please... 🎵 🎵 I don't care about your honey 🎵 🎵 You can give it to the bees... 🎵 🎵 All I wants for it to SNOW 🎵 🎵 Though winter passed long ago... 🎵 🎵 Make my wish come trrrururuuruuuuuuue ~ 🎵 🎵 All I want for Beesmas... 🎵 🎵 Is YOUUU! Toooo, Beeee-lieve Baabbeyy! 🎵 🎵 I might have you gathering pollen... 🎵 🎵 Touching tokens endlessly 🎵 🎵 I might have you catching [Snowflakes] 🎵 🎵 Decorating Beesmas Trees... 🎵 🎵 I know it might not feel right 🎵 🎵 Beesmas as a summer plight (?) ... 🎵 🎵 But at least it's stuff to doooOOoo 🎵 🎵 All I want for Beesmas... 🎵 Is for YOU to take this Cub Buddy! I may not be able to hit high notes like Mariah, but just like her I get what I want. And my Beesmas wish came true! Thanks to YOU!! You and your bees brought so much cheer and joy that a Cub Buddy has sprung to life! Fueled by the Honeyday magic that YOU manefested. It's a Beesmas miracle! From here on out, it'll be YOUR little helper (or the helper of whoever you trade it to). Grabbing Tokens, making gifts, and looking cute as a button. But wait. There's MORE! That's right, summertime Beesmas questing NEVER ends! It's like a sick joke, but merrier! It may be unseasonal, maybe even absurd, to continue on with the festivities well into September. Mere months before the rest of the world gets festive... But that's the magic of BEESMAS!!! It's ridiculous, inexcusable, and it is what it is! So when you're ready, come back for more. Cause there's a Limited Edition [Doodle Cub Buddy Skin™] in store!!!   
Doodle Cub Creation (1/5)  | Cub Buddies are one of my proudest creations. Right behind waterproof stockings, and the [Beequip Case]. But the Doodle Cub Buddy is my craftiest creation yet! It's entirely made of paper. Biodegradable, ho hoo! To offset the carbon footprint of all that coolant I've been pumping into the air. And while most of materials are quite cheap, we'll need to make a MAGIC ink. And that requires a hefty dose of Beesmas spirit, and a ridiculous amount of Stickers. Yes, yes, we'll condense down those Stickers into a potent brew. Then scribble up your Doodle Cub to bring it to life! It won't be easy, oh no noo. But we all BELIEVE in you! For each remaining quest completed, I'll give you another [Present] to deliver! And if you complete all 5 quests, the Doodle Cub Buddy will spring to life!  
  
_-During-_  
  
Every year, I bring a new Cub Buddy Skin to this mountain! It's such a joy returning and seeing the Cub Skins of yesteryear stroll by. They're precious reminders of eras past. And also, incredibly good stores of value. You see, my Cub Buddy Skins are LIMITED. That means no new ones will ever be created! The laws of supply and demand dictate that their value can ONLY go up. ... Maybe. I'm not an economist. I'm just a magical Bee Bear, ho hoo!  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
OOh there you are! And right on time. Not to alarm you, but... oh no... My coolant... it's running low! And my magic meter, it's running low too! We haven't much time left to finish your Doodle Cub Buddy! But by completing this quest you've restored some of my strength, and bought Beesmas some time, for now. Phew! Just 4 more quests left, and the [Doodle Cub Buddy Skin] will be ready. Hurry, hurry! Before everything melts away!   
Doodle Cub Creation (2/5)  | Trading is easy! It's just like gifting, but more formal, and with fewer surprises. When you give a [Present] to a bear, they give you an Ornament. See - that's trading! And gifting is GIVING. And giving - that's what Beesmas is all about! Ho ho ho ho! You give me Stickers, and I give you stuff like [Tickets] and [Presents]. See - that's trading too! Beesmas IS ALL trading! Don't you see? Open your eyes! So fortunate are we that trading has been added to Bee Swarm Simulator. Now the Honeydays are even more special, more pure, more highly condensed with giving. And every time someone gives and spreads that cheer, I grow a little more powerful And with more power, I can make stronger and stronger Cub Buddies So keep on giving, keep on trading, and before you know it, your Doodle Cub Buddy will be ready!  
  
_-During-_  
  
My quests work a little differently this year, ho ho. I'm asking every beekeeper for slightly different Stickers. Why? That's a Bee Bear's Secret! Ho ho ho! ... Well actually, it's to force you all to trade. Because trading is giving, and giving is magic!  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
Marvelous work!!! You're a natural barterer. Every trade is an opportunity to spread cheer - the movement of goods The exchanging of capital - that's where the magic of Beesmas is born! That's why bees are always making honey. Honey is money and money is capital! They know what's up. And you know what else is up? OUR TIME! Oh ho ho nooo! Or, almost... I'm using all my strength to fight off the summer! To keep this planet unnaturally cold! You can't tell, but I'm flying around at the speed of light, faster than your eye can see Grabbing ice off meteors and dropping it into the ocean. But I can't do this forever! And we haven't finished your Doodle Cub Buddy! Quickly, make haste, we've still got 3 more quests to do before summertime Beesmas is complete!   
Doodle Cub Creation (3/5)  | Doodle Cub Buddy is a little sloppy, a little imperfect. Just like you! Yes, we've all got wiggly, scribbly lines about us in some way. That's what makes us special! The Honeydays are for spending time with those we love, and cherishing them despite their quirks. Even though Doodle Cub Buddy's eyes are all wonky, I still love it! Just as I love all the bees, with their messed up faces, looking like they were drawn by a 4 year old. And those Stickers you gather for me! Sloppy reminders that things don't have to look perfect To be valuable. As long as you believe... ... In... keeping things messy? I've lost my train of thought. Ho ho ho ho! The point is, we've ALL got a little Doodle Cub Buddy in us. Finish 3 more quests, and unleash that Doodle Cub Buddy onto the world!  
  
_-During-_  
  
Bees have keen eyes and can see things we can't see. Well, I can see them, because I'm part bee, but you can't see them. That's why certain Stickers can only be found by specific bees, when gathering in specific fields. Check the Sticker Index to find which ones. Sometimes a certain type of bee has to be feeling a certain way to find something. Spending time in fields the bees love, or hate, can change their moods. Pay attention to the little emotion bubbles they display when you enter a field. That will surely come in handy! Ho ho ho!  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
🎵 I'mmmm dreamining 🎵 🎵 Oh a whiiiiite Beesmas 🎵 🎵 Though December passed long ago... 🎵 🎵 It's a little tougher, to bee-lieve in Summer... 🎵 🎵 But by Beesmas magic, make it so! 🎵 Ho ho ho ho hooo! Look, everyone! The beekeeper somehow got all those Stickers I asked for! Now THAT'S a real Beesmas miracle. I know it seems like a lot to ask for, but it's the only way to bring your Doodle Cub Buddy to life! And you can't take THIS Doodle Cub Buddy. That's mine. It's there for display. You'll need to earn yours the hard way! For now, take this [Present] we've just finished wrapping. After that, just 2 more quests. They're a doozy though!   
Doodle Cub Creation (4/5)  | Oh dear! Oh golly! All my magical, climate altering chemicals have run out! My methane, my micro-plastics, my hydrofluorocarbons - ALL GONE! That means summertime Beesmas is running on pure Honeyday magic alone... And that's ONLY generated through your belief in me, and my quests! Oh no no no, it's too soon. TOO SOON! But we should have just enough time to scribble up your Doodle Cub. Festive Bee's already done all the papercrafting and glue-sticking. All that's left is for you to collect a ridiculous menagerie of random Stickers And to give them to me with haste! Oh, and to purchase more bundles from my insanely low-priced catalog! Yes yes, that's the ONLY way we can string this untimely Beesmas along any further. Bubble Bee Man in the sky, have mercy on us! Lend us your strength and push just a little longer! My little helper has just 2 quests to go!  
  
_-During-_  
  
Doodle Cub Buddies are a bit flimsy. Don't get them wet! We spent all our funding last Beesmas on those Peppermint Robo Cubs. [Cogs] and Candycanes are expensive, so now we have to cut back. Cardboard, cardstock, and glue sticks. That's all we can afford. But with enough magic, and Stickers, that's all we'll need! Ho hoo!  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
Just in time! The Beesmas Tree is starting to wilt! But by completing this quest, I think you've bought it a bit more life. Now there's just 1 MORE quest to complete before summer Beesmas fully satisfies And your Doodle Cub Buddy will be ready to join your jolly little Bee Swarm family. Festive Bee, prepare those markers! Cub Buddies, get ready to scribble! Puppy Bee, don't bite those Stickers! Get ready, everyone, for the final quest!!!   
Doodle Cub Creation (5/5)  | They said it couldn't happen. That is shouldn't happen. That Beesmas in July, August, and September, would be an affront to nature. That it would feel too weird, too out of place. Embarassing, really. And by golly, they may have had a point... But reasonable points mean NOTHING in the face of magic. Belief is innately illogical! And magical entities like myself can not be reasoned with. So here we are, at our FINAL quest, despite all odds. You've spread so much joy and participated in so many festivities! And now it's time for it all to pay off. Complete this final quest, and the [Doodle Cub Buddy Skin] will be yours! It will be a challenge, but don't faulter or despair. By the sunk-cost-fallacy of all the Stickers you've already given me, you're nearly there!  
  
_-During-_  
  
Half the Stickers you collect for me are shredded up and boiled down into magical ink. The other half, I send Festive Bee to trade them away in the Hive Hub. Where do you think I get all the stock for my catalog, silly?? Beesmas is 50% magic and 50% capitalism. And 100% WORTH IT! Finish this final quest to obtain that Doodle Cub Buddy and see for yourself!  
  
_-Completion-_  
  
🎵 Ding dong, ding dong 🎵 🎵 Ding dong ding dong ~ 🎵 🎵 Ding dong 🎵 🎵 Ding Dong 🎵 🎵 ding dong.. 🎵 🎵 Ding Dong... 🎵 🎵 Joy to the bees 🎵 🎵 It's finally doone 🎵 🎵 All 20 quests completeee! 🎵 🎵 Despite the summer suuuun 🎵 🎵 We had our festive fuuun 🎵 🎵 Collecting Stickers like these 🎵 🎵 While spending time with our bees 🎵 🎵 And doodling up our Cub Buddiesss ~ 🎵 Ho ho hOOO HOOOOOOOOOO! Simply splendiferous!!! You've saved summertime Beesmas and completed every last of my quests! You are, without a doubt, Bee Bear's specialest little helper! And you've proven yourself worthy of this: the [Doodle Cub Buddy Skin]! This cardstock cub will be your loyal companion, from now until forever! That is, unless, you trade it away! Ho hoo! Which you might do, because they're EXTREMELY rare and valuable. Not many people are as dedicated as you! We've held off the summer just long enough. My coolants are out, and my magic grows weak... Unfortunately Beesmas must come to an end, for now... And it probably won't return in December or January because, well, that's just a few months away. My Festive Bees and I need time to restock the workshop, ho ho. But we'll be back, someday, somehow. As long as beekeepers like you continue to BELIEVE. For now, have fun with the Doodle Cub! And if you're not quite burned out of Beesmas, there's still a bit more to do before the snow melts away... Keep helping other bears with their decorations, and decorating that Beesmas Tree. And when all that's done, look towards the sky... There's a magical man / bee up there who might be waiting for you! So for now, so long and farewell Thank you for all those Stickers, and for all the joy you've spread! Until next time, stay festive! Merrrrrrry Beesmasssss! Gooodbyeeeee!   
Conclusion  | Festive Bee and I can move so fast, that it appears like we're still standing here when we're actually not. We're just popping in every other millisecond to keep things looking festive! Ho ho ho! I'm delivering thousands of gifts as we speak. That's the power of Beesmas! As long as you beelieve.   
  
## Words of Farewell

Multiple announcements were made by Bee Bear and Sun Bear before their departure. These announcements are:   
  
---  
Bee Bear: "Ho ho hooo, say it ain't so!! In under an hour, there'll be NO more snow 😔..."   
Bee Bear: "The memories of Beesmas will be with you forever! Hours of endless grinding and trading and hoping and begging - cherished memories burned into your brain!"   
Bee Bear: "Beesmas must end, and my jolly crew and I must go. But we'll be back, someday! Not this December, but someday! When? Well... that's a Bee Bear's secret~"   
Bee Bear: "So looong, farrrewell, and may your actual Winter be just as jolly as our Summersmas! Meerrry merry merry merry, MERRY BEESMAS TO ALL!!!! Except for Sun Bear"   
Sun Bear: "Uhh…"

### Dialogue

Quest name  | Dialogue   
---|---  
Robotic Beesmas  |  Ho ho ho! Look who's arrived with the snow! It's me, Bee Bear! The magical mascot of this most wonderful time of year... A time of joy! A time of gifts! A time of bees! Yes, it's Beesmas!!! The season where we all celebrate and cherish those most important [sic] in our lives. Our bees! I've brought along my entire crew... There's Festive Bee! And Reindeer Puppy Bee! And the Cub Buddy too! But that's not all, oh ho ho, oh no. I've brought along someone a little more novel than those guys. The Robo Bear!!! Or so that's what I've been told its name is. It's a gift from a secret friend of sorts. And Beesmas is all about the joy of gifts!!! How apt! Ho hooo! The Robo Bear is near the Rose Field, in the 15 Bee zone. (If you don't have 15 bees, well, you can peek at it from the Sunflower Field) It's got a nifty little cog-themed shop set up! Jankilly jolly! But don't get too excited just yet. The Robo Bear isn't working, you see... It's broken! Broken because of a lack of Beesmas joy! There's only 1 way to fix it: by partaking in Honeyday festivities! Each time you give a [Present], that poor Robo Bear's mechanical heart heals just a bit. Every time you complete one of my joyous quests, a short circuit repairs itself. And every purchase you make from my oh so generous Catalog Which you can access by pressing the [Gingerbear] timer on the right hand side of your screen Tightens its bolts and untangles its wires! Ho ho ho ho! You see, we'll all benefit! So let's get to making those [Presents]. There's no time to lose! Let's spread that joy before Robo Bear blows another fuse! _-During-_ Some say my Cub Buddies are robots too! I suppose they are in a way. But instead of batteries, they operate on magic! Cub Buddy - give the beekeeper some of that magic so they can complete my quest. _-Completion-_ 🎵 It's beginning to look a lot like Beesmas 🎵 🎵 The maps _[sic]_ covered in snow 🎵 🎵 We'll make [Presents] through the night 🎵 🎵 Spreading Honeyday delight 🎵 🎵 To repair RO-BO 🎵 Ho ho! Spectacular! We're one step closer to making a [Present]. It takes 2 of my quests to make a [Present]. That's how special they are! And it'll take at least 5 of my quests before the Robo Bear even starts to begin healing. Beesmas is joyful! But Beesmas ain't easy! For now, I've got a [Gingerbread Bear] just for you! But think twice before you take a bite. You just might want to spend that bear in my Catalog.   
Let's Get Festive  |  The bees do so much for us all year long. Beesmas is our chance to show them that we appreciate their hard work! But that doesn't mean we don't treat ourselves at the same time. If you complete this quest, I'll give you a [Present]! But it's not for you, you see... Not directly at least. Give it to any other bear of your choice and they'll give something back! That's what Beesmas is all about! Getting something back! For example: I took this [Glitched Drive] from the Robo Bear. And in return, I gave it a ride to Bee Swarm Simulator! Ho ho ho! Now, if we're going to finish this [Present], we'll need a little jovial inspiration. Let's decorate this place up a bit! If it weren't for me and my bees, you'd never know it was Beesmas. How sad! Go, go! The fates of Beesmas _[sic]_ and the poor Robo Bear are both on the line! _-During-_ [Snowflakes] are one of the simple joys of winter. Using a [Snowflake] forces that simple joy on ALL players in the server. They'll be sure to appreciate it! _-Completion-_ Good work little one! Yes, I could admire Black Bear's wreath all day! It's much nicer than the one Festive Bee insists on wearing. Now that that quest is done, we can finish this [Present]! Deliver it at once, don't be shy! You'll bring pure Beesmas joy to whoever you choose to give it to! Just 3 more quests and I think Robo Bear might just recover!   
The Joy of Buying  |  The joy of bees is free! But the items in my Catalog are not. No no, ho ho. That's just part of the festive fun! If you want that Robo Bear to come back to life, I suggest you take a look at my generous offerings. You might just find something extra magical! My deals will bring your bees endless joy, guaranteed. Trust me! I'm Bee Bear! I know what bees like best because I'm one too. And I also know that Puppy Bee's gotta eat. And he can't eat if I'm not getting paid! I may be magic but I'm not made of money. Ho ho ho! So go gather some [Gingerbread Bears] and some [Snowflakes]! Those are the most magical currencies imaginable! And the only ones I accept. Don't worry though little one, hunting for those is a cherished Beesmas tradition! _-During-_ Puppy Bee eats 1 in every 3 [Gingerbread Bears] we earn through our Catalog sales. It's a miracle he hasn't become Gifted yet! A Beesmas Miracle! Yes, it's just magic that Puppy Bee refuses to gain a Gifted Hive Bonus. I sure love spending an unreasonable amount on [Treats] just to level my bees because you refuse to change color. Thanks Puppy Bee! Anyways, remember: You can access my Catalog at any time by pressing the [Gingerbread Bear] clock icon. You can't miss it. It's conveniently located on the right of your screen at all times. _-Completion-_ 🎵 Oh the Catalogs so delightful 🎵 🎵 But inflations _[sic]_ gotten frightful 🎵 🎵 So if Beesmas is to undergooo 🎵 🎵 Spend that dough, spend that dough, spend that dough! 🎵 Gingerbread dough, preferably. Ho ho ho! Fantastic job! You and your bees are such jolly little helpers. And quite eager spenders as well! Now that's what I call a Beesmas miracle! Oh, and the Beesmas Tree is beginning to fill out with delightful [Ornaments]! Beesmas has truly begun! Complete 1 more quest and I'll have another [Present] ready for you to deliver. And complete just 2 more quests, and I think Robo Bear might just start to feel the magic!   
The Gift of Gifts  |  Have you happened to notice something else around the map? Something a little less coggy than that broken ol' Robo Bear? Something a little more jolly... a little more boxxy... A little bit GIFT boxxy? Surprise!! I brought those gift boxes specifically for you and your bees! But hold your horses: You can't open them just yet! That's not how Beesmas works, silly! Oh ho hoo, not at all. You'll need to help even more bears decorate the map first! They're sealed shut by a seal that can only be broken with Beesmas magic. Just like the Robo Bear. Only a sufficiently joyous aura can activate them! But I feel it in my heart: we're nearly there! Go spread that Beesmas magic! _-During-_ Gifts don't come for free! That's one of the rules of Beesmas. For example, my gift of allowing Robo Bear to hitch a ride to this mountain Was EARNED when it gave me it's oh so delightful [Drives]. The joy of earning your way in life, without expecting handouts... That's what Beesmas is all about! Ho ho! _-Completion-_ By golly you've done it! You EARNED those gift boxes! And you've earned this [Present] too! They're different things, I realize the names are similar. But you haven't earned my most special present just yet... There's one more quest to go. And it just may bee _[sic]_ too merry for you to handle. Ho ho! We'll see! I hope not! Beesmas itself depends on you!   
Decorating For Drives  |  That poor Robo Bear looks all beat up! Run down! Broken! Ready to be scrapped for parts! Oooh, you'd think saving it would be impossible. But anything is possible, as long as you bee-lieve... I have a feeling if you and your bees complete 1 more quest Robo Bear can be saved. Saved by the magic of Beesmas. By the joy of collecting pollen! The joy of making honey! The joy of Tokens! And the joy of whatever other random stuff we do during this most merry time of year. You see - I've got a magical little device in my pocket! It fizzles and rizzles, it shines and spin-dizzles! It glows with all the colors _[sic]_ of the Beesmas lights! And it sells for a considerable price on certain unregulated markets! It's the [Glitched Drive]! Ho ho! This thing has brought me and my jolly crew so much joy. Festive Bee uses it as a paperweight when wrapping gifts. Cub Buddy and Puppy Bee throw it around and play a little fetch. And I, well, I plan on using it to reward a very special beekeeper. Spread just a bit more Honeyday happiness, and that beekeeper might just be you! _-During-_ Yes, that [Glitched Drive] and much more fits right in my magical pocket. Where are my pockets? That's a Bee Bear's secret! _-Completion-_ 🎵 You better watch out 🎵 🎵 It's coming for war 🎵 🎵 You better level up your bees some more 🎵 🎵 Robo Bear is coming, to tooooooown 🎵 Ho ho hoo! You've done it! You've done just enough to make this place recognizably festive! The teamwork between you and your bees is truly magical. Soo magical, that I think the Robo Bear might just have a chance! What's this? The [Glitched Drive]... it's spinning at full speed! It must have felt the magic too! Ho ho! Oh yes, and now that it's spinning this much I can finally give it to you! Couldn't have otherwise. That wouldn't have been very merry now would it. Take this [Glitched Drive], and seek out the remaining [Drives]! If you haven't already, maybe take another peek in my Catalog... And when you've found them all, repair the Robo Bear! But beware - that bear is unlike the others. How do I know? Because I'm a magical bear that can see everything. Ho ho ho! Well, that was fun. But we're not done yet. Come back soon and I'll have many more merry quests for you.   
Commence The Wrapping  |  Robo Bear may be saved, but Beesmas is still in jeopardy! We've got so much more joy left to spread. If you complete 5 more of my quests, I've got a special gift for you... A [Festive Planter], and more! [Festive Planters] are packed full of all sorts of jolly junk. They're fantastic! If you want to experience all that junk for yourself, you better get to work! Ho ho ho! _-During-_ Regular Planters are such a bore. [Festive Planters] bring joys galore! Complete 5 of my quests, and you'll see for yourself! _-Completion-_ Splendid! Ho hooooo! Things are much merrier now that those naughty Rhino Beetles have been put in their place. And for that, you've earned yourself another [Present]! While you were doing that quest, Festive Bee's been wrapping and stuffing stuff with gifts. Just 4 more quests and that [Festive Planter] should be ready!   
Shiverin' In The Snow  |  Ho ho ho hoo! Don't you just love all this snow??? The mountain is sooo much merrier decked out in white. [Snowflakes] and blizzards - winter's most wonderful delight! Yes, bees love nothing more than being chilled to their core. They don't mind the cold at all! They enjoy shivering away in snow covered flowers! Isn't that right Festive Bee? (She can't talk but I know she agrees) So go frolic for a bit and have some freezing fun! _-During-_ Spiders... yuck! What's with all those legs? Anything with that many legs goes straight on the naughty list. Go shoo them off! Go! There's no room for them in a winter wonderland. _-Completion-_ 🎵 I'mm dreaming ~ 🎵 🎵 Of a Whiiiite, Beesmas.... 🎵 🎵 Where flowers wilt beneath the snoooow 🎵 🎵 Where the spiders wither 🎵 🎵 While the bears all shiver 🎵 🎵 And the honey meters overfloow 🎵 Ho ho ho! Feeling a little TOO cold? Don't worry little one! We'll warm you and your bees up with the next quest. For now, take these gifts! But if you want that [Festive Planter], you've got 3 more quests to go. Ho ho.   
Holly Jolly Gingerbread  |  Regular [Treats], they're fine and all... But [Gingerbread Bears] are truly magic! Feeding one to any of your bees has a chance to turn them Gifted! Gifted Bees have increased powers. That's because they're smarter! Not everybody is born gifted - that's a sad fact of life. Ho ho ho! But they can be made gifted. Through [Gingerbread Bears]!!! How does that work? They're spiked with the magic of Beesmas! So go give some gifts and gather some [Gingerbread Bears]. Beesmas can't be saved without them! _-During-_ Cub Buddies can't enjoy [Gingerbread Bears]. They're stricktly _[sic]_ for the bees. Unfortunately that means the Cub Buddies will remain ungifted forever. That's ok though. They've got other stuff going for them. It doesn't take a gifted intellect to collect tokens. Oh no, ho ho! _-Completion-_ Look at all those jolly [Gingerbread Bears]! Lookin' so scrumptious! But think twice before taking a bite. They're too special for beekeepers! They're only for the bees. No exceptions. Except for 1 very important exception: Spending them in my ridiculously reasonably priced Catalog! Now deliver this [Present] and let us continue the festivities. There's so much more to do! Just 2 more quests, and I've got a special [Festive Planter] just for you!   
A Special Gift for Snowbear  |  All year long, every little thing you do... Every step you take. Every move you make. Someone's watching you. Ho ho ho, it's weird but true! There's someone in the sky, way way up high Keeping a list of whose been naughty and whose been nice. But I can tell you right now, there are some who are ALWAYS naughty. For example, that Snowbear! That awful awful grinch! Each year I celebrate when it melts away, and I cry when Panda Bear brings it back. There's nothing nice about throwing snowballs at poor little innocent beekeepers! I don't care how much loot it gives! You can't bribe your way onto the nice list. So why don't you and your bees go teach it a lesson? Let's deliver to Snowbear the only gift it deserves[.] Have your bees beat it up for the sake of Beesmas! _-During-_ Having trouble collecting the items for my quest? I can't help you. I'm sorry. I don't come up with those tasks. Festive Bee's the one who makes the [Festive Gifts]. She insists these items are necessary. If you have any complaints, you'll need to write her a letter. But do keep in mind that bees probably don't know how to read. _-Completion-_ 🎵 Rockin' around, the Spider Field 🎵 🎵 While the Snowbear's snowflakes drop 🎵 🎵 We'll be dodging snowballs until it yeilds _[sic]_ 🎵 🎵 And it's reign of terror stops! 🎵 Ho hoo! Your bees really dug into that thing. It deserved it though! Now that it's gone, we can go back to spreading joy without fear! At least for 2 hours. Just 1 more quest, and I'll spread the joy of a [Festive Planter] into your inventory.   
Finishing The Festive Planter  |  The magic of Beesmas is truly beginning now! And it's all thanks to you and your adorable little bee helpers. While you've been hard at work, Festive Bee's been working even harder! And your [Festive Planter] is nearly done! We've made so much progress, in fact, that I've even had some spare time to whip up some [Super Smoothies]! [Super Smoothies] are super sweet and splendid. And very very expensive! But if you complete this quests _[sic]_ , I'll give you some along with your [Festive Planter]! A little bonus for being sooo nice this year. Now, we've collected most of the items needed already, but there's a few more on the list. Not much, ho ho oh no. Just a few stragglers. You round those up, and we'll make it worth your while! _-During-_ Just a few little knick knacks left to collect. Nothing major. But before you know it it'll be done! Just remember, collecting is all part of the magically merry fun! _-Completion-_ Whats _[sic]_ this? Could it be... You've collected it all! How merrilly _[sic]_ marvelous! I was lying when I said it wasn't very much. I expected it to take much longer. But you completed it sooo fast that the [Super Smoothies] haven't even melted at all! See how fast we can get things done when we just believe in ourselves? When we believe in our bees? When we bee-lieve in the magic of Beesmas! And for that, you've earned this: the [Festive Planter]! And 3 [Super Smoothies]! And 1 [Loaded Dice]! And 1 [Red Balloon]! And 1 [Robo Pass]! And 1 [Present]! Go celebrate and cherish those rewards, maybe do a boost or two! But don't celebrate TOO long. Ho ho ho. That's right... There will be even more quests very soon. Quests to earn yourself a [Cub Buddy]! Or a [Star Treat], if you already have one. Two of the most magically merry gifts I can possibly fathom. So check back in with me soon. But for now, enjoy! Ho ho ho ho ho!   
Merry Mechsquitos  |  If you complete 5 more of my quests, you'll earn yourself a little robot bear of your own: A [Cub Buddy]! A magical little automaton, animated by pure joy and Honeyday cheer. They collect Tokens and look cute doing it! Just check out the one to my left, toddling around on that box. Adorable! Ho hooo! (If you already have a Cub Buddy, I'll give you a [Star Treat] instead! Pretty much the same.) Now, hows [sic] about we check in with that Robo Bear now that it's had time to rev up. We brought it to life with Beesmas magic. Let's find out if that was a mistake! _-During-_ The Robo Bear was super heavy. Reindeer Puppy Bee had to put it [sic] some real sweat to haul that thing. If I would have known it would be that heavy, I would have asked for more [Drives]! _-Completion-_ 🎵 Jingle cog, Jingle cog, Jingle cog rock 🎵 🎵 Jingle cogs clang and Jingle cogs cling 🎵 🎵 Sounds real neat-o as you bash those Mechsquitos 🎵 🎵 That's the jingle cog roooock ~ 🎵 Hooo ho ho! It was snowing Mechsquito parts all over the place! Guess it might not have been a good idea to repair that Robo Bear after all. Oh well! Ho ho! It's too strong to stop it now and Beesmas must go on. Just 4 more quests and that [Cub Buddy] (or [Star Treat]) is yours!   
Courtesies For A Cousin  |  Every bee is special, each and every one! Beesmas is all about remembering that. But just like the bees, every bear is special, too! Except for Snowbear. And Robo Bear I guess. But there's one bear who's _[sic]_ super extra special, especially to little ol' Bee Bear. Take a guess... It's Polar Bear! My culinarily gifted cousin! Oooh ho ho, him and I are as close as cousins can be without it getting weird. That's why I want to make his Beesmas extra magical. Why not check in with my dear cousin? I think he could use your help. I can't finish your [Cub Buddy] (or [Star Treat]) until I know his Beesmas has been made. _-During-_ The Mondo Chick is not seasonally appropriate for Beesmas. There's a whole other Honeyday for chicks and eggs and bunnies and other lame things. Beesmas is about gifts and trees and lights and bees! So go get rid of that Mondo Chick. Maybe have Polar Bear cook it up! Ho ho! _-Completion-_ Wonderful! My cousin's Beesmas has been saved! And that means my Beesmas has been saved too! ... Hmm... no, no somethings still not right. It doesn't feel festive enough just yet. Go deliver this [Present] and return for more quests! Your [Cub Buddy] / [Star Treat] is waiting for you after 3 more.   
Upgrading The Honeydays  |  Festive Bee and I have been brainstorming. We've come up with a plan to put that Robo Bear to some Beesmas use. We might be able to use its Upgrades to enhance our magical gift-making powers! Ho ho ho! Maybe. Why don't you go test it out for us? And while you're at it, we've written down a few other ideas. _-During-_ Mutating bees is truly magical! Stuff them up with a [Neonberry] and they start to glow! Feed them [Bitterberries] after that, and, what do you know! Their DNA gets all festively funky! Ho ho! _-Completion-_ 🎵 We wish to extend our Beesmas 🎵 🎵 Could upgrades prolong our Beesmas 🎵 🎵 Mutate to draw out our Beesmas 🎵 🎵 Through the rest of the year! 🎵 Ho ho! It might just work! We'll find out in March. Good work little one! Your Bee Bear's #1 helper! Just 2 more quests to go before you get a little [Cub Buddy] helper of your own! (Or [Star Treat])   
Red And Blue Beesmas  |  My Festive Bees are hard at work on your [Cub Buddy] back at my workshop. It's nearly ready. But we've still got some Beesmas cheer to spread first! We'll need to gather some pollen, both red and blue. And of course, we can't forget that Beesmas goo! And while we're at it, some additional funding through my Catalog would be splendid. So go complete this quest with haste! Work as hard as a Festive Bee would! It takes a little bit of magic and a LOT of hard work to make the Honeydays happen. _-During-_ Every year on Beesmas night... Me and my magical bees take flight... We visit every bear and bee, placing gifts beneath their trees... We ask a certain man up in the sky, if they've been good, or a little too sly... The good ones get some splendid toys! But we've only got coal for naughty boys. And girls. _-Completion-_ Jolly! Jolly! Jolly! There's nothing you and your bees can't do! You're making everybodys Beesmas wishes come true! And for that, I've got another [Present] for you. Just 1 more quest, and I've got something even better. Ho ho!   
Cogs For The Cub Buddy  |  Just look at all the wonderful work you've done! This place is all decked out! Overflowing with Honeyday joy! You've delivered so many [Presents], put up so many decorations... I'd say you've single-handedly saved Beesmas!!! And a Beesmas hero deserves the best gift of all. The love and companionship of a [Cub Buddy]! (Or the high monetary value of a [Star Treat]) But we'll need a few more things to bring that [Cub Buddy] to life. A bunch of cogs for all its joints... A couple [Drives] for it's brain... And the soul of a level 9 Snow Bear! So go, ho ho! The work'll be done before you know! _-During-_ What's that? You're wondering something? If Robo Bear has so many [Drives] to sell in it's shop, how did it break in the first place? Why didn't Bee Bear just take some of the [Drives] from the shop? Why did he need the ones essential to Robo Bear's operation?? Ho ho ho. Because I wanted those one, silly. They were shinier, and I could tell they were Robo Bear's favorite. Favorite things are always more magical. _-Completion-_ 🎵 Joy to the bees 🎵 🎵 The work is done 🎵 🎵 Let you, receive, your thinnngs! 🎵 🎵 A [Cub Buddy] for youuu 🎵 🎵 A [Star Treat] is fine tooo 🎵 🎵 And a couple of other things 🎵 🎵 Some super super festive things 🎵 🎵 A couple of extra useful things 🎵 Ho ho ho ho ho ho! By golly you've done it! You and your bees! You've made this Beesmas the most merry one yet! And for that, you've earned this: Your [Cub Buddy] / [Star Treat] if you already have one! Either one will fill a void in your heart you didn't even know you had. Cherish that gift! And let it bring you Beesmas joy for the rest of the year! Show it off to all your friends! Soak in the festive atmosphere you've worked so hard to set up! And then return to me in a few weeks or so. Because I've got something in store that's even better than a regular ol' [Cub Buddy] This year's limited [Cub Buddy Skin]. Don't think I haven't forgotten! But you'll just have to appreciate the regular one for now.   
Conclusion (before Part 2 update)  | Don't cry little ones, Bee Bear's not leaving. I just got here! We've still got many, many [Presents] to make and deliver! See you soon! Ho ho!   
Peppermint Preparations (1/5)  | Your Cub Buddy sure is sweet, ain't it? But it could be sweeter... Sweet as candy, like Peppermint Robo Cub Buddy! Ho ho! This adorable automaton is the newest Cub Buddy model in town. It's made of candy-coated cogs, and charged with 100 jollywats of Beesmas cheer! Jealous? You should be. But you don't have to be for long... Because if you complete these 5 exceptionally challenging quests I'll construct a [Peppermint Robo Cub Buddy Skin] just for you! Ho ho! Have you been working on your Robo Party Cake? I sure hope so! We'll need to collect a whole plethora of parts And we'll be repurposing some Party Robot components. Help others defeat the Party Robots summoned by their Robo Party Cakes. If you just wait around for your Robo Party to be off cooldown, this'll take forever. And we don't have forever! Beesmas is approaching its untimely end. So go, make haste! Don't let this super sweet opportunity go to waste! -_Completion-_ Ho hoo! Great work! You bashed those Party Mechsquitos to smithereens. And while you did, Festive Bee and I were running around collecting their parts. Now we're getting ready to dip them into molten hot melted peppermints! It's quite messy, and dangerous, but that's the only way we'll be able to make your Peppermint Cub. We wouldn't do this for just anybody! Heck no. But you spread so much Beesmas joy this year that it's the least we can do to give back. Complete 4 more quests, and the [Peppermint Cub Buddy Skin] will be complete!   
Peppermint Preparations (2/5)  | Ho ho ho ho! Lo and behold: Bee Swarm Simulator is 5 years old! And although Robo Bear just got here, it's the only one that seems to be celebrating. That's because it's Beesmas, and Beesmas is more important. The veteran bears know that. There's no room for anniversary celebrations when we're still busy with our festivities. That's why I'm appropriating the Robo Party Cake into Beesmas! To make it acceptible _[sic]_. For this next quest, you'll need to collect 100 stacks of Robo Party Blessing. Every time you defeat a Party Robot, you have a chance of obtaining a stack. The higher the robots _[sic]_ level, the higher the chance. And when a Robo Party is completed, everyone in the server receives some stacks! (The person who started the party receives more than the others) The higher the party's rank, the more stacks you'll receive! So be sure to help as many others as possible with their Robo Party Cakes. Think of it as a gift you're giving to others. Except it's really for yourself. -During- Every time you defeat a Party Robot... You've got a chance to obtain a stack of Robo Party Blessing! And every time anyone in your server completes a Robo Party, you all get stacks! Ho ho! And thank goodness for that! This quest would take forever if you can only get Robot Party Blessing from your own cake. Robo Party Blessing is splendid! At 100 stacks (the max stacks), it grants... +100,000 Capacity, +50% Critical Power, +50% Red, White, Blue Pollen! Why not +50% pollen? Why have all 3 colors listed? That's a Bee Bear's secret! ... Actually, it's not a secret. It has to do with how additive buffs work in this game. +50% would be way more powerful than +50% of each color separately. Maths. Soooo boooring. -_Completion-_ 🎵It's the most wonderful time of the yeearr 🎵 🎵Robo Parties we're hosting🎵 🎵Get Party Bots roasting🎵 🎵And buff us with cheeeeer! 🎵 🎵It's the most, wonderful time... 🎵 🎵OF THE YEEEAARRRRRRRRR!! ~ 🎵 Ho hoo! That was a lot of work. And Peppermint Cub Buddy seems very impressed! Which is impressive itself, since it's a robot without any feelings. What's that? Pepper _[sic]_ Cub Buddy is asking to go with you! Sorry Peppermint Cub Buddy. You're mine forever. I need you here to persuade beekeepers into doing quests. But soon, we'll finish up work on a second Peppermint Cub Buddy. And that one WILL get to go have fun with the beekeeper! Just 3 more quests to go! Ho!   
Peppermint Preparations (3/5)  | Every year, when the snow begins to melt. My festive bees begin to fade...lose their lust for life...During Beemas they're chipper and magical. And EXTREMELY Productive. But during the rest of the year, they barely want to leave their hive slots! I have used shock collars under their [Festive Wreaths] to keep them Motivated. And oooh ho ho how it hurts me so, to resort to such nasty measures. But this year, thanks to your hard work, we've manage to extend Beemas longer than ever ! My Festive Bees are ecstatic knowing they won't have to endure any shocks until May! Let's keep up the jubilation so they keep constructing your [Cub Buddy Skin]. It's almost done! Sort of!   
_-During-_ Peppermint is extremely exotic and rare to make! It's distilled from the essence of a bunch of rare stuff. Like Cogmower gears , and Festive Nymph tears...Things that can't be gotten through magic, unfortunately . You'll have to earn that peppermint the hard way. Sorrrrry! -_Completion-_ Hoo ho ho ho ho! Now that was NICE! Watching you do all that stuff really got my Festive Bees going! They've gotten back to work big time. I'd say your [Peppermint Robo Bear Cub Skin] went from like, 10% complete, to basically 95%! That's a jollily gigantic amount of progress! But unfortunately , the last 5% to go is the hardest 5% of all. There's probably 80% of work left in that 5%. But at least it's split up between 2 quests. And that's only, like, 2.5% to go per quest. 2.5% is basically nothing! Except when it's 2.5% of 80% , which is , what? 40%? 50? ... I may not be very good at math, but who need math when you're MAGIC! Anyways, just 2 more quests to go!   
Peppermint Preparations (4/5)  | The designs for Peppermint Robo Cub cam to me in a dream... A dream I had after I got Bubble Bee Man to spy on Robo Bear. Who was in the process of designing the regular [Robo Cub Skin] . B.B.M told me all the deets. Then I had a cool dream and the [Peppermint Robo Cub Skin] was born! My version is soo much more festive and fun! And much MUCH easier to earn, if you can believe it. To obtain the regular [Robo Cub Skin] , you need to collect 250,000 [Cogs]! Who has time for that? Ho ho! Barely anyone. And you thought my quest were hard. Well, maybe they still are... I admit I've asked a lot of you and your bees this year. But you haven't missed a beat, and you've spread copious amounts of cheer! The map is sooo beautifully decorated! Everyone is happy! All thanks to you! Why stop now? -_Completion-_ 🎵Here comes Peppermint 🎵 🎵Here comes Peppermint ~ 🎵 🎵Right down Peppermint lane 🎵 🎵Watch it prance and watch it dance🎵 🎵Like a funky little candy caaane! 🎵 🎵[Cogs] are clinking, buttons blinking🎵 🎵All is merry and briiight ~ 🎵 🎵So try your best to finish these quests🎵 🎵Cause Peppermint comes toniiighttt!🎵 Ho hoo! That is, if you finish this next quest tonight. That's right little one! Just one more quest to go! Then you'll get a funky little Peppermint pal to call your own.   
Peppermint Preparations (5/5)  | If it were up to me, Beesmas would last forever! We're doing 4 months this year, and it's STILL not enough... Bees are sooo special, they should be celebrated year round! Alas, it can't be... because I have to get back to my workshop. Gotta prep next year's [Presents], ho ho ho... But before I go, we simply must finish your [Peppermint Robo Cub Buddy]! We're so very close! We've collected tons of gizmos and gadgets from Party Robots And there are only a few more parts left to go! So do your best to complete this last quest, take your time. But don't take too long, because when that timer runs out, I'll be gone! For real this time! Ho hoo! Chop chop! -_Completion-_ 🎵Ro-botic Niight ~ 🎵 🎵The [Drives] and [Cogs] are shiiiiiNing.. 🎵 🎵It is the niight, of your cub skin's birthhh!!!!! 🎵 That's right! It's done!! Progress on your [Peppermint Cub Buddy] is 100% complete! And Beesmas is truly saved! You delieved _[sic]_ those gifts! You purchased those bundles! You demolished those Party Bots! You've done it all!!! And that means, until next year, I've got no more quests for you... I'll be off soon. But don't be sad! Just remember: I'll be with you, always As long as you keep the spirit of Beesmas alive in your heart! So never stop beelieving ~ And if that's not enough, there are still a few more Gift Boxes to open. Maybe check in with a certain man in the sky... But for now, goodbye!! So long!! Farewellllll! Merrrrry Beeesmas!!!!   
Conclusion  | Festive Bee and I can move so fast, that it appears like we're still standing here when we're actually not. We're just popping in every other millisecond to keep things looking festive! Ho ho ho! I'm delivering thousands of gifts as we speak. That's the power of Beesmas! As long as you beelieve.   
  
## Words of Farewell

Multiple announcements were made by Bee Bear before his departure. These announcements are:   
  
---  
Bee Bear: "Ho ho ho, it's FINALLY time to go! Beesmas ends in 1 hour. For real this time, sadly."   
Bee Bear: "I used ALL of my honeyday magical might to extend this Beesmas into May! Now i'm out of mana. Gotta recall to my workshop."   
Bee Bear: "The end is near!! Wrap up those Robo Parties, cause we're taking the cake with us too. Ho hoooooo"   
Bee Bear: "Seasons change, snow melts away, but Beesmas lasts forever. Not literally, but in our hearts. As long as you beelieve... (Also: exit the game before the timer ends)"   
Bee Bear: "The time has come for us to part, but don't cry!! I'll be back before you know it... Soo looong, farewell and Merrrrrrrrrry BEEEEESSSSMMAaaaaaasssssssss"
