![Snow Cub](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 [Snow Cub](/wiki/Cub_Buddy#Skins "Cub Buddy")  
  
### Dialogue

Quest name  | Dialogue   
---|---  
Bringing On Beesmas  |  Ho ho ho ho! Well wouldn't you know! It's me, Bee Bear! The magical gift-giving Honeyday bear. Here to celebrate the most wonderful time of the year... Beesmas! A joyous season of snow, gifts, and most of all: bees. I've brought along my Festive Bee, my Reindeer Puppy Bee... And of course, my Cub Buddies! Ho ho! Say, will you help us jolly up this map? Let's make it merry! If you complete 5 quests, I'll give you a [Star Jelly]... If you complete 10 quests, I'll give you some goodies including a [Festive Planter] and 3 [Super Smoothies]... And if you help me complete 15 quests, I'll give you a [Cub Buddy] of your own! (Or a Star Treat if you've already got one, 1 cub helper per person, ho ho) And that's not all, oh no, ho ho! I have even more gifts for you hidden around the map. If you help the other bears around the map decorate for Beesmas, you can open! What do you say? Are you ready to get festive? Your bees look ready! Let's not make them wait any longer! Collect 1000 Red pollen and 500 pollen from the Mushroom Field and Clover Field. _-During-_  
Without the bees, the world would be a drab and scroogie place. That's why we honor them each Beesmas. They deserve it! Help me celebrate our most loyal of friends, the bees. First: Collect 1000 Red pollen and 500 pollen from the Mushroom Field and Clover Field. _-Completion-_  
Ooohoo! That's what I'm talking about! You and your bees make a fantastic additions to my team of Honeyday helpers. We've got much more to do, but here's some rewards... Including 3 [Paper Planters]! Set a Planter in a flower field, and it will automatically grow over time (while you're in game). Your bees will sip Nectar from it, which grants you special boosts! Every time they sip from the Planter, it speeds up the growth. Once the Planter is fully grown, it's ready to harvest! When harvested, Planters grant you Pollen, Nectar, and random rewards! The rewards change based on the field you plant it in. How exciting! Oh ho ho, please do try one out! Then talk to me again when you're ready for your next quest. Just 4 more for a [Star Jelly], and 14 more for a [Cub Buddy] (Or [Star Treat], if you already have a _[sic]_ one)!   
Let It Snow  |  Ho ho ho! Just check out all that snow! Falling [Snowflakes] are one of the purest simple joys of Beesmas! Pristing _[sic]_ fields of snow hide all the nasty things nobody wants to see anyway. And if you use a [Snowflake], it grants a short but special boost to all players in the server! Spreading free gifts to everyone? Sounds like something ol Bee Bear would do, ho ho! Don't you want to be like Bee Bear? Then catch that snow! And don't forget to try your [Paper Planters]. Place one in a field, and let it grow. Stand nearby and your bees will collect some Nectar from it, which boosts your Honeymaking for a little while. Why don't you give it a try? _-During-_  
Place a [Paper Planter] in a flower field, and soon enough you'll get rewards! Standing near a Planter will let your bees gather Nectar from it. While you wait for that, why not catch some [Snowflakes]? They're falling all around on the fields. These simple tasks unlock worlds of joy! Go on now, try them. Then report back for more Beesmas business! _-Completion-_  
🎵Oh the weather outside is frightful...🎵 🎵Our Planters don't seem to mind though🎵 🎵So even though it's so cold🎵 🎵Let em grow, let em grow, let em grow!🎵 Great work little one! You and your bees make collecting [Snowflakes] look like a breeze! Here are some jolly rewards, including a [Pink Balloon]! Collecting pollen under a [Pink Balloon] fills it up. After a while, it floats back to your hive! Converting the pollen from the [Pink Balloon] at your hive grants you special boosts! How bubbly, how buoyant, how brilliantly Beesmas is that? And I have something even better foy you: A [Present]! Exchange this with one of the other questgiving characters around the map. They'll give you a special [Ornament] that you can place on the Beesmas tree! Every [Ornament] on the Beesmas Tree boosts your honeymaking powers throughout all of Beesmas! Go on, pick your favorite bear and give them that [Present], then report back. Complete 3 more quests and I'll give you a [Star Jelly]. And complete 13 more quests for you [Cub Buddy] (or [Star Treat] if you already have one)!   
Happy Honeydays  |  At my workshop, I've got a whole hive of merry little Festive Bees! They work all year long to make this Honeyday magic a reality. Most of them stay home while I travel the world, but the one on my right insist _[sic]_ on joining me. She loves coming to the mountain of Bee Swarm Simulator because she ADORES honey! Ho ho, can you believe it? A bee that loves honey?? You know who else loves honey? Black Bear! Why not help Black Bear decorate for Beesmas. He's been a good bear all year! And while your _[sic]_ at it, why not browse a bit within... ⭐Bee Bear's So Wonderful Great Deals of Winter Catalogue!!! ⭐ That's right: Bee Bear has some special offers for you. Click on the [Gingerbread Bear] icon on the right of your screen to open my exclusive, limited time shop! Spend your [Snowflakes] and [Gingerbread Bears] for special rewards. All proceeds go to me, Bee Bear, one of the most important and magical mascots of Beesmas! Your generous funding is what makes all this possible! Ho ho ho! _-During-_  
Helping the quest-giving characters prepare for Beesmas unlocks special decorations around the map. Each one provides you a useful power, so they're definitely worth it! Delivering [Presents] and helping bears decorate - those are the traditions of Beesmas. But neither is as important as the main tradition of buying things from my catalogue. Click on the [Gingerbread Bear] countdown icon on the right of your screen to learn more! _-Completion-_  
🎵Oh Beesmas tree, oh Beesmas tree...🎵 🎵So tall never bending🎵 🎵Oh Beesmas tree, oh Beesmas tree...🎵 🎵You grants us boosts unending!🎵 Ho ho ho! You've adorned the tree with your first [Ornament]! Can you feel its power? Your bees can! They're buzzing mad with power! But that's just the start of it. Imagine how powerful you'll be with a full tree of [Ornaments]? Unlimited power, ho ho! No one will be able to stop your honeymaking! Complete 2 more quests and I'll give you a [Star Jelly]. And complete 12 more quests for you [Cub Buddy] (or [Star Treat] if you already have one)!   
The Wonders Of Unwrapping  |  Cub Buddies love standing on gift boxes because they're very short, and it makes them feel less short. Puppy bee loves gift boxes because they might contain a [Treat] or two! All bees love gift boxes because they contain so many marvelous trinkets for speeding up your honeymaking. I love gift boxes because they incentivize you to help the bears decorate for Beesmas. And YOU love gift boxes, because you just like to unwrap things! Isn't that right, ho ho ho? Busting things open just to see what they've got inside. That's the meaning of Beesmas. So why don't you go unwrap some gifts? And while your _[sic]_ at it, unwrap some bad bugs around the map! When you think about it, beetles and Spiders are also kind of gift boxes. You pop them open and get random rewards! How jolly! -_During_ -  
To open [Gift Boxes], you must first help other characters decorate for Beesmas. Not all gifts are free! Ho ho ho ho, no no. You've got to work for them! -_Completion_ -  
Marvelous! You unwrapped those boxes, beat up those naughty bugs, and got some rewards! What did you get? Was it something festive? Something jolly? Oooh ho ho ho! I bet it was both! It is Beesmas, after all. But theres _[sic]_ far more gifts left to open, and so much more work to be done! Take this [Present] and let someone else unwrap it, then talk to me again. Complete just 1 more quest and I'll give you a [Star Jelly]! And complete 11 more quests for your [Cub Buddy] (or [Star Treat] if you already have one)!   
Snowbear Showdown  |  The magic of Beesmas manifests itself from the bond between beekeepers and their bees. It's a pure and merry magic. Somewhat like love! Somewhat like friendship. But sometimes, it goes wrong. Ho ho ho ho ho In his foolish hubris, by creating a living Snowbear, Panda Bear has unleashed an EVIL Beesmas magic on this mountain! The cold hearted Snowbear now terrorizes the [Spider Field](/wiki/Spider_Field "Spider Field"), Interupting _[sic]_ innocent beekeepers who are just trying to farm. Hurling snowballs at them, stun-locking them in a devilish display of poorly considered game design! Won't you help restore balance to the magic of Beesmas, and bring this bad bear down a notch? Beat down that Snowbear so that we may continue spreading joy and not fear! But it won't be easy! If you're having difficulty, ask others for help. Anyone can help you defeat your Snowbear! Even those cool people with Gummy Masks and Demon Masks. Why not try asking them for help? They'll surely cherish the opportunity to help a random stranger. Gather up some strong looking Beekeepers and defeat a level 5 Snowbear! And while you're at it, collect some Nectar from [Planters](/wiki/Planter "Planter")! Invigorating Nectar gathered from the Clover, Cactus, or Mountain Top Field boosts your bee's attack! Complete these daunting tasks, and I'll give you a [Star Jelly], and more! -_During_ -  
Collecting Nectar is as easy as placing a planter in a flower field, and waiting! Standing near a Planter allows your bees to sip from it, granting a few seconds of Nectar at a time. When you harvest the Planter after its _[sic]_ done growing, you get a whole bunch of Nectar! There are 5 different types of Nectar, which each grant different bonuses. Discovering which fields give which Nectars is a joy! Ho ho ho! But not as joyful as begging others to defeat your Snowbear for you. Nothing is more merry than a free lunch. _-Completion_ -  
🎵Frosty the Snowbear🎵 🎵Is a very naughty soul🎵 🎵With a black top hat and a snowball attack🎵 🎵He is just downright awful🎵 Ho ho ho! Bet you showed him! And now I'll show you something amazing: A [Star Jelly]! AND 2 [Ticket Planters]! [Star Jellies] transform a bee into a random Gifted bee, which grants your entire hive a special boost. And [Ticket Planters] are like [Paper Planters], but a million times merrier! They grant much more Nectar, at least 10 Tickets, and a bunch of other random items! Marvelous! You have done a Bee Bear proud. But there's always room for more pride! When you're ready to keep up the festivities, talk to me again for more quests. Complete 5 more quests and I'll give you 3 [Super Smoothies] and [Festive Planter] _[sic]_! And complete 10 more quests for your [Cub Buddy] (or [Star Treat] if you already have one)!   
Puffshrooms for Presents  |  High up in the sky, watching us all... He knows if we've been naughty or nice. A very special entity. A magical entity. An iconic entity, that's both bee and man. Yes, I'm speaking of Bubble Bee Man. He's the reason Bee Bear became Bee Bear! Bubble Bee Man is the spirit of Beesmas itself! He's everywhere, and watching us now. How did I meet Bubble Bee Man? Why do I praise him now and forever? I met Bubble Bee Man while chasing Puffshroooooms! What are Puffshrooms? Well, they're a nasty fungus that infects our Planters. Sometimes you'll notice, when your Planter is full grown, it'll be covered in smoke. That smoke is actually Puffshroom spores! Ho ho ho ho. When you harvest a Planter covered in spores, it'll spawn a Puffshroom! You have 5 minutes to collect the pollen near the Puffshrooms to pop them and gain rewards. Everytime you pop a Puffshroom, it sends out spores to spawn another one! Just like Bubble Bee Man's gaze, Puffshroom spores go all over the place! Work with other beekeepers to defeat Puffshrooms at higher levels for better rewards. Just like how I worked with Bubble Bee Man to defeat the Festive Puffshroom and gain my magical powers. All planters have a chance of spawning a Puffshroom, but the [Ticket Planter] always spawns one! Remember, you can use up to 3 different planters at a time in 3 different fields. Go hunt down some Puffshrooms and give out some presents, then report back! -_During_ -  
The [Ticket Planter] I gave you will always spawn a Puffshroom once it reaches 100% growth! If you need more Puffshrooms, why not ask around? Maybe others have a Planter ready with a Puffshroom. -_Completion_ -  
Ho ho! Fantastic! You and your bees make pretty good Puffshroom poppers! Nothing brings us together more than trying to stop a highly prolific invasive species of fungus. And bringing everyone together is what Beesmas is all about! Ho ho ho. Here's a [Present] to bring yet another character together, onto your Beesmas Tree that is. Complete 4 more quests and I'll give you 3 [Super Smoothies] and [Festive Planter] _[sic]_ \- ooooh fancy. And complete 9 more quests for your [Cub Buddy] (or [Star Treat] if you already have one)!   
Very Merry Berries  |  Nice bees get sweet treats, like [Strawberries] and [Blueberries]. Naughty bees get yucky, gross [Bitterberries] and [Neonberries]! But those Naughty bees have a chance of gaining a beneficial mutation from those [Bitterberries]. The Nice bees, at best, gain a tiny tiny chance to become Gifted from their [Strawberries]. What does this teach us? That being naughty always wins, and no good deed goes unpunished. But that's not the point, because being nice is a reward in itself!!! So be nice and go collect some berries. Go on, ho ho! ... Wait, Festive Bee says it wants some Refreshing Nectar as well. I think that's a jolly good idea! Festive Bee says you can gather Refreshing Nectar in the Blue Flower, Strawberry, and Coconut Fields. Refreshing Nectar grants bonus Blue bee convert rate, Red [Pollen](/wiki/Pollen "Pollen"), and even extra pollen from your bee's special abilities! Those do most of the work, you know. Go make Bee Bear proud, but most importantly, go make my Festive Bee proud. Beesmas is about pleasing the BEES after all! Not me! Ho ho hoo. -_During_ -  
Nectar is the key to speeding up your honeymaking. Ho ho, yes yes. If you aren't using Nectar, you're wasting time and honey! And Beesmas doesn't last forever! We need to squeeze in as much joy as we can, as efficiently as possible. To do that, make sure you've always got a Planter gathering you _[sic]_ nectar. Dont _[sic]_ forget, you can get Refreshing Nectar in the Blue Flower, Strawberry, and Coconut Fields. -_Completion_ -  
🎵On the fifth day of Beesmas I got from Festive Bee🎵 🎵Fiiiive [Neonberrriiies]🎵 🎵Four [Pink Balloons]🎵 🎵Three [Field Dice]🎵 🎵Twooo [Whirligigs]🎵 🎵And an [Oil] to speed up my Bees🎵 Ho ho ho! And I'm giving all of those to you! Collecting Nectar is easy if you use multiple types of Planters at once. You can use 3 different types of Planters at a time, in 3 different fields! There are [Paper Planters] for sale in Pro Bear's shop in the 10 bee zone for 10,000 Honey and 1 [Sunflower Seed]. There are [Ticket Planters] and [Festive Planters] for sale in my generous catalogue. And there are other planters for sale in Dapper Bear's shop. Although you will need a nice hat and some cool boots to enter. A Propeller Hat and Hiking Boots ought to work, but anything nicer is fine too, he's not that picky! Ho ho! Complete 3 more quests and I'll also give you 3 [Super Smoothies] and [Festive Planter] _[sic]_. And complete 8 more quests for your [Cub Buddy] (or [Star Treat] if you already have one)!   
Twinkle Up the Night  |  Gathering around the Beesmas tree at night with the ones you love most (your bees) That's where the magic of the Honeydays really happens. The lights twinkling against a pitch black sky... The Fireflies glowing and moving very slowly... Trying desperately to get hit by Science Bear's Falling Beesmas Lights on your laggy device... and many more! You can gather Comforting Nectar from [Planters](/wiki/Planter "Planter") in the Dandelion Field, Bamboo Field, and [Pine Tree Forest](/wiki/Pine_Tree_Forest "Pine Tree Forest"). So head out into the night and experience some of that magic! You really must feel it first hand. -_During_ -  
My deals are beyond unreal - they're magical, they're metaphysical. They're beyond reason. How can Bee Bear supply such great offers to SO many beekeepers for just [Snowflakes] and [Gingerbread Bears]? That's a Bee Bear's secret, ho ho ho ho! Don't forget, you can get Comforting Nectar in the Dandelion Field, Bamboo Field, and [Pine Tree Forest](/wiki/Pine_Tree_Forest "Pine Tree Forest"). -_Completion_ -  
Wasn't all that nonsense calmingly beautiful? Ho ho! I think so. So beautiful, that we should celebrate with a [Present]. Each present makes the Beesmas Tree even MORE beautiful. It's getting even more beautiful than Puppy Bee, who is a very beautiful puppy. With a very cute nose that glows just like the Beesmas Lights, thanks to its Beequip (Available exclusively in Bee Bear's Catalogue for the low low price of many [Gingerbread Bears] and [Snowflakes]). To use a Beequip that you find, you'll need a [Beequip Case] from the darling Dapper Bear. You can find him in the pink building in the 10 bee zone. Complete 2 more quests and I'll also give you 3 [Super Smoothies] and [Festive Planter] _[sic]_. And complete 7 more quests for your [Cub Buddy] (or [Star Treat] if you already have one)!   
By Golly, Get Jolly  |  This time of year is full of endless joy! But also endless stress... My Festive Bee knows all about that! Ho ho ho! No breaks for the bees! So many [Presents] to wrap and Beequips to build... Sooo much labor demanded of sweet little bees to make all this magic happen! But they don't let it bring them down. They are pure, unadulterated joy, always! Ho ho! And if they can do it, so can you. Don't let stress bring you down, just be like the bees! If you need some motivation, why not try Motivating Nectar? You can gather Motivating Nectar from [Planters](/wiki/Planter "Planter") in the Mushroom, Spider, Rose, and Mountain Top fields. It grants bonus Blue [Pollen](/wiki/Pollen "Pollen"), Convert Rate, and Bee Ability Rate to help the bees work even more! And if that's not enough, why not open some more Gift Boxes? You go get jolly out there then come back for more work, ho ho! -_During_ -  
Cub Buddies are always jolly, because I didn't add an un-jolly chip to their little brains. That's right. Cub Buddies are physically incapable of sadness, pain, grief, and more! That means they wouldn't miss you if you were gone. Sounds sad but its not! Ho ho! Don't forget, you can get Motivating Nectar in the Mushroom, Spider, Rose, and Mountain Top fields. -_Completion_ -  
🎵Have a Holly, Jolly, Beesmas🎵🎵It's the best time of the yeaaarr🎵🎵When honey flows, and planters grow🎵🎵And Nectar makes us cheer!🎵 Ho ho! That's Cub Buddy's favorite song! I can tell because it wiggles a little when I sing it. I wonder what your Cub Buddy's favorite song will be? And if you already have a Cub Buddy, I wonder what your [Star Treat]'s favorite song will be? Hmmm? Complete 1 more quests _[sic]_ and I'll also give you 3 [Super Smoothies] and [Festive Planter] _[sic]_. And complete 6 more quests for your [Cub Buddy] (or [Star Treat] if you already have one)!   
Quality Cousin Time  |  As you know, I'm Bee Bear. The _[sic]_ magical spirit responsible for spreading gifts to bees and bears all around the world. I've been doing my jolly ol job for hundreds, maybe thousands of years. I don't keep track, ho ho! But I still have a family you know. And I'm not talking about Festive Bee, who is more of a very close acquaintance. I'm talking about Polar Bear! My dear, dear little cousin, and the greatest chef in the whole wide world! My next rewards for you are super special. The [Festive Planter] grants tons of items and Nectar! Each [Festive Planter] takes my Festive Bees over 3 months to prepare. 1 Festive Bee may only live long enough to make a couple of them. So I don't give them out willie nillie! That's why I have a very special quest for you: Make Polar Bear's Beesmas truly magical! He deserves it! And make my Beesmas truly magical, buy _[sic]_ purchasing things in my Catalogue! Maybe even a [Festive Planter]! -_During_ -  
Polar Bear may be my cousin, but he didn't inherit any of my magical genes. Ho ho ho, yes I do admit he's a genie in the kitchen. But I have TRUE magic. I can travel around the world in the blink of an eye and touch my elbow with my nose. -_Completion_ -  
Ho ho ho yes! Polar Bear is practically bursting with Beesmas joy now! You make me as proud as Puppy Bee after a grooming in which he was very brave and didn't even bite nobody! The joy of the Honeydays is really setting in thick now, Like a fantastical fog of pure bee-appreciating bliss! And that's why I want to give you this [Festive Planter] and 3 [Super Smoothies]! [Festive Planters] are like [Ticket Planters] except a billion trillion times more jolly. And a [Super Smoothie] is the ultimate boost granting beverage! Very hard to come by, oh yes. But honestly _[sic]_ probably most important of all, is this [Present]! They just keep on coming! Ho ho ho ho! If you complete just 5 more quests, I'll give you a [Cub Buddy] (or [Star Treat] if you already have one)! But you'll need to come back in a few days, as I'm still ramping up my Honeyday Heaters. For now, why not continue helping the other characters prepare for Beesmas?   
After Completing All Quests -_Before Beesmas Part 1.5_ \-  |  My Festive Bees and I are still putting together the [Cub Buddies] and [Star Treats]! We spent all our time making those [Festive Planters], now we're out of buddies. So sad! But don't cry, little ones, they'll be done soon. Come back in a few days for more quests.   
Warming Up For Winter  |  Ooooh ho ho hooh ho hoh oh ho! I'm cold! Festive Bee stays toasty hot in that skin-tight Festive Wreath. Puppy Bee, well he's too dumb to even realize how cold he is. Poor thing, can't be helped! And Cub Buddy, well, Cub Buddy is a robot, constructed in my wonderful workshop. But I didn't raise enough funding through last year's Catalog to afford cold-detecting sensors... That's right, little ones. The Cub Buddies can't even tell if they're freezing _[sic]_ And it's all because the beekeepers didn't believe in enough Beesmas magic to purchase ALL And I do mean ALL of my generous offers. Oh ho ho hooowhoa _[sic]_ is Bee Bear. You can repay me though, by warming me up through completing this quest. If you do that, I'll forgive you. And you'll get a free pass from Naughty to Nice. On my list, at least. Ho ho! But probably not on Bubble Bee Mans! -_During_ -  
Dapper Bear's Samovar brews up random Nectar using winter powers from Russia _[sic]_ Where Father Frost Bear roams, and I dare not set foot. We have an arrangement, oh yes! Ho ho! He gets to give gifts to the bees of Russia and I get everywhere else. And parts of Russia too. Remember, you can collect Invigorating nectar from the Clover, Cactus and Mountain Top fields. And the hot hot [Pepper Patch](/wiki/Pepper_Patch "Pepper Patch")! If you can get there, that would warm me up fast! Ho ho! -_Completion_ -  
🎵Planters growing near an open fire...🎵🎵Aphids nipping at your toeees....🎵🎵Although it's been said, many times, many waaays🎵🎵Merry Beesmassssss, Guangzhouuu🎵 Ho ho ho. It's hard to rhyme with "toes"! Marvelous! I'm all warmed up now. No more shivering and whining from Bee Bear. Back to the festivities! Just 4 more quests, and I'll give you your Cub Buddy (Or [Star Treat] if you've already got one)!   
Merry Mushroom Mania  |  Pretty pretty puffshroom, on pristine fields of snow! Watch them pop, and watch them grow! Ho ho ho ho ho ho hoo. Your bees sure do like Puffshrooms. (I can read their minds, because I'm magic) They're all thinking the same thing: "Please Bee Bear, more Puffshroom quests" "We want quests where fate is out of our hands, and RNG takes the wheel!" We're tired of grinding and ready for waiting and praying for RARE PUFFSHROOMS!" Well you're in luck little bees! For our next Beesmas festivity, we're hunting for Rare Puffshrooms! Also the boring brown normal ones. But Rare ones too! Ho ho! I wish I could join in. But I've been banned from foraging by the game warden. My magic is an "existential threat to innumerable native species" according to the so called government. If Puffshrooms weren't meant to be picked by a magical half bee half bear, who can fly at over 75% the speed of light, then the great Bubble Bee Man wouldn't have made them so weak and slow! Ho ho! -_During_ -  
Puffshrooms spawn from [Planters](/wiki/Planter "Planter"), but not all the time! It depends on the Planter you're using. Oh yes! Better Planters are more likely to spawn Puffshrooms, and at higher levels! Ho ho how helpful! With a Petal Planter, gone are the days of chasing around level 2s and 3s. -_Completion_ -  
Jovially done little ones! That Puffshroom slaughter was magically morbid! And that enthusiasm with which you purchased my generous deals! Oh ho ho ho! It's enough to bring a Bee Bear pure Beesmas bliss! I knew you would be worthy little helper. But I didn't realize just HOW worthy you would be! You're so smart you even noticed that 1 Puffshroom spawns on a random flower field, twice an hour. Once at 15 minutes passed, and another at 45 minutes passed _[sic]_ the hour. You used your elf-like helper magic to intuit that important fact all on your own! Now that's what I call merry ol-fashioned holly golly jolly folly lolly dolly MAGIC!! Now go use that magic to deliver this [Present]. Just 3 more quests, and I'll give you your Cub Buddy (or [Star Treat] if you've already got one)!   
Blue Beesmas  |  Every Cub Buddy deserves a true-blue Beekeeper to lead them down the path to a life full of honey. That's why I can't just hand one over, oh ho ho oh no. We've got to run you through the ringer! To prove that you're a true jolly jack! What's a jolly jack? You'd know if only you were jolly enough to own a Cub Buddy. There's still time to prove yourself, to me at least. Festive Bee says she's seen enough. Let's move on to another beekeeper. Someone a little faster, a little bit merrier, a little bit fresher. I said now wait a minute, this beekeepers done a decent job. She said: Decent doesn't make 7.9 billion presents in time for Beesmas each year. We need extraordinary beekeepers! Cub Buddies deserve extraordinary beekeepers! And she's right! So you've got just 3 more quests to prove yourself. Get yourself in good favor with Festive or your Cub Buddy (or Star Treat) will have to wait for next year! Ho ho ho ho, pressures on! -_During_ -  
Festive Bee looks so jolly and joyful all the time, right? Kind of hard to believe she's so stern and demanding... You just have to take my word for it, ho ho ho ho! And as you know, I cannot tell a lie! -_Completion_ -  
🎵I'll have a bluuuuuue, Beeesmaaas, without you🎵🎵I'll be so bluuuuue, waiting, for that goo🎵🎵I'll be waiting all night, with no [Gumdrops] in sight🎵🎵Oh yes I'll have a blue goo-less Beesmaaas🎵 Ho ho! Or that's what I would have said, if you had let me down. Even Festive Bee is impressed with all you did out there. But lets not get ahead of ourselves... Just 2 more quests, and I'll give you your Cub Buddy (or [Star Treat] if you've already got one)!   
Beesmas Bouquet  |  During the Honeydays, we take time out of our hectic schedules to appreciate the little things. To savor each moment with the ones we love, like Puppy Bee, and Festive Bee. We stop and smell the roses, and the sunflowers, and the [Honeysuckles]. And that's what makes the Honeydays truly magical. They remind us about what life's all about! Flowers and pollen, and the honey can _[sic]_ make from it! Let's sloooow down and put together a little Beesmas Bouquet. Your bees would truly love it. And Beesmas is all about keeping them happy. Before you know it, you'll have a Cub Buddy, or [Star Treat], and you won't have time for your bees anymore... So just appreciate them in the meantime, and show them they're special! -_During_ -  
There's a bit of Beesmas magic hidden in every field... Only Bee Bear can see it... But so can your Planters! Make sure to move your Planters around to different fields. You might find hidden rewards! -_Completion_ -  
Oh ho ho! Now your bees know! They know you love them and aren't just waiting to move onto something better, like a Cub Buddy. Well that's why Bee Bear's here! I live to spread love and cheer! And soon you'll be the most cheerful one of all! Just 1 more quest, and I'll give you your Cub Buddy (or [Star Treat] if you've already got one)!   
Cub Buddy Beckoning  |  🎵It's beginning to look a lot like Beeeeesmaaaaas🎵🎵Eveeeerryyywhere we goooooo🎵🎵Take a look at the quests you've done🎵🎵All that decorating fun🎵🎵Has maaade itttt soooo🎵Yes! You've done it, you and your jolly little bees! Bee Swarm Simulator, decked out in convivial crafts, holiday-themed just in time for January!!! Who doesn't love seeing Beesmas stuff well passed _[sic]_ the new year, far into February! It's jolly. It's chippy. And by golly it's timely! But we're not done yet! You've still got to get you _[sic]_ Cub Buddy, or [Star Treat]! Both will add immensely to the festive vibe we've already got flowing so hard! Ho ho ho ho! Complete this final, maybe-just-a-little-bit-more-difficult-than-the-others quest and I'll happily wrap it up and send it off with you and your swarm! Yes yes! Goooo! True Beesmas joy awaits you at the end of this technically navigable tunnel! -_During_ -  
Didn't nectar seem so scary at first? Ho ho ho. Not so scary when you've got 3 Planter _[sic]_ growing at once, and you start to understand the multipliers. Yes yes. Everything easy _[sic]_ when you have unlimited time to just grind away on ROBLOX without a care in the world, as free as a dang Windy Bee! Ho ho ho ho hooooh ho. -_Completion_ -  
Look Festive! Look Puppy! Look little black Cubby! The beekeeper, they've done it! By golly jolly they've done it! 🎵Ding dong ding dong🎵🎵.. Ding 🔔 dong ding dong 🔔🎵🎵Joooy to the woooorld🎵🎵This questlines done🎵🎵It's tiiime, for Cuuub🎵🎵Buuuudddyyyyyyyy🎵 Oh yes it is indeed! You and your bees have been the greatest Beesmas helper a Bee Bear could ever ask for! And I'm saying that right in front of Festive Bee, with a straight face. You've certainly earned this: A Cub Buddy! Or a [Star Treat]!!! Not certain exactly! Either one is truly magical, and will make this Honeyday even sweeter. Ho ho ho hoooo ho! Yeeeeeehhhaaaaaawwwwwwwwwww! Not sure where that came from! Ho ho! Cub Buddy loves nothing more than to pick up tokens and give you gifts. It will pay for itself in due time, all this questing labor. It'll be nothing compared to the hours Cub Buddy puts in for you! So go now and be jolly together as a new family! But be sure to come back, in a week or so, depending on Onett's stamina... That's right little ones. Bee Bear's not done yet! There's a newer, cooler, cuter Cub Buddy in town, just around the bend. Sorry, Black Cub Buddy. This happens every year. An exclusive Cub Buddy skin, available during this Winter Event only that Onett has totally already made, and hasn't just hastilly _[sic]_ sketched in a spiral notebook! So come back soon and find out what it is, and maybe, just maybe you'll earn that one too. Ho ho ho ho ho! Merry merry Beeeesssmaaaaas!!!   
Snow Cub Reformation (1/5)  |  Ho ho ho ho! Aren't Cub Buddies just the jolliest? They're pure hearted creatures brought to life by 100% pure Beesmas magic, after all! Ho ho, oh yes! Or so you'd think... Not all cubbies are nice. There is one who is naughty... VERY naughty... This one right here: the Snow Cub Buddy! It refuses to help you beekeepers, says you should all eat snow. Mean little thing keeps pushing Black Cub Buddy off that present box. SO RUDE. It even threw Puppy Bee's tennis ball into the 35 bee zone... I only have 2 bees, we can't get in there. The balls _[sic]_ as good as lost. Why is Snow Cub so cruel? There's only one way to get a naughty cub in check: Strike FEAR into it's _[sic]_ cold heart through displays of pure force and domination! Ho ho! Yes, by completing these 5 ridiculously daunting quests, and beating down some Snowbears I believe we can teach Snow Cub Buddy the joy of Honeyday kindness yet. Complete this quest to intimidate Snow Cub buddy _[sic]_ through a show of pure beekeeping force Let's teach this delinquent what Beesmas is all about! _-During-_  
Snow Cub Buddy was carved by chance from a Snowbear's snowball. That very same snowball was responsible for the death of 6 BEEKEEPERS! The tears from their bees accidentally formed the ball into a wretched little cub. And then I accidentally dropped a magical hat on it when I was flying over the mountain! Oooh ho ho it was a _[sic]_ incredibly unlikely series of unfortunate events And now we have to deal with this mean little punk! We need to reform Snow Cub and save Beesmas once and for all! -_Completion-_  
Ho hooo! That was some marvelous work all around. Your Cub Buddy was sooo brave out there, standing up to Werewolves and Windy Bee! Do you see that, Snow Cub? See how their Cub Buddy was a brave little helper? See how they got so much loot and honey and had an all around jolly time? See how wonderfully fun the world can be when you stop being a bratty bully?? Of course you don't you naughty lump of snow. Never should have given you that magic hat, ho no no! Well I'm still convinced that after 4 more quests, this Snow Cub will wise up!   
Snow Cub Reformation (2/5)  | Snow Cub HATES planters. How could anyone hate something so full of jolly gifts? Snow Cub throws snowballs at them and knocks them over. I had a Festive Planter at full growth, Puffshroom spores and everything, ready to open. Snow Cub smashed it with snowballs then called me a baby for crying about it. Well it hurt! I don't get Festive Planters for free! I have to buy them from my own catalogue, with my own hard earned [Gingerbread Bears]. It took me ages, and then I had to wait for it to grow and everything. All for nothing! Oh no... oh no no no no, I'll never let it go. That's why we need to ramp up our Beesmas cheer spreading to unprecedented levels! We need to complete quests so very jolly that even the scroogiests cubs will come around. We'll show Snow Cub that life is sooOO much better when you let joy into your heart and stop being a jerk.   
-_During-_  
Close your ears little bees, for I am about to say a truly horrible fact: Snow Cub Buddy's favorite treats are... [Basic Eggs]! Oh it's just terrible to think about! Who eats bee eggs? There are even legends of a super evil GIFTED Snow Cub who ate over 25,000 of them. Now that's ho ho horrifying! We'll need a naughtier-than-naughty list for that monster. -_Completion-_  
🎵Deck Snowbear with rounds and volleys🎵🎵Fa-la-la-la-laaa, la la, la laaaa🎵🎵Make it pay for being naughty🎵🎵Fa-la-la-la-laaa, la la, la laaa🎵🎵Striking fear into Snow Cubby...🎵🎵Fa-la-la, la-la-la, laa laa laaaaa🎵🎵Maybe then he'll be your buddy!🎵🎵Fa-la-la-la-laaaa, la la, la laaaaaaaaa!!🎵We'll just see about that... ho ho ho ho ho! Just 3 more excruciating quests to go!   
Snow Cub Reformation (3/5)  | Honeyday joy should NEVER stop, no matter what people say... "It's March, take down that Beesmas tree, stop singing carols and get back to work" Just tell them firmly: NO! This magic will not go! Life without Beesmas is barely worth living! When the snow melts, so does my will to get up each morning. The only thing that keeps me going are my precious Festive Bees And even they barely hang on in those dark summer days... That's why this year we're just not leaving. We're extending that countdown, pretending like it was never going to end at all. What deadline? What do you mean Beesmas is a temporary, seasonal event? I think you've gone a little loony, ho ho! Now you've got plenty of time to complete this mercilessly merry quest!   
-_During-_  
I've seen Snow Cub Buddy terrorize beekeepers in the middle of huge boosts! Yes, when they've spent all their saved up resources, used a [Super Smoothie], And even donated a Mythic Egg to the Wind Shrine. Then BAM Snow Cub Buddy out of nowhere starts bombarding them with snowballs. WHYYYY Snow Cub WHY? Think of all the honey that could have been made! You're the scroogiest of scrooges! _-Completion-_  
Ho ho ho ho!! Splendid work little one! With your help, Beesmas joy will finally last year round! And eventually this nasty little Snow Cub will have to join in on the festivities Or be ostracized by literally everyone else! Do you hear that, Snow Cub? We've got no room for grinches in this new land of eternal jolliness! If you don't change your attitude after these remaining 2 quests, we're sending you outside the map. Ho hoo, you won't have anyone to terrorize out there! Except yourself.   
Snow Cub Reformation (4/5)  | Magical moments are hard earned! Yes indeed. Honeyday magic is fueled by the arduous yet always-enthusiastic labor provided by you and your bees. Every planter you harvest, every Puffshroom you pop, every Mythic Meteor you catch... Provides a little more gusto, a little more pep, a little more JOY to everyone around you! So every time you think to yourself, "I can't finish this quest. Has Onett lost his mind?" "Does he know how ridiculously long these take to complete? Does he even care?" Just remember that when it's all over, that special Beesmas magic makes it all worth it. Or at least I think so. Ho hoo ho ho hooo ho ho ho hoooooooo ho! Hopefully you know too because otherwise I don't think you'll complete this next quest!   
-_During-_  
Snow Cub Buddy uses its snowballs to steal loot from Sprouts. When it finds a Vicious Bee's spike, it 1 shots it with a snow ball before anyone else can get a hit. There is no end to the downright cruel tomfoolery Snow Cub Buddy gets into. Oh ho ho hoo. Why can't you just be nice like the other cubs??? _-Completion-_ 🎄 Up on the mountain, bears with paws 🎄🎄 Give beekeepers quest because 🎄🎄 Making honey's what they enjoy 🎄🎄 Enjoy of it for every girl and boy! 🎄🎄 Ho ho hoo! These quests are slow! 🎄🎄 Ho ho hoooo, they're worth it thooUgh 🎄🎄 Up on the mountain, click click click 🎄🎄 Make enough honey to get us sick! 🎄 Yaaaaaay! How merrily indulgent! That was my rendition of "Up On The HouseTop". I hope you could tell! I'm running out of songs. Good thing we're almost done, ho ho! You have certainly made a Bee Bear proud. Everyone is so thankful for all the Beesmas joy you've spread throurgout this mountain. Puppy Bee gets so excited now, whenever you and your bees come around. Black Cub Buddy says it hopes one day it'll be adopted by a beekeeper even half as good as you! Even Festive Bee says you've exceeded her wildest expectations, which are already insanely high! Yes, EVERYBODY is SOOO thankful, and we ALL agree you're simply wonderful! Everyone, except... _[sic]_ Snow Cub Buddy, boooo! But I think it's starting to come around... Yesterday I gave a Blue Backpacker a quest, and Snow Cub Buddy didn't even throw a snowball at them. Snow Cub Buddy HATES Blue Backpackers, whats changed? Could it be that Snow Cub Buddy is beginning to beelieve in the power of Beesmas? If you complete one final quest, one totally completable quest, _[sic]_ I think Snow Cub Buddy may change its ways once and for all!   
Snow Cub Reformation (5/5)  |  Ho ho! This is it, everybody. The moment we've all been waiting for! The big one, the jolliest job of them all! The quest SO BIG that it should finally transform Snow Cub Buddy into a nice cub!! It's got a little bit of everything festive wrapped up in it. It'll take a TON of perseverance, and just a hint of Beesmas magic, to complete it _[sic]_ But I know you can do it! You're Bee Bear's greatest helper yet! So go out there and spread some joy, collect a simply unreasonable amount of stuff _[sic]_ Purchase a few more quality goods from my generous catalogue _[sic]_ Wait a ridiculously long time for a whole variety of Planters to grow _[sic]_ Beat down the biggest Snowbear yet, use those [Snowflakes] _[sic]_ And save Beesmas once and for all!!! From Snow Cub's terrible attitude!   
-_During-_  
There's still hope for you, Snow Cub Buddy. And for every lost scoundrel soul out there in this magical world. Let the light and love and joy of the BEES into your heart, little cub _[sic]_ And check yourself before you wreck yourself! Watch as this wonderful beekeeper completes this final ridiculous quest _[sic]_ Be in awe of their extreme determination in the face of incredible monotony and unending time-sinks _[sic]_ And change your ways before it's too late! OR BEESMAS IS CANCELED FOREVER! _-Completion-_  
Oooh HO HO! Splendid, simply splendid!! That was a show of beekeeping so talented and determined I could just cry! You've moved us with all that pollen and token collecting. All of us, including Snow Cub Buddy!!! That's right, through a mixture of inducing fear through defeating Snowbears _[sic]_ And inspiring awe through the bond between you and your bees... Snow Cub Buddy finally believes in the magic of Beesmas and wants to help you spread it even more! Ignore the suspiciously unnearving smile on it's face, that's just how it looks. With Snow Cub Buddy by your side, Beesmas will literally follow you around wherever you go _[sic]_ And everyone will see your Snow Cub and think, "Wow, that person plays a lot of Bee Swarm Simulator" Nice! And with that, I suppose my time here is coming to a close. I'll just hang here until the timer runs out, _[sic]_ Then I'm off to spread honeyday cheer and great catalogue deals to a different mountain. I could never thank you enough for all the festive joy you've helped me spread this year! So thank you, thank you, a thousand million times thank you!! And keep the magic of Beesmas alive in your heart year-round. Before you know it I'll be back! Keep Snow Cub Buddy in check while I'm away. Don't let it sense your weakness - remember, YOU'RE in charge. Now so long to you and your wonderful bees! 🎄 Farewell, and Merrrrrry MEEERRRYY BEEEEEESMAS!!!!!! 🎄   
Conclusion (prior to the 2022-02-28 update)  | Don't cry little ones, Bee Bear's not leaving just yet. We've still got this year's exclusive Cub Buddy skin to reveal and give out through a quest line so absurdly jolly and merilly _[sic]_ mindless you'll want to pop your own Puffshrooms out! Ho ho ho hooo! See you soon!   
Conclusion  | My Festive Bee and I can move so fast, that it appears like we're still standing here when we're actually not. We're just popping in every other millisecond to keep things looking festive! Ho ho ho! I'm delivering thousands of gifts as we speak. That's the power of Beesmas! As long as you beelieve.   
  
## Words of Farewell

Multiple announcements were made by Bee Bear before his departure. These announcements are:   
  
---  
Bee Bear: Oh ho ho, say it ain't soOoooo. Nearly time for Festive, Puppy, the Cubs and I to pack our things and go...   
Bee Bear: If you keep the magic of Beesmas alive while I'm away, it'll be like I never left! Ho ho ho ho ho!   
Bee Bear: Ok little ones, get ready for the server reboot! Take good care of your bees and Cub Buddies while I'm off doing whatever I do for the other 9 months of the year!   
Bee Bear: I'll pack up all this festive stuff and be gone in the blink of an eye! Just wait until the servers reboot - you'll see how fast I truly am.   
Bee Bear: Haaaappy Honeydaays and M E R R Y B E E S M A S to all!!!!!!!!!! Farewelllll!

### Dialogue

Quest name  | Dialogue   
---|---  
Beesmas Beequips  | Ho ho ho! Yes, it's time! It's Beesmas! The most magical honeyday of the year! It's a time for appreciating our bees, and everything they do for us! A time for giving gifts, and receiving gifts too. This year, I've got the perfect gift for you and your bees! The [Beequip Case]! Oh ho ho ho! What's that, you ask? Well.. As a beekeeper, you wear all sorts of equipment. Hats, bags, and more. Why shouldn't your BEES do the same? Well, this year they CAN! Just look at Puppy Bee, with that red nose! So festively fashionable! With the [Beequip Case], you can give BEEs EQUIPment to hold - or as I like to call it, Beequipment! Ho ho! And if you help me prepare for Beesmas, I've got a special [Beequip Case] just for you. There's so much to do, and Beesmas is approaching fast! So let's not delay: Collect 1000 Red Pollen, 1000 White Pollen... And collect 5 [Snowflake] tokens! It's winter, so [Snowflakes] are falling all over the flower fields! _-During-_ Hurry, hurry! Your bees have been so good this year, they deserve Beequips. Complete my quest, and it'll be time to dress them up! _-Completion-_ Ho ho! You've done it! Quickly, Festive Bee - fetch that [Beequip Case]. The beekeeper has earned it! With this [Beequip Case], you'll be able to equip certain items to your bees. Beequips can have many effects: they change a bee's stats, grant bonuses to your entire hive... And even teach your bees new abilities! To get you started, I'll give you an [Elf Cap] to test out. Currently, your [Beequip Case] can only hold 3 Beequips at a time. That means you can only equip 3 Beequips to your bees at once! But if you find extra Beequips that can't fit in your case, just check the Beequip Storage to your right. Now, let's make haste! There's more to do and so little time! And so, so many special rewards for you to earn! Ho ho ho ho!   
Bee Bear's Catalog  | Beesmas is about the joy bees bring to our lives! It's also about the healthy stimulation of cash into our economy, generated by people purchasing gifts! That's why this year, I've brought along something special: Bee Bear's Catalog! In here, you can purchase a variety of useful items. But not with Honey! Oh ho ho oh no, I only accept the most merry of currencies: [Gingerbread Bears] and [Snowflakes]! See, Gingerbread Cub here isn't just standing around to be cute! His job is to entice you into purchasing the Gingerbread Cub Pack from my catalog! I pay him by the hour, ho ho! By completing my quests, and by helping other Bears around the map prepare for Beesmas... You can earn yourself enough [Gingerbread Bears] to purchase a Gingerbread Cub Buddy of your own! First, why not help Black Bear with his Honey Wreath? Oh ho... poor Black Bear just can't seem to get it right. If you do, I've got some special rewards for you... Now, collect 5000 White pollen, 5000 pollen from the Clover Field. Admire Black Bear's Honey Wreath 2 times. Equip 1 Beequip to your bee - the [Elf Cap] I gave you should do! And purchase 1 offer in my Catalog! Just click the little [Gingerbread Bear] icon on the right of your screen. _-During-_ Black Bear has been such a good, good bear this year. But being well behaved doesn't mean you've got talent, ho ho ho! _-Completion-_ 🎵 Deck the Bees with hats and trinkets 🎵 🎵 Fa la la la laaa, la laa laa laa 🎵 🎵 Collect snow to purchase Beequips 🎵 🎵 Fa la la la laaa, la laa laa laa 🎵 Good work, little one! Now you're getting into the groove of Beesmas! Whats [sic] that? How does Bee Bear keep his catalog prices so low, you ask? Ho ho! With Beesmas Magic of course! And the loving labor of my hive of Festive Bees. All work and no pay, ho ho! If you complete just 13 more of my quests, I'll give you a [Festive Bee] of your own! (Or 500 tickets if you already have one, ho ho) But that's for later: For now, here's your reward: A [Present]! Ho ho, but it's not for you - not really! Deliver this [Present] to a bear of your choice to make their Beesmas truly magical. If you do, I'm sure they'll have something in return to give to you. Now go, go! Deliver the [Present], then return to Bee Bear for more winter wonder work!   
Beesmas Unboxing Begins  | Delivering [Presents] is all fine and good. But opening presents is even better! And if you help enough bears decorate the map for Beesmas... You'll be able to open Gift Boxes I've hidden just for you all around the map! Ho ho ho. But you better not open them until you help those bears! Bee Bear is always watching, and so is you know who... You wouldn't want to end up on his naughty list! Ho ho! Collect 2000 Blue Pollen... Collect 10 [Snowflake] tokens... Defeat 3 Spiders... Deliver 1 [Present] to a character of your choice... And open 1 Gift Box - AFTER you help 3 Bears prepare for Beesmas. _-During-_ Oh, we're so, so far behind... The bears will all need your help if we're going to save Beesmas this year. Quickly, go talk to each one and see what they need. _-Completion-_ Oh, how magical! You've brought joy to so many bears already - and you've gotten some joy in return! But you know what would bring you even more joy? A bigger [Beequip Case]! Complete just 2 more of my quests, and I'll double the size from 3 slots to 6! And complete 12 more of my quests, and you'll earn that Festive Bee / market equivalent ticket reward!   
Search In The Stockings  | Little cubs and bees all around the world wait patiently all year for Beesmas... But especially for Beesmas Stockings! Stuffed to the brim with the jolliest of junk! So many trinkets, so many toys - so many [Beequips] can be found in Stockings! Don't believe me? Ho ho! Just ask Brown Bear. Collect 50,000 White pollen... Collect 30,000 pollen from the Bamboo Field... Collect 30,000 pollen from the Strawberry Field... Collect 8 tokens from Brown Bear's stockings... And open 2 Gift Boxes around the map! _-During-_ Gingerbread Cub was born in a Stocking! Ho ho ho. That's right! Stuffing stockings with cookies, that's how Cub Buddies are made! _-Completion-_ 🎵 Puppy the red-nosed Event Bee 🎵 🎵 Had a very shiny nose 🎵 🎵 He loves collecting pollen 🎵 🎵 With his tennis ball that glows! 🎵 Ho ho! Puppy Bee loves it when I sing. Marvelous work, little one! Keep the ball rolling! Complete 1 more quest, and I'll double the size of your [Beequip Case]! And complete 11 more quests and you'll earn your Festive Bee!   
Subdue the Snowbear  | To be a good Bee Bear helper, your bees will need to be disciplined. Oh yes. Just look at my prescious [sic] Festive Bee. Obeys my every command! And is willing to get down and dirty when things get rough! Beesmas battles are a perfect way to keep your bees disciplined and in shape! Thankfully Panda Bear's here to help us with that... For the next quest, you'll need to take down a Level 5 Snowbear! Those naughty bears need someone to keep them in check. Ho ho ho ho ho. _-During-_ Mother Bear's Gingerbread House is magic, just like me! It generates [Gingerbread Bears] out of thin air! All it costs is the electricity needed to run your device! Hopefully you don't pay the bills. Ho ho ho ho! _-Completion-_ Ho ho! You took down the Snowbear, I bet you're feeling quite jolly! But don't get too jolly, or that'll be your folly. Each time you defeat the Snowbear, it comes back bigger and badder. But that's a problem for another day. For now, your reward: 3 [Beequip Case Slots]! And a Warm Scarf to test them out. Now you can equip Beequips to up to 8 bees at a time! Magnificent! But we aren't done yet, not even close! Beesmas day approaches fast and we still have so much preparing to do!   
Snowflake Feast  | Giving gifts is one of the selfless ways you can prove you're a good person! Yes, every time you do a good deed, it doesn't go unseen! The great Bubble Bee Man in the sky is always watching, especially around Beesmas! Everything good you do gives you +1 point on his Good Beekeeper list. But everything bad you do... oh ho ho ho... thats +60 points on the Naughty list. So, whats your score? Have you been a naughty or nice? If you're worried, I have an offer for you: Complete this quest, and I'll put in a good word with Bubble Bee Man for you directly! _-During-_ Bubble Bee Man is the one who originally blessed us with this most fabulous of honeydays! His splendor and beauty is unmatched by any bee or man. And he's so gracius, too! How did I first gain all my festively magical powers? By worshiping _[sic]_ Bubble Bee Man! Even when my parents said it was unhealthy! Ho ho! _-Completion-_ 🎵 Oh the numbers of quests are frightful... 🎵 🎵 But these prizes are so delightful 🎵 🎵 So if you wanna make that dough 🎵 🎵 Catch the snow, catch the snow, catch the snow! 🎵 Ho ho ho! You've done it! You caught those flakes! And look, my Festive Bee has another [Present] for you to deliver! Beesmas is coming along splendidly! But we can't rest until all the decorations are done. Just 10 more quests until you get a Festive Bee (or 500 Tickets) of your own!   
Naughty Knock-Out  | The Beesmas Lights twinkle and glow, and fill us all with wonder. They also cause stray lightbults _[sic]_ to fall from the sky into the fields, quite violently! If one hits you, it'll fill you with Beesmas Inspiration! Granting x2 Pollen for 5 seconds! Yes! It's magical. But you want to know what's not magical? The baddies around the map! Spiders, Snowbears, and even Vicious Bees. Naughty naughty creatures! They want to destroy the festivities. They're waging a war against Beesmas! Beesmas of all things! Oh ho oh no no no. Let us put an end to those who would seek to destroy Beesmas! Gingerbread Cub says to do it now, swiftly, in an overwhelming show of force, ho ho! _-During-_ Vicious Bee has never been on Bubble Bee Man's nice list. Not once in years and years! That arrogant bee doesn't even try to be nice! It WANTS to be naughty! Well, there's no place for creatures like that in this winter wonderland. Show Vicious Bee we mean Beesmas Business! _-Completion-_ Ho ho! You did it! You game _[sic]_ them a brilliant Beesmas beatdown! With those nasty Vicious Bees out of the way, we can get back to what really matter. _[sic]_ Purchasing things from Bee Bear's Catalog! And completing more of my quests! Complete 3 more, and I'll give you 3 additional [Beequip Case Slots]. And complete 9 more for a Festive Bee or 500 Tickets of your own!   
Beesmas Bundles and Bean Bugs  | Reindeer Puppy Bee is so adorable, and Gingerbread Cub too! All those adorable things and more in Bee Bear's jolly ol' Catalog! You won't find better prices anywhere - this I can guarantee! Bee Bear has all the best sources. Because Bee Bear is magic! Purchase a little piece of that magic for yourself to hold dear year round only from 1 place: Bee Bear's Catalog! So bring your [Gingerbread Bears], bring your [Snowflakes]! Bring your Festive Bee, if you have one. If you don't, well bring a Basic Bee, it doesn't matter. There _[sic]_ something for all bees of all types and rarities in Bee Bear's Catalog! But you won't find Bean Bugs in there. Although you'll need to find some for this quest! Just wait around, and sure enough they'll come. _-During-_ Bean Bugs aren't naughty or nice. Their brains are too small to comprehend such things! Oh ho ho. To be blissfully unaware of the moral implications of your own actions! That would be a Beesmas blessing! _-Completion-_ 🎵 Oh, Festive Bee, oh Festive Bee 🎵 🎵 Your ribbon is so pretty 🎵 🎵 Oh, Festive Bee, oh Festive Bee 🎵 🎵 I'm happy that you're with me 🎵 Ho ho ho. Yes, a song for Festive Bee too! All the bees deserve their own songs, because they bring us so much joy! And you know who else brings us joy? The bears, here on the mountain! So take this [Present] and deliver it to one of them right now, to show you care. Then hurry back, you know the drill! Ho ho!   
Presents for Polar Bear  | The Honeydays are a time for being with family. That's why I brought Festive Bee, Puppy Bee, and Gingerbread Cub! But the reason I'm here in the first place, is because I have family who live here. Can you guess who? Ho ho ho! I bet you know! My dear, dear cousin Polar Bear! The greatest chef in all the land. He's been having a tough year, sad to say. But we can fix that! Why don't you complete a few of his quests, while we continue our work on preparing for Beesmas? _-During-_ Polar Bear is my baby cousin. Can you believe it, ho ho? I know what you're thinking. Why do I look so much younger, and more jubilent? It's because I'm never stressed! I'm always filled with pure joy and it keeps me young! _-Completion-_ Look Festive Bee! The beekeeper completed its latest quest! By golly, think of the boundless Beesmas joy they've already spread! Decorations all around us - coming together so splendidly. Ho ho ho. Just 1 more quest, and I've got 3 special [Beequip Case Slots] for you! And just 7 more quests until you earn a Festive Bee of your own! Or [Tickets]! Both are good.   
Festive Feast With the Whole Family  | On Beesmas Day, we take time to appreciate what really matters. We sit down with our loved ones, and we eat an obscene amount of honey. We don't judge eachother, because we LOVE one another. It doesn't matter how much we eat. Puppy Bee eats non-stop but I don't judge his _[sic]_ , isn't that right Puppy Bee? Puppy Bee will eat ANYTHING, ho ho ho! The veterinarian bills can vouch for that. And that's good because it means he appreciates everything he's given. I appreciate all your help! And I appreciate the Beesmas Feast my cousin made! I can smell it all the way from down here! Oh how I wish I could just have a taste. But I can't, oh no. If I move, new beekeepers won't be able to start their quests. But YOU can do it for me! Oh yes! Do have a taste, and then come tell Bee Bear everything about it. Ho ho ho ho. I want a description so detailed I can taste it. But that's not all, theres _[sic]_ so much more to do! Be sure to take down that naughty Snowbear as well. It's back for more! _-During-_ Sharing [Jelly Beans] is just one of the ways you can show you care! Another way you can show you care is by completing my quest. _-Completion-_ 🎵 Siiiilent night.. 🎵 🎵 Hooooney night... 🎵 🎵 Beesmas Feast is quite a sight 🎵 🎵 Stuff our faces, so morbid and wild 🎵 🎵 [Treats] and Honey so tasty yet mild 🎵 🎵 🎵 Our hungers never ceeeeAAsse 🎵 🎵 While digging in to Beesmas Feast 🎵 Oh ho ho ho! You look so satisfied that I can practically taste the meal! Your dedication, and boundless hunger, has earned you this: 3 additional [Beequip Case Slots]! ## Words of Farewell

Multiple announcements were made by Bee Bear before his departure. These announcements are:   
  
---  
Servers will reboot at the end of this hour to remove the winter event.   
Bee Bear: Ho ho ho, cool your jets little ones! We'll be back next year!   
Bee Bear: Its up to the Beekeepers to keep the spirit of Beesmas alive while we're gone.   
Bee Bear: Time to pack up, and fly back home! Imagine how boring it will be here without us, ho ho!

### Dialogue

Quest name  | Dialogue   
---|---  
Beautify The Beesmas Tree  | Ho ho ho ho ho! It's beginning to look a lot like Beesmas! That's right, it's the most wonderful time of the year! And I'm back! Can you believe it's been a whole year since we met? It's my duty each year to flood the world with festive holiday cheer! It's a huge responsibility and a lot of work, but thankfully I don't work alone. My Festive Bees do most of the strenuous stuff, oh yes! But even they're not able to keep up with the global demand for Beesmas festivity. That's why this year I've brought along some extra special little helpers... The [Cub Buddies]! These adorable cubs are actually stuffed toys brought to life by pure Beesmas magic! And I've brought them here to help make this year's Beesmas truly special. If you work to spread Beesmas cheer, you may be able to bring a [Cub Buddy] of your own to life! Behind you I've set up a Beesmas Tree. But it's not complete yet - oh no. It need _[sic]_ Ornaments! Work with the bears around the map to make [Ornaments] to decorate the tree. Once you've put enough [Ornaments] on the tree, you'll be able to open presents! I've hidden presents just for you all over the mountain! But they can only be opened through the power of a well-dressed Beesmas Tree. Those presents are worth it though! Ho ho ho! I've put a [Festive Bee Egg] in one of them, can you guess which? And there's even the possibility of a [Mythic Egg] in one of the more difficult presents... [Mythic Eggs] always hatch into Mythic Bees - the newest, rarest, and most ostentatious bees yet! I won't spoil all the surprises! But here's something you can know for sure: If you help decorate the Beesmas Tree and complete 10 of my quests... I'll help you bring a Black Bear [Cub Buddy] of your own! That little munchkin will help keep the wonder of Beesmas alive in your heart all year long! It will also help you collect Tokens! And occasionally give you gifts! But we need to make haste, Beesmas day is approaching fast! To begin, here's what you'll need to do: Collect 1,000 _[sic]_ Red Pollen and put 1 [Ornament] on the Beesmas Tree! -_During-_ If we don't decorate the tree before Beesmas ends, the world will fall into eternal gloom! Ho ho ho! Or not, but let's not risk it. Collect 1,000 _[sic]_ Red pollen and put 1 [Ornament] on the Beesmas Tree! _-Completion-_ 🎵 It's beginning to look a lot like Beesmas 🎵 🎵 Everywhere you goooo 🎵 🎵 Take a look at the Beesmas Tree 🎵 🎵 Then turn around and look at me 🎵 🎵 That's hoooow yoooou knooow! (that it's Beesmas in Bee Swarm) 🎵 🎵 Ho ho ho! 9 more quests to go until you receive a [Cub Buddy]! And before you ask, if you purchased the Cub Buddy pack already... I'll give you 250 Tickets in it's place (you can't have 2 Cub Buddies equipped at once, ho ho!)   
Slay Bells Jingling  | Ho ho ho! We're getting jolly already! But something's just not right... listen here: There are scruges _[sic]_ out there actively working to disrupt our festivities. Naughty beetles and spiders want to destroy our tree and ruin the mood! It's a war on Beesmas! We have to fight back! Get ready to jingle some SLAY bells, cause it's time for a good old-fashioned Beesmas beat-down! Collect 10,000 White pollen, defeat 4 Rhino Beetles, and defeat 2 Spiders. _-During-_ Naughty bugs deserve EXTRA firm hugs! Collect 10,000 White pollen, defeat 4 Rhino Beetles, and defeat 2 Spiders. _-Completion-_ Oh ho ho! Splendid work out there, an _[sic]_ quite persuasive! The bugs will certainly learn to appreciate Beesmas after that. Yes, there's nothing more jolly than spreading Beesmas cheer by force! Ho ho! 8 more quests to go until we bring your Black Bear [Cub Buddy] to life!   
Blue Beesmas  | My Festive Bees are perfectly cheery little helpers! All year long they work in my workshop, creating gifts and [Cub Buddies]! But they're no help when it comes to collecting Blue pollen. Ho ho, oh yes. Their Red Bombs are worthless out there in the Blue fields! But if we're going to make this Beesmas truly special, we'll need pollen of all colors. Won't you and your bees help where my Festive Bees can't? Wonderful! Your go-get-em attitude warms a Bee Bear's heart. Collect 50,000 Blue pollen, 20,000 pollen from the Blue Flower Field, collect 25 [Blueberry] tokens Feed 25 [Blueberries] to your bees (they deserve it!) And make sure you've put 3 [Ornaments] on the Beesmas Tree! _-During-_ Festive Bees work all year long, non stop, with smiles on their faces! They're purely motivated by the joy of others. No need for a paycheck. Ho ho! Collect 50,000 Blue pollen, 20,000 pollen from the Blue Flower Field, Collect 25 [Blueberry] tokens, feed 25 [Blueberries] to your bees and put 3 [Ornaments] on the Beesmas Tree! _-Completion-_ 🎵 I'll have a bluuuuuue... Beesmas... without Blue 🎵 🎵 I'll be so bluuuue... thinking... about Blue (pollen) 🎵 🎵 We'll be doing alright... sticking to Red and White 🎵 🎵 But I'llll have a bluuue bluuuue Beesmas ~ 🎵 Ho ho ho, crisis averted! This'll be plenty of Blue pollen! 7 more quests to go until you receive your [Cub Buddy]! (Or 250 Tickets if you already bought one)   
Beesmas Blitz  | The spirit of Beesmas animates the [Cub Buddies], motivates the Festive Bees, And it also gives me the power to fly all around the world in less than 24 hours! Ho ho ho! I move so fast hyped up on Beesmas bliss that I can't even be seen with human eyes! You'll need to move fast too if we're going to save this Beesmas. Here's a free [Hasty Bee Jelly] to get things moving! Merry Beesmas!!! Collect 150,000 Pollen, 100 Haste Tokens and 10 Tokens from Sprouts. _-During-_ Oh ho ho! I move so fast sometimes my bees can't catch up! But my [Cub Buddies] - oh those little ones are so clever! They just teleport right behind me, no matter where I go. It can be rather startling! And sometimes quite awkward, depending on what I'm doing. Ho ho! Collect 150,000 Pollen, 100 Haste Tokens and 10 Tokens from Sprouts. _-Completion-_ You completed that quest faster than a rabid reindeer! Wonderful! This Beesmas is shaping up to be a marvelous one indeed. Your [Cub Buddy] is already starting to twitch - look! It's still just a doll, but I think it's on it's way to becoming a real cub! All through the magic of Beesmas! Ho ho ho! 6 more quests and that should do it.   
A Time For Sharing  | Beesmas is a time for giving! A time for sharing! A time for loving! A time for snowing! A time for eating! A time for shopping! A time for bees! A time for bears! A time for people of all ages on to play Bee Swarm Simulator on ROBLOX! A time for completing Bee Bear's quests to spread honeyday cheer! A time for putting [Ornaments] on the Beesmas Tree - at least 5 of them! A time for collecting 300,000 Red pollen! A time for sharing 10 Jelly Bean tokens with other players! (Use the [Jelly Beans] items on a flower field and have other players pick them up) A time for receiving [Jelly Beans] from Bee Bear so you can do that! A time for feeding 50 [Sunflower Seeds] to your bees! A time for defeating 2 Spiders! A time for waiting until Spiders respawn so you don't speed through this quest line! A time for joy! A time for festive family fun and so much more! _-During-_ Beesmas is a time for questing! A time for looking at your Quest Menu to see what I already told you to do! _-Completion-_ 🎵 The boosts are right 🎵 🎵 The buffs are up 🎵 🎵 We're farming fast 🎵 🎵 And that's enough 🎵 🎵 Simply, having, a wonderful Beesmastime! 🎵 🎵 ~ Simply, having, a wonderful Beesmastime! ~ 🎵 Ho ho ho ho! I do prefer Paul's solo stuff. A wonderful Beesmastime indeed - we're halfway through your quests! The Beesmas Tree is coming together marvelously! Just look at all those [Ornaments]. If you complete just 5 more quests, there should be enough Beesmas magic radiating off that tree To bring your [Cub Buddy] to life! Yes, pure Beesmas radiation will animate your cub!!   
Naughty Or Nice  | There's nothing more exciting and satisfying than opening a present! The joy of surprise, mixed with the joy of knowing someone cares about you - Oh ho ho, it warms a Bee Bear's heart just thinking about it! That's why each year I bring gifts to every good boy and girl and cub! Everyone deserves to know the joy of opening a present on Beesmas day! EXCEPT for those who have been naughty... The only surprise the naughty ones deserves is the shameful surprise of coal in their stocking! Yes, that's something that really happens. It's not just an old wives' tale. I've got an entire mining operation just to keep up with the coal demand! Ho ho ho. Yes, the naughty list is outrageously long! But you and your bees, you've been good all year, haven't you? That's why I'm giving you these [Magic Beans]! Popping open a Sprout is just like opening a Beesmas Present! Except it takes more work, and it's mandatory for completing this quest. Collect 400,000 pollen from the Strawberry Field... Collect 400,000 pollen from the Pineapple Patch... Defeat 1 Werewolf, 1 Windy Bee, and 1 Vicious Bee.. And collect 100 Tokens from Sprouts! Ho ho ho! _-During-_ The Werewolf has been VERY naughty. Attacking innocent beekeepers, that's not nice! But what's worse, His appearance completely clashes with the childish aesthetic of this game It breaks the immersion! Collect 400,000 pollen from the Strawberry Field... Collect 400,000 pollen from the Strawberry Field... Collect 400,000 pollen from the Pineapple Patch... Defeat 1 Werewolf, 1 Windy Bee, and 1 Vicious Bee.. And collect 100 Tokens from Sprouts! _-Completion-_ Ho ho ho! Well done little one, well done! You and your bees are certified nice! Not like those rascals Vicious and Windy Bee. Oh no no. Those trouble makers have earned themselves spots on my naughty-for-life list! Just 4 more quests until you receive your Black Bear [Cub Buddy].   
Unsilent Night  | The [Ornaments] on the Beesmas Tree twinkle and glow... Ho ho ho! It pleases me so! Festive decorations light up the night and bring a wholesome warmth to otherwise scary places! They glow just like [Moon Charms] and Fireflies - nature's Beesmas Lights! Let's light up the night with pure Beesmas brilliance! Collect 1,000,000 White pollen... Collect 250 "Bomb" Ability Tokens... Collect 1,000 Honey Tokens... Collect 25 Moon Charms... Use the Moon Amulet Generator 1 time... Defeat 2 Werewolves... And put 6 [Ornaments] on the Beesmas Tree! _-During-_ I'm using new magical light sources in this year's Beesmas Decorations. They're cutting edge and purportedly "not proven to be consumer safe". But as long as we believe in the spirit Beesmas _[sic]_ , nothing can harm us! Collect 1,000,000 White pollen... Collect 250 "Bomb" Ability Tokens... Collect 1,000 Honey Tokens... Collect 25 Moon Charms... Use the Moon Amulet Generator 1 time... Defeat 2 Werewolves... And put 6 [Ornaments] on the Beesmas Tree! _-Completion-_ 🎵 Siiiilent night.. 🎵 🎵 Hooooney night... 🎵 🎵 Bees make great friends alright... 🎵 🎵 Round yon honeycomb, bear, bee, and child 🎵 🎵 Make some honey, so tasty and mild 🎵 🎵 Watch that honey increee-EEease 🎵 🎵 Wah-atch our hoooney increase 🎵 What a splendid spectacle that was indeed! I can feel the Beesmas spirit illuminating from those Bomb Abilities! And the ever-increasing fluorescent glow of the Beesmas Tree! Ho ho ho! It's getting so bright I can practically feel it! I can sense you [Cub Buddy] is nearly ready... Just 3 more quests to go!   
Presents For Polar Bear  | Want to know the secret ingredient to Beesmas magic? It's not honey! Ho ho no no - it's FAMILY! The ones you love most and who love you in return bring Beesmas magic life! We're all one big happy family here, but there's one bear here whose even MORE family to me! Polar Bear - my beloved cousin and the greatest chef I know, ho ho! Help me make his Beesmas truly magical by completing 5 of his quests! And collect 1,000,000 Pollen from the Cactus Field and Rose Field. _-During-_ I remember when Polar Bear was just a Cub not unlike these [Cub Buddies]! But not nearly as cute. He was quite an ugly baby! Thankfully there's no Polar Bear Cub skin for the [Cub Buddies]! Ho ho ho! Complete 5 quests for Polar Bear. And collect 1,000,000 Pollen from the Cactus Field and Rose Field. _-Completion-_ Oh, splendid! Polar Bear must be simply glowing with Beesmas cheer! Did you know that Polar Bear is my YOUNGER cousin? He's only in his 30s - but I'm over 250 years old! How can that be? Ho ho ho! That's a Bee Bear's secret. Just 2 more quests until your [Cub Buddy] is brought to life! (Or if you already bought [Cub Buddy], until 250 Tickets are brought to your wallet!)   
All I Want For Beesmas Is Goo  | 🎵 I don't want a lot for Beesmas 🎵 🎵 There is just one thing I need 🎵 🎵 I don't care about the tokens 🎵 🎵 Hidden in the Beesmas Tree 🎵 🎵 I just want this to be real: 🎵 🎵 Fields covered in pink and teal 🎵 🎵 Make my wish come truuUUUE 🎵 🎵 All I want for Beesmas... 🎵 🎵 Is Goooooooo 🎵 🎵 gOOOOOooooo 🎵 🎵 Goooooo-oo-ooooo baaaaabyyyyy ~ 🎵 Ho ho ho ho ho!! Nobody takes it higher than Mariah! That's right - it's time for some Beesmas GOO. How do you think I keep all these Festive Bees and [Cub Buddies] together? And how do you think we're keeping those [Ornaments] on the tree? Ho ho! It's 50% magic and 50% GOO! Gather up those [Gumdrops]. I'll give you 10 to start! Craft some [Gumdrops] in the Blender at the Badge Bearer's Guild. And start gathering that GOO to save Beesmas and earn your [Cub Buddy]! (There's just too many tasks to list, so be a good helper and read your Quest Menu) _-During-_ My Festive Bees and I use goo to help spread Beesmas cheer. The Gummy Bear uses goo to subjugate weaker civilizations to his authoritative rule! Naughty naughty! Goo should be used for good. _-Completion-_ Marvelous! You and your bees are some of the best helpers Bee Bear has ever seen! And there's just 1 more quest now before we'll have enough Beesmas spirit To bring your [Cub Buddy] to life! This mountain is truly beginning to radiate pure Beesmas cheer! It's getting quite hot - all this radiation - ho ho! But that's good! The Beesmas Tree is glowing because the magic is becoming REAL! It's perfectly safe to stand around! Ho ho ho! Just one quest to go!   
A Beesmas Miracle  | It warms my heart to see the Beesmas Tree spring to life with [Ornaments]! You and your swarm have done a splendid job - my Festive Bees are most impressed! And your [Cub Buddy] is nearly ready to spring to life, powered by pure Beesmas Magic! Ho ho ho! One more [Ornament] should do it! Just one more glowing [Ornament] on that appropriately-sized artificial tree! That ought to do it! Beesmas will be saved and your [Cub Buddy] will be real! Oh, and just a few other minor tasks we've got to wrap up: Collect 5,000,000 Red and White pollen... Collect 250 Tokens from Sprouts... Collect 50 Moon Charm Tokens... Collect 500 "Boost" Ability Tokens... Collect 50 Tokens from Wild Windy Bee... Share 50 Jelly Bean Tokens... Feed 200 Sunflower Seeds to your bees... Defeat 5 Werewolves... And put 7 [Ornaments] on the Beesmas tree! Ho ho ho! You're practically done! _-During-_ The [Cub Buddies] to my right are my personal helpers! It's their full time job to look cute, and entice beekeepers to continue on with their quests. Collect 5,000,000 Red and White pollen... Collect 250 Tokens from Sprouts... Collect 50 Moon Charm Tokens... Collect 500 "Boost" Ability Tokens... Collect 50 Tokens from Wild Windy Bee... Share 50 Jelly Bean Tokens... Feed 200 Sunflower Seeds to your bees... Defeat 5 Werewolves... And put 7 [Ornaments] on the Beesmas tree! _-Completion-_ Ooooh! Look, Festive Bees! Look at the tree! Look at what our friends have accomplished! By working together with the people of this mountain, you've made Beesmas truly spectacular! Ho ho ho! This whole mountain is practically covered in a thick layer of Beesmas smog! It warms our hearts, brings us closer together, and tastes a bit like peppermint! And it might even do a little something more - like MUTATE YOUR BEES! THE BEESMAS TREE IS FULLY POWERED! THE REAL GIFT OF BEE MUTATION HAS BEEN BROUGHT TO THE MOUNTAIN, THANKS TO YOU! HO HO! Yes - the Beesmas Tree will bombard your bees with radiation and hopefully cause some merry mutations! And that's not all - look! Your [Cub Buddy] is alive! It's a Beesmas Miracle! You and your bees are truly special. This [Cub Buddy] (or 250 Tickets) will be in good hands! Take a look at the Cub Console in your System Menu to see more about it. Go on and get acquainted with your new friend, then come pay Bee Bear another visit. If you're willing to take this Beesmas to the next level, I've got another reward for you! Ho ho ho!

Bee Cub Workshop 1  | Ho ho ho! Your [Cub Buddy] seems to adore you already! Just look at how it can't stand to be apart from your side. What a precious little tot! Although... I do believe we could make it even MORE precious. How? Oh ho ho, with a little Beesmas flair of course! If you're willing to work overtime during the honeydays... You can earn yourself a [Cub Buddy Skin] made in the image of your truly! That's right! A [Bee Cub Buddy Skin]! I'm already adorable - can you imagine how cute I was as a baby? But to make such something that cute takes tremendous effort, And nearly incomprehensible levels of Beesmas magic! We're already on our way to making this the most festive Beesmas yet. Why stop now? HO HO! What's more important? Spending time with your real family, or your [Cub Buddy]? Ho ho ho ho ho. We both know the answer to that. Complete these next 5 quests, and you'll earn this event's exclusive skin. It won't be easy though! Meeerrry Beesmas! Collect 10,000,000 Blue pollen... Collect 5,000,000 pollen from the Clover Field and Pumpkin Patch... Collect 10 Royal Jelly Tokens... Feed 10 Bitterberries to your bees... Defeat 8 naughty Scorpions... And collect 100 Tokens from your [Cub Buddy]! _-During-_ Cub Buddy skins are purely cosmetic! Ho ho. Unless you count the psychological advantage you have over the plebs who missed out on your exclusive skin. That's something truly magical! Collect 10,000,000 Blue pollen... Collect 5,000,000 pollen from the Clover Field and Pumpkin Patch... Collect 10 Royal Jelly Tokens... Feed 10 Bitterberries to your bees... Defeat 8 Scorpions... And collect 100 Tokens from your [Cub Buddy]! _-Completion-_ 🎵 Laaaast Beesmas, I gave you some quests 🎵 🎵 But the very next day, you went AFK… 🎵 🎵 Thiiiis year, to keep you right there 🎵 🎵 I’m giving you something special ~ 🎵 A [Bee Cub Skin], after you complete 4 more quests! HO Ho Ho! You can have some of these goodies for now though.   
Bee Cub Workshop 2  | Ho ho ho! Everything is going so splendidly this Beesmas! We've been truly blessed by Bubble Bee Man this year. But we mustn't let up yet - the whole world is depending on us! And more importantly - your [Cub Buddy] is depending on you to get that skin! Oh, just look it look at me... so longingly... It wants to be a Bee Bear too! Your cub's been good to you - it deserves a special gift this Beesmas. Just 3 _[sic]_ more quests and my Festive Bees and I will be able to help you make that a reality! Collect 25,000,000 Red Pollen... Collect 10,000,000 pollen from the Mountain Top Field and Pineapple Patch... Collect 100 Treat tokens... Feed 100 Pineapples to your bees... Defeat 10 Rhino Beetles... Defeat 4 Vicious Bees... And craft 25 ingredients in the blender! _-During-_ A special [Cub Buddy] deserves a special name. You can rename yours any time in the Cub Console. But keep it PG, or you'll go straight on the naughty list! Collect 25,000,000 Red Pollen... Collect 10,000,000 pollen from the Mountain Top and Pineapple Patch... Collect 100 Treat tokens... Feed 100 Pineapples to your bees... Defeat 10 Rhino Beetles... Defeat 4 Vicious Bees... And craft 25 ingredients in the blender! _-Completion-_ You're quite possibly my greatest little helper yet! The honeyday cheer over you and your bees are spreading over the mountain makes me stronger and stronger! I can feel pure untamed Beesmas magic running through my veins! No one bear should have all this power! And yet I HAVE IT! So much power that I can even bring toys to life! Ho ho ho ho ho! Good thing I'm a benevolent Bee Bear whose only ambition is to spread festive cheer. 3 more quests to go until you obtain the [Bee Cub Skin]!   
Bee Cub Workshop 3  |  Oh golly, don't tell me you're starting to feel wary of Beesmas! You can NEVER have too much Beesmas, ho ho! Oh no.
