Just 2 more to go!   
Bee Cub Workshop 4  |  So often nowadays people forget the real meaning of Beesmas! All they care about it _[sic]_ buying this and receiving that. Oh it's terrible! Beesmas is about the BEES. And Bubble Bee Man. And other strange and esoteric ROBLOX memes. But in Bee Swarm Simulator it's about appreciating our BEES, and all they do for us. If we're going to conjur _[sic]_ up enough Beesmas magic to make your [Bee Cub Buddy Skin]... We'll need to return to the real heart of Beesmas. It's time to give back to your bees! And time for all of these other things too, ho ho: Collect 200,000,000 pollen... Collect 250 [Strawberry], [Blueberry], and [Sunflower Seed] tokens... Feed 1 [Neonberry] to your bees... Feed 1,000 [Treats] and 250 [Strawberries], [Blueberries], and [Sunflower Seeds] to your bees... Complete 10 quests for Polar Bear (he's important too!)... Share 100 Jelly Bean tokens with other players (yes, even they're important)... Craft 30 ingredients in the Blender... And put 9 [Ornaments] on the Beesmas tree! _-During-_ The [Cub Buddies] give all sorts of gifts all year long! In that way they're a lot like the Festive Bees who made them. Those [Cub Buddies] can give [Jelly Beans], [Micro-Converters], [Bitterberries] and more! Where do they find them? That's a [Cub Buddies'] secret! Collect 200,000,000 pollen... Collect 250 [Strawberry], [Blueberry], and [Sunflower Seed] tokens... Feed 1 [Neonberry] to your bees... Feed 1,000 [Treats] and 250 [Strawberries], [Blueberries], and [Sunflower Seeds] to your bees... Complete 10 quests for Polar Bear... Share 100 Jelly Bean tokens with other players... And put 9 [Ornaments] on the Beesmas Tree! _-Completion-_ Ho ho ho! We're nearly through! To be honest, I feel like I have enough Beesmas magic to finish the [Cub Buddy Skin] already! But my Festive Bees insist we end on a nice, even number. 9 [Ornaments] on a tree... why not make it 10? One more quest will appease my Festive Bees and grant your [Cub Buddy] the most wonderful gift! The gift of being me, Bee Bear! Ho hO! _[sic]_  
Bee Cub Workshop 5  |  This is the last quest before we make your exclusive event skin! Looks like your [Cub Buddy] can hardly hold its excitement in! Ho ho ho! Don't get ahead of yourself little one. Bee Bear saved the best and most festively fun quest for last! And by festively fun, I mean impossible to speed through it for people even with the most overpowered hives. It's time to give it all you've got! Beesmas's end approaches fast! So fast I don't even have time to list the task _[sic]_ required in this quest! Go! Go! Make Beesmas truly wonderful with this final quest! Do it for the children! Do it for your [Cub Buddy]! _-During-_ Festive Bees are the fastest converters the world has ever known. They convert 150 honey in just 1 second! How do they do it? Ho ho, through the magic of Beesmas of course! You can use it too to help you finish the final [Bee Cub Buddy Skin] quest! _-Completion-_ Ooooh splendid work indeed! A frighteningly festive show of effort. Ho ho! You did it! You devoted your precious winter break towards unlocking an item with purely cosmetic value! And it was so worth it! Look at how excited your [Cub Buddy] is getting. Finally there's enough Honeyday Cheer in the air for my Festive Bees to make something so adorable So, so preciously cute as this: your [Bee Cub Buddy skin]! Ho ho ho ho! You've done it! You've made this Beesmas truly, truly, truly special! And just in time - Bee Bear got to start moving if he's gonna spread festive cheer to the entire world This isn't the only place with bears and beekeepers that need gifts you know. Come, Festive Bees! Our jolly work here is done, thanks to this amazing beekeeper and their bees! So long my friend! Until next year, Merrrrrrrry Beesmas!!!!!   
Conclusion  | My Festive Bees and I can move so fast, that it appears like we're still standing here when we're actually not. We're just popping in every other millisecond to keep things looking festive! Ho ho ho! I'm delivering thousands of gifts as we speak. That's the power of Beesmas! As long as you beelieve.   
  
## Words of Farewell

Multiple announcements were made by Onett, Bee Bear and Noob Cub Buddy before Bee Bear's departure. These announcements are:   
  
---  
Bee Bear, the Beesmas Tree, and the Winter Packs will leave in hour. Time to wrap it all up!   
If you haven't used the code "FestiveFinale", make sure to do it before the reboot!   
Bee Bear: "Ho ho ho! Just 30 minutes to go!"   
Bee Bear: "The game will reboot soon, and everything will be back to normal! I'll pack all this up in the blink of an eye."   
Bee Bear: "Take care of those Cub Buddies while I'm gone! Always be good - never naughty! Remember - I'm always watching, ho ho hoo!"   
Bee Bear: "How? Ho hoo! That's a Bee Bears secret! Quickly finish those quests and open any final presents (and buy those packs if you were going to)."   
Noob Cub Buddy: "Okee-doke. Wets get out of hewre. Everybwody out WIGHT NOW! Watch for a pwomo-code in the title after the wee-boot."

### Dialogue

Quest name  | Dialogue   
---|---  
Beesmas Begins  | Ho ho ho! Merry Beesmas!! Happy Honeydays!!! That's right, it's the most wonderful time of the year! A time devoted to appreciating the bees, and everything they do for us. And also a time for sharing gifts!!!! I'm Bee Bear, the magical mascot of Beesmas! (I'm also Polar Bear's cousin) And I've come to the mountains to spread the spirit of the season! As you can see, I've brought along my Festive Bees to aid me. If you help me spread Honeyday Cheer throughout the mountain... I'll grant you a 1st Edition Festive Bee of your own, to keep that Beesmas spirit alive year round! (Or the market value of 500 [Tickets] if you happen to already own one!) I need you and your bees to be my little elves! Help me complete 3 quests, and I'll be able to make a [Present]. Bring a [Present] to a bear, and they'll give you something in return! Once we've built 6 [Presents], we'll build you one of your own: A Festive Bee (or 500 [Tickets])! I'll have you know it won't be easy - there's much much work to be done! So let's get started! Beesmas day is approaching fast! Here's what I need first: Collect 250 White pollen and 250 Red pollen. _-During-_ It's my job to make [Presents] for every bear in the world! The only way I can do that on time is with your help. Collect 250 White pollen and 250 Red pollen to help me finish this [Present]! _-Completion-_ 🎵 Oh Beesmas tree, oh Beesmas tree...🎵 🎵 You should be made of honeyyyy... 🎵 🎵 Oh Beesmas tree, oh Beesmas tree... 🎵 🎵 I'd put you in my tummyy... 🎵 Ho ho ho! 2 more quests and I'll be able to finish the first [Present].   
Beating Beetles on Beesmas  | Beesmas is a time to spend with the ones you love most... Your bees! It's a time to reflect on all they do for us... Collecting pollen, making honey, defending us from attackers... Bees do it all! They're a true blessing! Without them, I'd never been able to finish these [Presents]. Next up, collect 1000 pollen from the Clover Field... And defeat 3 Rhino Beetles! _-During-_ Beesmas is a wonderful time, but also the busiest time of the year! It's also very good for the economy, you know. Ho-ho! Next up, collect 1000 pollen from the Clover Field... And defeat 3 Rhino Beetles! _-Completion-_ That was quite a jolly beat down! Just 1 more quests _[sic]_ and I'll be able to finish the first [Present]!   
BEElieve  | I'm just one of the magical entities devoted to spreading Beesmas cheer! There is another... much older and much wiser. He goes my _[sic]_ many names: Father Beesmas... Santa Buzz... But his proper name is Bubble Bee Man. This majestic creature is at once both a Bee and a Man. It has no relation to Bubble Bee though, let's get that clear. Ho ho ho! If you bee-lieve in him, he can bring to you Beesmas cheer... Even in your darkest hour. Complete this next quest, and I'll give you one of [Bubble Bee Man's badges]. It will bless your profile with Beesmas spirit, but only if you BEELIEVE!!!! Collect 2000 Blue pollen... Collect 25 Ability Tokens... Defeat 1 spider... And, most importantly, you must BEElieve!!! _-During-_ Bubble Bee Man isn't just the magic mythological icon of Beesmas... He's also famous for his astonishingly good looks. Ho ho ho! He's one of the rare examples of someone truly beautiful, both inside and out. By belief _[sic]_ in him, it's said that his beauty shines through us as well. Collect 2000 Blue pollen... Collect 25 Ability Tokens... And defeat 1 Spider. _-Completion-_ 🎵 He sees you when you're sleeping... 🎵 🎵 He knows when you're awake... 🎵 🎵 He knows if you've used external programs to exploit in this game or not... 🎵 🎵 So don't hack for Bubble Bee Man's sake!!! 🎵 That's just one of the many little jingles we sing in praise of Bubble Bee Man. Ho ho ho ho ho ho ho ho ho ho ho ho ho!!!!!!!!!! And with that, the first [Present] is finished! Deliver _[sic]_ it to a bear of your choice and exchange it for a surprise!   
White Beesmas  | Where I'm from, it snows all year long! It's a truly magical place. With very limited access to the outside world. As far as you can see, there's nothing but brilliant white snow! Oooh, it's wonderful! Ho ho ho ho. For this next quest, I'll need you to collect 10,000 White pollen! To make it fun, pretend you're shoveling snow! Oh, and don't forget to deliver that [Present]! _-During-_ Shoveling snow is an ancient pastime _[sic]_. And there's a true art to it, you know. Ah, there's nothing more satisfying than clearing a sidewalk or driveway. Collect 10,000 White pollen. Just pretend you're shoveling snow! Ho-ho! _-Completion-_ 🎵 Iiii'm dreaming... 🎵 🎵 Of a sweeeeeeeet, Beesmas... 🎵 🎵 Just like the ones I've heard about... 🎵 🎵 Where the bees are merry... 🎵 🎵 And the bugs aren't scary...🎵 🎵 And there's no cub that's left without! (honey) 🎵 Great work! 2 more quests and the next [Present] will be complete!   
A Time For Giving  | This is the season for giving! And nobody deserves gifts more than our bees. Before you can do any more work, we should fill your bees up with tasty treats! Anyhow, I'm here to tell you to seek out Bubble Bee Man once more... In your darkest hour, he will be your light!   
  
## Tips

  * **Use[field boosters](/wiki/Field_Boost "Field Boost") whenever possible**. They help with collecting pollen, these can also be stacked using present rewards or codes. If necessary, using [crafting material](/wiki/Crafting "Crafting") boosts can also help.
  * **Collect honey tokens from[sprouts](/wiki/Sprout "Sprout"), [honeystorms](/wiki/Honeystorm "Honeystorm"), and the [Stick Bug Challenge](/wiki/Stick_Bug_Challenge "Stick Bug Challenge")**. Players can summon a honey storm using the [Honey Storm Summoner](/wiki/Honeystorm "Honeystorm") near Brown Bear every 4 hours or use the [Special Sprout Summoner](/wiki/Special_Sprout_Summoner "Special Sprout Summoner") every 16 hours near the [Red HQ](/wiki/Red_HQ "Red HQ"). [Stick Bug](/wiki/Stick_Bug "Stick Bug"), [Stick Nymphs](/wiki/Stick_Nymph "Stick Nymph"), and Stick Bug's Defense Totems also drop honey tokens so collect those too. This applies to A Pinch Of Magic and Honeyday Harvest. Many of Bee Bear's quests (All I want for Beesmas is Goo, Unsilent Night, and A Beesmas Miracle) also require special tokens ([Gumdrops](/wiki/Gumdrops "Gumdrops")/[Moon Charms](/wiki/Moon_Charm "Moon Charm")) that require active [firefly](/wiki/Fireflies "Fireflies") hunting or sprout planting.
  * **Use the[Glue Dispenser](/wiki/Glue_Dispenser "Glue Dispenser")**. If the player doesn't have a [Gummy Bee](/wiki/Gummy_Bee "Gummy Bee") but has a Goo Hotshot badge, use a Field Booster and then the Glue Dispenser in [Gummy Bear's Lair](/wiki/Gummy_Bear%27s_Lair "Gummy Bear's Lair"). Using it gives the player the [Unlimited Gumdrops buff](/wiki/Buffs_%26_Debuffs#From_Areas "Buffs & Debuffs") that, when coupled with a field booster, would help the player with the goo requirements in Festive Bee Workshops 1 and 3, as well as in All I Want for Beesmas is Goo.
  * **Give[presents](/wiki/Present "Present") to NPCs.** If an NPC gives a [Field Boost](/wiki/Field_Boost "Field Boost") that the player needs for a quest, they should give them a present if they haven't yet. In 2018, players could only give a present to [Onett](/wiki/Onett "Onett") after completing all of his Star Journey quests. [Gifted Bucko Bee](/wiki/Gifted_Bucko_Bee "Gifted Bucko Bee"), [Gifted Riley Bee](/wiki/Gifted_Riley_Bee "Gifted Riley Bee"), and Stick Bug require a [Translator](/wiki/Translator "Translator") before a present can be given to them.
  * **Use[Magic Beans](/wiki/Magic_Bean "Magic Bean") to summon Sprouts**. In some of Bee Bear's quests, it is required to collect special treats such as strawberries. Sprouts are the best way to collect special treats. Sprout drops are varied towards the [field](/wiki/Fields "Fields") it is in. For example, a sprout found in the [Strawberry Field](/wiki/Strawberry_Field "Strawberry Field") will drop more strawberry tokens than any other treat. Sprouts are especially useful for Festive Bee Workshop 3. Some of Bee Bear's 2019 quests also required tokens from sprouts.
  * **Avoid defeating any mobs that take a long time to respawn** if one of Bee Bear's upcoming quests requires completing [Polar Bear](/wiki/Polar_Bear "Polar Bear") quests. This includes [mantises](/wiki/Mantis "Mantis"), [scorpions](/wiki/Scorpion "Scorpion"), [spiders](/wiki/Spider "Spider"), and [werewolves](/wiki/Werewolf "Werewolf"). Otherwise, the player may have to wait longer to complete these quests due to their cooldowns.
  * **Server-hop** for [Rogue Vicious Bees](/wiki/Rogue_Vicious_Bee "Rogue Vicious Bee"), [Wild Windy Bees](/wiki/Wild_Windy_Bee "Wild Windy Bee"), and [Bean Bugs](/wiki/Bean_Bug "Bean Bug"). This is as many of Bee Bear's Cub Buddy quests require tokens from Wild Windy Bee or the defeat of Wild Windy Bee.
  * Place [planters](/wiki/Planter "Planter") in specific fields ahead of time if you know you will need to harvest hours' worth of nectar in an upcoming quest.
  * During Beesmas Summer 2024, trade in the [Hive Hub](/wiki/Hive_Hub "Hive Hub") as much as possible to get all the [stickers](/wiki/Sticker "Sticker") required for Bee Bear's quests.

## Trivia

  * One of the three present tokens hidden in the game could have been found behind Bee Bear's gift boxes.
  * Bee Bear arrived on day 9 of Beesmas 2018.
  * Bee Bear is the third traveling bear to be introduced to the game.
  * Bee Bear is the first bear to require completion of another bear's quests, the second being [Spirit Bear](/wiki/Spirit_Bear "Spirit Bear"). 
    * One of his quests, 'Presents for Polar Bear' requires the completion of 3 Polar Bear quests. His "Quality Cousin Time" quest and "Family Festivities" quest also requires the completion of 6 Polar Bear quests.
    * Several of his quests also rely on the completion of other NPC's Beesmas quest.
  * Bee Bear uses the Cartoony Animation Pack.
  * Bee Bear is Polar Bear's older cousin and is just a polar bear in a bee costume.
  * Bee Bear, [Mother Bear](/wiki/Mother_Bear "Mother Bear"), and [Science Bear](/wiki/Science_Bear "Science Bear") are the only quest-giving bears in the game that are not directly based on real-life bears.
  * Just like [Panda Bear](/wiki/Panda_Bear "Panda Bear"), Mother Bear, Science Bear, [Black Bear](/wiki/Black_Bear "Black Bear"), [Bubble Bee Man](/wiki/Bubble_Bee_Man "Bubble Bee Man"), and Spirit Bear, he is aware that he is in a game. For example, he sings: "🎵 He knows if you've used external programs to exploit in this game or not... 🎵 🎵 So don't hack for Bubble Bee Man's sake!!".
  * The dialogue while the player is completing "The Beesmas Rush" states that Bee Bear hasn't missed a Beesmas deadline in over 250 years, suggesting that he is over 250 years old. 
    * He also directly states that he is over 250 years old while comparing his age to Polar Bear, who is ~30.
    * He says this again in his Beesmas 2020 dialogue when referencing past Beesmas's
    * Bee Bear could also be over a thousand years old as mentioned in his 2021 quest dialogue "Quality Cousin Time".
  * Some of these quests have contradictory descriptions. 
    * In 'Bee-liz Honey-dad,' the initial dialogue as well as the quest menu mention defeating 10 mantises, but the "during" dialogue does not.
    * In the quest 'BEElieve,' Bee Bear says collect 2,000 [Blue](/wiki/Category:Blue "Category:Blue") pollen but the quest menu says to collect 2,500 Blue [Pollen](/wiki/Pollen "Pollen").
    * In the quest 'Naughty Or Nice,' in the quest menu, it tells the player to collect 400,000 pollen from the [Clover Field](/wiki/Clover_Field "Clover Field"), but in the during dialogue, it tells them to collect 400,000 pollen from the Strawberry Field instead. Players didn't need to collect 400,000 pollen from the Strawberry Field to complete the quest.
    * In the quest 'Beesmas Blitz,' Bee Bear tells players to collect 25 tokens from sprouts, even though they only needed 10.
    * In the quest 'Bee Cub Workshop 4,' Bee Bear tells players to collect 500 ![Strawberry](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Strawberry](/wiki/Strawberry "Strawberry"), ![Blueberry](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Blueberry](/wiki/Blueberry "Blueberry"), and ![Sunflower Seed](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Sunflower Seed](/wiki/Sunflower_Seed "Sunflower Seed") tokens, although players only needed to collect 250 of each.
    * In the quest 'Snowbear Showdown', in the quest menu, it tells the player to summon and defeat a level 5 [Snowbear](/wiki/Snowbear "Snowbear"), but the player needs to summon and defeat 5 [Snowbears](/wiki/Snowbear "Snowbear") instead.
  * The quest "The Beesmas Rush" initially required using the [Treat Dispenser](/wiki/Treat_Dispenser "Treat Dispenser") 3 times. This was taken out of the quest soon after since it required players to be in the [Bee Swarm Simulator Club](https://www.roblox.com/groups/3982592/Bee-Swarm-Simulator-Club#!/about) group to use it.
  * The quest "Twinkling Tinsel Token Treck" initially required the player to catch 100 [Beesmas Lights](/wiki/Beesmas_Lights "Beesmas Lights"). This was nerfed to 75 to accommodate the second questline.
  * In the conclusion dialogue, the final sentence, "Anyhow, I'm here to tell you to seek out Bubble Bee Man once more... In your darkest hour, he'll be your light!" is telling players to go to the [30 Bee Zone](/wiki/Bear_Gate "Bear Gate") at night, do the [Moon Charm Parkour](/wiki/Obstacle_Courses "Obstacle Courses"), and talk to Bubble Bee Man in the 30 Bee Gate to get the final event quest: 'B.B.M. Mission'.
  * Bee Bear is the longest staying traveling bear, staying for 44 days from December 19, 2018 to January 31, 2019 and for 50 days from December 22, 2019 to February 9, 2020. 
    * On his third visit, he stayed for 66 days, from December 25, 2020 to March 1, 2021.
    * On his fourth visit, he stayed for 96 days, from December 26, 2021 to April 1, 2022.
    * On his fifth visit, he stayed for 133 days, from December 26, 2022 to May 7, 2023.
    * On his sixth visit, he stayed for 104 days, from July 17, 2024 to October 29, 2024.
  * The ![Hivesticker flying bee bear](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Flying Bee Bear Sticker](/wiki/Sticker#Sticker_Index "Sticker")'s description implies that he can fly at speeds at least 25,000 miles per hour.
  * Bee Bear is the first and only seasonal traveling Bear in the game.
  * A server-wide message appeared in his last 1 hour, and another in his last 10 minutes.
  * Bee Bear has his own [Cub Buddy](/wiki/Cub_Buddy "Cub Buddy") skin. 
    * This makes him the only traveling bear to have his own Cub Buddy skin.
  * Bee Bear stated that he is so fast that he can look like he's at two different places at the same time.
  * When starting the quest "A Beesmas Miracle," the quest name is misspelled in the chat as "A Beemas Miracle".
  * Bee Bear has the most quests out of all the traveling bears. 
    * He has a total of 61 quests across all three years putting him in second place for the most quests in the game overall, behind Black Bear who has a total of 79 quests not including his repeatable ones.
  * During Beesmas 2020, Bee Bear gave 69 gingerbread bears in total.
  * The quest "Festive Wreath Workshop 2" originally had you gander at [Onett's Lid Art](/wiki/Onett%27s_Lid_Art "Onett's Lid Art") 2 times. This was changed to 1 time, as twice could take up to slightly below 16 hours.
  * 2018 is the only year when there was not a second quest line from Bee Bear.
  * There was a bug that says you have to inspect Mother Bear's Gingerbread House 1 time but in reality, you have to do it twice. This has now been fixed.
  * Bee Bear says that the ![Paper Planter](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Paper Planters](/wiki/Paper_Planter "Paper Planter") costs 10,000 Honey, but it actually costs 25,000 Honey.
  * Bee Bear is the only questgiver to require the player to defeat a [Coconut Crab](/wiki/Coconut_Crab "Coconut Crab"). The quests that requires it are "Snow Cub Reformation 5/5" and "Cheering Up Gloomy Cub (5/5): Anything???".
  * Bee Bear mentions a Festive Puffshroom on "Puffshroom For Presents" that isn't in the game.
  * Bee Bear uses catalog's alternative spelling, 'catalogue', in his 2021 dialogue. This is the only time it is spelled as such.
  * During Beesmas 2022, in the quest "Decorating For Drives", Bee Bear asks for the player to purchase 4 items from Bee Bear's Catalog. However, later on in the quest "Shiverin' In The Snow", Bee Bear asks the player to purchase 3 items from the catalog instead.
  * During Beesmas 2022, in the quest "Merry Mechsquitos", Bee Bear asks for "Collect 1,000 tokens from bomb". However, later on, the objective was renamed to "Collect 1,000 Bomb Tokens."
  * Bee Bear's "Decorating For Drives" quest was originally named "Drive".
  * Noob Cub Buddy is one of the only NPCs featured in the Words of Farewell. 
    * The other NPCs are Bee Bear and Sun Bear.
  * In his quest "Puffshrooms for Presents," Bee Bear reveals he gained his powers from a Festive Puffshroom.
  * The rewards for beating some of the Peppermint Preparation quests before they were nerfed are: 
    * ![Enzymes](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)5 [Enzymes](/wiki/Enzymes "Enzymes")
    * ![Ticket](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)5 [Tickets](/wiki/Ticket "Ticket")
    * ![Red Extract](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)5 [Red Extracts](/wiki/Red_Extract "Red Extract")
    * ![Blue Extract](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)5 [Blue Extracts](/wiki/Blue_Extract "Blue Extract")
    * ![Ticket Planter](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)2 [Ticket Planters](/wiki/Ticket_Planter "Ticket Planter")
    * ![Black Balloon](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 [Black Balloon](/wiki/Black_Balloon "Black Balloon")
    * ![Purple Potion](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)3 [Purple Potions](/wiki/Purple_Potion "Purple Potion")
    * ![Gumdrops](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)150 [Gumdrops](/wiki/Gumdrops "Gumdrops")
  * Bee Bear is likely the only Quest Giver capable of telepathic communication.
  * There was a bug in Bee Bear's 14th quest of Beesmas Winter 2024-2025 where the "Open 5 Gift Boxes around the map" part of the quest could not be completed, even if all gift boxes had been opened. This has since been fixed.
  * There are a couple of bugs in Bee Bear's 20th quest for Beesmas Winter 2024-2025: 
    * Although you need to collect 250 stacks of Puffshroom Blessing, it says to collect only 25 stacks in-game.
    * The requirement "Pop 10 Gold Bubbles" was not counting, which meant the quest was impossible. This got fixed on April 2.
  * In Bee Bear's Beesmas Starts quest Bee Bears asks for 10 Snowflakes but the quest requirement only wants 5.
  * For the requirement "Catch 50 Marigold Bloom Petals", Merigold is spelt as Marigold so the quest was impossible on old servers. In later servers the requirement was automatically collected.



### References

  * The quest title, 'Bee-liz Honey-dad,' refers to 'Feliz Navidad,' ('Merry Christmas' in Spanish) a popular Christmas song. Similarly, 'White Beesmas', 'Black Beesmas' and 'Blue Beesmas' are all an allusion to the classic Christmas song (and movie), 'White Christmas,' and 'All I Want For Beesmas Is Goo' references the song, 'All I Want For Christmas Is You'. Also, the quest titles, "Walking In A Windy Wonderland" and "Shopping In A Winter Wonderland" are references to the song 'Walking In A Winter Wonderland'.
  * Bee Bear is bilingual, or at least knows enough Spanish to rewrite the "Prospero año y felicidad" ("prosperous year and happiness"), a line of 'Feliz Navidad' to "Osos felices, esa es la verdad" ("happy bears, that's the truth").
  * The dialogue in 'All I Want For Beesmas Is Goo' referenced hidden tokens in the Beesmas Tree.
  * In Festive Wreath Workshop 8, Bee Bear says "Have you ever seen Monsters Inc? The Pixar movie? Well, you know how the monsters charge up batteries by scaring children? Ho ho ho, well what we're doing is basically that, but the opposite! We charge up our festive powers through spreading pure joy! ... Festive Bee has just informed me that the monsters end up charging their batteries through laughter. So basically the same thing we're doing. **Please don't sue us Pixar!** Ho ho ho ho!", which is a direct reference to Monsters. Inc.
  * The completion dialogue for the quest Merry Mechsquitos is a reference to the song “Jingle Bell Rock” by Bobby Helms.
